### [1] [Twisted scattering from Chern-Simons theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30232)
*Guillermo Arias-Tamargo,Philip Boyle Smith,Rishi Mouland,Max Velásquez Cotini Hutt*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了保持非反常连续对称性的二维狄拉克费米子边界条件，将其等价于保持对称性的外尔费米子拓扑缺陷，并利用陈-西蒙斯理论构造了共形嵌入对称性下的此类缺陷；这些缺陷总存在且可实现为某种有限范畴对称性的半空间规范缺陷；文中计算了费米子经缺陷散射后附着的扭曲线、对应折叠边界态的边界中心荷及其模2 SPT类；应用包括验证阿贝尔对称性情形下已知结果，以及提出SU(2)×SU(4)×U(1)²对称性的新例子，其中扭曲线具有本征不可逆性并属于TY(Z₃×Z₃)或Rep(S₃)×Z₃对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究保持非反常连续对称性的二维狄拉克费米子边界条件，及其等价的外尔费米子拓扑缺陷，以拓展对对称性保护拓扑相和边界物理的理解。

Method: 利用陈-西蒙斯理论对共形嵌入对称性构造拓扑缺陷；将边界条件映射为半空间规范缺陷；计算散射过程中的扭曲线、边界中心荷和模2 SPT不变量。

Result: 证明此类缺陷总存在且可实现为有限范畴对称性的半空间规范缺陷；计算出对应的扭曲线、边界中心荷及模2 SPT类；在SU(2)×SU(4)×U(1)²新例子中发现本征不可逆的扭曲线，归属TY(Z₃×Z₃)或Rep(S₃)×Z₃对称性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了狄拉克边界条件与拓扑缺陷之间的系统联系，提供了统一框架来刻画对称性保持边界态的全局性质，并揭示了非阿贝尔对称性下扭曲线的新奇非可逆结构。

Abstract: We consider boundary conditions of two-dimensional Dirac fermions which preserve a non-anomalous continuous symmetry which acts differently on the left- and right-movers. Such boundaries are equivalent to topological defects of Weyl fermions preserving the symmetry. We give a general construction of such defects for conformally-embedded symmetries using Chern-Simons theory. They always exist and can be realised as half-space gauging defects for some finite categorical symmetry. Using this technology, we compute the twist lines to which fermions become attached upon scattering through the defect, the boundary central charge of the corresponding folded boundary state, and its mod-2 valued SPT class. As applications, we first consider abelian symmetries and find agreement with known results in the literature. We then study a novel example where an $SU(2) \times SU(4) \times U(1)^2$ symmetry is preserved. In contrast to the abelian case, the twist lines are intrinsically non-invertible and belong to either a $\text{TY}(\mathbb{Z}_3 \times \mathbb{Z}_3)$ or $\text{Rep}(\mathbb{S}_3) \times \mathbb{Z}_3$ symmetry depending on whether scattering is to the left or the right.

</details>


### [2] [Finding CFTs from spatial positivity of partition functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30234)
*Léo Buisine,Horacio Casini,Leandro Martinek*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出并探讨了一种推广了模不变性的二维共形场论（CFT）配分函数的正性性质，将其与Rényi熵的正性及拓扑缺陷线联系起来，并用于构造非模不变但局域幺正的CFT模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找比模不变性更普适的CFT基本约束，以刻画更广泛的物理合理模型（如非模不变但局域幺正的CFT）。

Method: 通过分析配分函数的正性、Rényi熵的正性以及拓扑缺陷线的作用，建立三者之间的理论联系，并以此为工具构造新模型。

Result: 提出了一个关于CFT配分函数正性的普适猜想；揭示了其与Rényi熵正性和拓扑缺陷线的内在关联；成功构造出一类一致的、局域幺正但未必模不变的CFT模型。

Conclusion: 配分函数正性是一个比模不变性更基础的物理约束，在理解CFT的分类与构造中具有关键作用；该性质为探索非传统CFT提供了新框架。

Abstract: We discuss a positivity property for partition functions that generalizes modular invariance and which is here conjectured to hold for general 2D CFTs. We show its relation to positivity properties of Rényi entropies and its meaning in terms of topological defect lines. We apply the conjecture to find consistent local unitary CFT models which are not necessarily modular invariant.

</details>


### [3] [Collinear Gluons from Gravity: Poincaré-Birkhoff-Witt Decomposition and Twisted Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30235)
*Jin Dong,Stephan Stieberger*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过混合开闭弦关系中的单引力子振幅，显式重构了次领头阶胶子散射振幅，并利用PBW定理和Stirling数对胶子排序空间进行分解，最终将重构系数表示为Parke-Taylor形式与森林上同调元之间的扭曲交点数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从引力理论出发重构规范场论振幅，探索Einstein-Yang-Mills理论及其弦修正中胶子与引力子振幅间的深层联系。

Method: 利用混合开闭弦关系中的单引力子振幅，结合Lie代数递归、PBW定理、shuffle求和、弦周期矩阵消去技巧，以及Parke-Taylor形式与森林cocycle的扭曲交点理论。

Result: 实现了次领头阶胶子振幅对单引力子振幅的显式重构；证明重构系数可分解为线性运动学因子与二分划因子之积；并将系数统一表达为扭曲交点数。

Conclusion: 胶子振幅的次领头阶结构可完全由引力振幅和纯代数/几何结构（Lie递归、PBW分解、扭曲交点）决定，揭示了规范-引力对应在高阶α'修正下的精细结构。

Abstract: We give an explicit reconstruction of subleading collinear Yang--Mills amplitudes from one-graviton amplitudes in Einstein--Yang--Mills theory and its string corrections. We realize the monodromy kernel in the mixed open-closed string relation as a weighted Lie-algebraic recursion on words of gluon labels. At order $α'^r$, its output contains at most $r$ Lie factors. The Poincaré--Birkhoff--Witt (PBW) theorem decomposes the space spanned by gluon orderings into sectors whose dimensions are given by unsigned Stirling numbers of the first kind. Appropriate shuffle sums of one-graviton amplitudes extract the degree-$r$ sector, for which the string-period matrix drops out. Accordingly, we show that the reconstruction coefficients factor into linear kinematic factors over the product of bipartition factors. Finally, we express the reconstruction coefficients as twisted intersection numbers between Parke--Taylor forms and explicit forest cocycles constructed recursively from logarithmic four-point differentials.

</details>


### [4] [Reentrant $d=2+1$ Superfluidity at High Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30236)
*Subham Dutta Chowdhury,Joan Elias Miro*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一个在2+1维中具有全局U(1)对称性的紫外完备模型，该模型在任意高温下均存在BKT有序相；通过尺度分离构造出可控的二维欧氏有效场论，并与格点蒙特卡洛结果匹配确定BKT相边界；高温相中存在无能隙静态模和代数衰减关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个在任意高温下仍保持BKT有序的紫外完备模型，解决高温下强耦合导致有效理论失控的问题。

Method: 采用尺度分离技术构造二维欧氏有效场论，并通过与格点蒙特卡洛模拟结果匹配来确定BKT相边界。

Result: 发现该模型在T→∞极限下仍存在BKT有序相，高温相中具有无能隙静态模式和代数衰减的关联函数。

Conclusion: BKT物理可在高温甚至无穷高温极限下稳健存在，挑战了传统对热涨落破坏长程关联的认知，且该模型为研究强耦合下的低能有效理论提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present a UV-complete model with a global $U(1)$ symmetry in $d=2+1$ dimensions that exhibits a Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless (BKT) ordered phase at arbitrarily high temperatures. A careful separation of scales yields a two-dimensional Euclidean effective field theory whose construction remains perturbatively controlled even when its soft modes become strongly coupled. We determine the BKT phase boundary by carefully matching this theory to existing lattice Monte Carlo results. The high-temperature phase supports gapless static modes and algebraically decaying correlations even as $T\to\infty$.

</details>


### [5] [Onsager Algebras and Lattice Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31653)
*Ioanna Kourkoulou,David Tong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了四种时空维度下自由格点费米子（交错、Wilson 和重叠形式）的精确Onsager代数，表明其在低能极限下恢复完整的手征对称性U(2N)，但不同离散化方案中生成元的量子化与局域性存在差异和权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解格点规范理论中手征对称性的实现机制，特别是不同费米子离散化方案如何在低能极限下恢复连续理论的手征对称性。

Method: 分析交错、Wilson 和重叠费米子的哈密顿量与欧几里得形式下的精确Onsager代数结构，比较其生成元的局域性、量子化性质及对应紧致子群。

Result: 证明三种格点费米子方案均导出可还原为U(2N)手征对称性的Onsager代数；生成元的局域性与代数最大紧致子群之间存在权衡关系。

Conclusion: Onsager代数是统一刻画格点费米子手征对称性的代数框架，其具体实现依赖于离散化方案，但低能极限下均能恢复连续理论的U(2N)手征对称性。

Abstract: We study the exact Onsager algebra of free lattice fermions in staggered, Wilson, and overlap incarnations, working in both Hamiltonian and Euclidean formulations in four spacetime dimensions. In each case, we show that the exact lattice algebra emanates the full chiral symmetry of the low-energy theory: if there are $N$ massless Dirac fermions in the continuum, the Onsager algebra reduces to a $U(2N)$ chiral symmetry. The details of which generators are quantised or ultra-local differ between formulations. There is also a trade-off between the locality of generators and the maximum compact subgroup of the Onsager algebra.

</details>


### [6] [Defect flows from functional relations in the asymptotic semiring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30242)
*Federico Ambrosino,Anatoly Konechny,Ingo Runkel,Gérard M. T. Watts*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究二维共形场论中由全纯缺陷场引发的拓扑缺陷微扰，发现平移不变缺陷在红外极限下必流至拓扑缺陷，并构建了描述该RG流的半环同态结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解相关全纯缺陷场对拓扑缺陷的微扰效应及其在重整化群流下的红外行为。

Method: 利用共形场论、范畴论（融合范畴、幺半范畴）和半环代数结构分析平移不变缺陷的RG流性质。

Result: 证明平移不变缺陷构成扩展拓扑缺陷融合范畴的幺半范畴；RG流诱导从平移不变缺陷半环到由拓扑缺陷构造的‘渐近半环’的半环同态；该同态保持函数关系，并在Virasoro极小模型中约束缺陷流终点。

Conclusion: RG流可系统地用半环同态刻画，为理解缺陷的红外固定点及分类提供了代数框架；相关结论在极小模型中得到验证。

Abstract: We study perturbations of topological defects by a relevant holomorphic defect field in two-dimensional conformal field theory. This produces translation invariant defects and these necessarily flow to topological defects in the infrared. Translation invariant defects form a monoidal category which extends the fusion category of topological defects. We argue that the renormalisation group flow defines a semiring homomorphism from the semiring of translation invariant defects to an "asymptotic semiring" which we construct from topological defects. The semiring homomorphism preserves functional relations, and we illustrate in the example of Virasoro minimal models that the resulting relations in the asymptotic semiring constrain the defect flow endpoints. The companion paper [1] contains a detailed study of such defect flows in several minimal models, both numerically and analytically.

</details>

*Rafael Carrasco,Carlos Pérez-Pardavila*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种局部方案，将全息复杂度中的bulk可观测量与欧几里得源制备的边界态的二次代价几何联系起来，通过传输哈密顿流并利用其在参考态处的Hessian定义代价度量，并在AdS₃标量扰动下计算了最大体积等可观测量对应的制备代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清全息复杂度中各类bulk可观测量与边界态制备代价之间的关系尚不明确，亟需建立明确的对应机制。

Method: 基于bulk哈密顿流到欧几里得制备源的全息输运，利用传输生成元在稳态参考态处的Hessian定义候选代价度量；当Hessian正定时，最小化制备能量在二次阶上复现bulk可观测量的变化；进一步在AdS₃标量扰动下计算最大体积及含二次标量/梯度修正的体积可观测量的制备惩罚。

Result: 发现大共形维数下代价度量与Fubini-Study度量成正比，而有限维数下对场位移与共轭动量的源扰动产生不同且均为正的惩罚，破坏比例性；梯度修正项引入模态依赖性并选择性改变位移代价。

Conclusion: 建立了bulk可观测量选择与边界态制备惩罚之间明确、局部的联系，为全息复杂度的物理诠释提供了新路径。

Abstract: Holographic complexity admits a broad family of bulk observables, yet their relation to boundary state-preparation costs remains unclear. We propose a local prescription that associates suitable bulk observables with quadratic cost geometries on families of states prepared by Euclidean sources. Building on the holographic transport of bulk Hamiltonian flows to Euclidean preparation sources, we use the Hessian of the transported generator at a stationary reference state to define a candidate cost metric. When this Hessian is positive definite, the minimised preparation energy reproduces the change in the bulk observable to quadratic order. We characterise the resulting preparation penalties relative to the Fubini-Study metric and compute them for the maximal volume in scalar perturbations of AdS$_3$. At large-conformal dimension, we recover the known proportionality between cost and Fubini-Study metrics. At finite dimension, we obtain positive but generally unequal penalties for source variations that displace the bulk field and its conjugate momentum, breaking this proportionality even for a single scalar operator. We further study a deformation of volume by quadratic scalar and gradient terms in the large-dimension regime, determining how the couplings modify the penalties and constrain positivity. These additions selectively change field-displacement costs, with the gradient term introducing mode dependence. Our results provide an explicit local connection between the choice of bulk observables and the assignment of boundary preparation penalties.

</details>


### [8] [3D de Sitter from complex Virasoro TQFT: Observers and knots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30247)
*Jeevan Chandra,Diego Liska,Boris Post*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维爱因斯坦引力中正宇宙学常数下的欧几里得鞍点，提出了一类球面圆锥流形上固定拓扑的精确引力配分函数，并通过解析延拓Virasoro TQFT配分函数的乘积构建；该方案在半经典极限下复现球面体积，并与引力壳上作用量一致，还关联了世界线算符相关函数、静态块标量两点函数、观测者反冲效应及扭结/Seifert/手术流形的球面体积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解正宇宙学常数下三维德西特引力中的量子几何结构，尤其是含重观测者的时空鞍点及其精确配分函数构造。

Method: 基于Virasoro拓扑量子场论（TQFT）配分函数乘积的精细解析延拓，构造球面圆锥流形上的精确引力配分函数，并结合超几何函数、球面体积计算、Dehn手术等数学工具进行验证。

Result: 得到了一类球面圆锥流形的精确引力配分函数；验证其半经典极限给出球面体积；建立了‘球面体积猜想’；将theta/tetrahedron图幅与观测者世界线上的两点/OTOC四点函数联系起来；复现了含背反应的静态块两点函数和观测者反冲效应；推广至扭结、Seifert流形及Dehn手术所得光滑球面三维流形，均与球面体积一致。

Conclusion: 球面圆锥几何为正宇宙学常数下三维引力提供了统一框架，Virasoro TQFT解析延拓是构造精确配分函数的有效途径，支持球面体积作为半经典引力作用量的核心角色，并拓展了全息对偶在dS空间中的潜在实现形式。

Abstract: The Euclidean saddles of 3D Einstein gravity with positive cosmological constant are spherical three-manifolds. When one adds a heavy observer, the backreacted saddles are spherical cone manifolds, with a conical singularity along the observer worldline. We propose exact gravitational partition functions at fixed topology on a class of spherical cone manifolds that undergo hyperbolic-to-spherical transitions as the cone angles increase. Our construction relies on a careful analytic continuation of the product of two Virasoro TQFT partition functions. We test the proposal by showing that the exact partition functions exponentiate in the semiclassical limit to the spherical volume, in agreement with the gravitational on-shell action. This leads to a spherical volume conjecture for the product of continued Virasoro TQFT amplitudes. As further evidence, we relate the theta and tetrahedron graph amplitudes to the two-point function and out-of-time-ordered four-point function of operators on a massive observer's worldline. In appropriate limits, we recover the static patch scalar two-point function with finite observer backreaction and the semiclassical effects of observer recoil. In addition, we analyze examples with knotted worldlines and reproduce their spherical volumes. We also apply our prescription to Seifert manifolds, which are off-shell in AdS$_3$ gravity but admit dS$_3$ saddles, and find agreement with their spherical volumes semiclassically. Finally, using Dehn surgery on torus knots and links, we obtain partition functions on smooth spherical three-manifolds.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在无局域源条件下，光滑紧致空间上实现非恒定扭曲的可能性，并研究其宇宙学演化与低能有效理论适用性。

Method: 构造三种具有不同内部通量（H₃、F̃₂、F̃₄）的解析双扭曲型IIA超引力解；推导其十一维提升与四维爱因斯坦帧宇宙学方程；分析哈勃参数与KK质量比以评估尺度分离；检验超对称性条件。

Result: 获得三个均打破全部IIA型超对称性的解析解；对应宇宙经历从大爆炸开始的减速膨胀、达最大尺度后收缩至大挤压；在爱因斯坦帧中短暂出现小Hubble/KK质量比（|H_E|/m_KK ≪ 1）。

Conclusion: 即使无局域源，光滑紧致流形上仍可存在非恒定扭曲解，但其宇宙学演化为非稳态（振荡型），且虽短暂满足低能有效描述所需尺度分离，却完全破坏超对称性。

Abstract: We construct three analytic, doubly warped cosmological solutions of massless type IIA supergravity with different choices of fluxes, namely, $H_3$, $\tilde F_2$ and $\tilde F_4$ along internal directions. The $H_3$ solution also belongs to the common NS--NS sector of type IIB. Our solutions show that, without localised sources, nonconstant warping on a smooth compact space is possible. We derive their eleven-dimensional uplifts and four-dimensional Einstein-frame cosmology. Each universe undergoes (decelerating) expansion from a Big Bang, reaches a maximum size, and undergoes contraction towards a Big Crunch. We also analyse the scale separation of these backgrounds and find that they exhibit a small Einstein frame Hubble scale to KK mass ratio $\frac{|H_E|}{m_{\mathrm{KK}}}\ll1,$ although for a brief period of time, around the maxima of the scale factor in the Einstein frame. Finally, we analyse the supersymmetry of these backgrounds, which demonstrates that all three solutions break all forms of type IIA supersymmetry.

</details>


### [10] [Pressure in Wilsonian effective field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30249)
*Vinícius Bernardes,Atakan Hilmi Fırat*

Abstract: Neural simulation-based inference has been shown to extract significantly more information from LHC data than histogram-based analyses. Abstract: We provide a fully quantum-mechanical description of the continuous measurement of wavelike dark matter. Abstract: Neutrino-nucleus cross sections are essential inputs for long-baseline neutrino oscillation experiments, yet remain challenging to model with high precision. Recent work demonstrated that fully black-box data-driven cross-section models cannot accurately generalize between near- and far-detector fluxes, but imposing Standard-Model symmetries via structure-function parameterizations provides a well-posed learning problem in a simplified DUNE-inspired muon-neutrino disappearance analysis. Here, we extend this framework to multiple lepton flavors and both neutrino and antineutrino beam modes, incorporating the electron appearance channel and enabling a simultaneous determination of $\sin^2θ_{23}$, $Δm^2_{31}$, and the CP-violating phase $δ_{\rm CP}$. We find that the learned cross section model recovers the injected oscillation parameters without significant bias, with constraints comparable to those obtained using the exact underlying cross section. Flux uncertainties have more significant effects in the multi-channel case, and incorporating flux nuisance parameters into the learning problem is required for accurate results. Our results demonstrate that this data-driven approach can be extended to the multi-channel measurements needed for long-baseline neutrino oscillation analyses.

Abstract: Discrete non-Abelian symmetries such as $\mathrm{A_4}$ are widely used in models of lepton flavor to explain neutrino mass hierarchy and their large mixing. If such a symmetry arises from the spontaneous breaking of a continuous group, cosmic strings can form. We study the breaking $\mathrm{SO}(3)\to \mathrm{A_4}$, for which the strings are classified by the non-Abelian group $π_1(\mathrm{SO}(3)/\mathrm{A_4})\cong \mathrm{T}'$, the binary tetrahedral group. Because the string fluxes do not commute, colliding strings may reconnect, pass through each other, or become joined by a bridge whose flux can belong to a different string network. We derive the probabilities of these outcomes directly from the group structure; in network models they are usually treated as free parameters. Including them in a multi-network velocity-dependent one-scale model, we find that the correlation length of the networks decreases and their energy density increases relative to Abelian $\mathrm{U}(1)$ strings of the same tension. The resulting stochastic gravitational-wave background is enhanced by up to nearly three orders of magnitude, showing that the algebraic structure of $π_1$ leaves a direct imprint on the gravitational-wave spectrum.

Abstract: We study neutrino masses in a six-dimensional framework with two compact dark dimensions. In the conventional flat-bulk realization, the sterile-neutrino zero mode is strongly volume suppressed, so a fundamental scale near $10~\mathrm{TeV}$ requires a large higher-dimensional Yukawa coupling to reproduce the observed neutrino masses, while the accompanying two-dimensional Kaluza--Klein tower is strongly constrained by oscillation data. We show that localizing the sterile-neutrino wave function near the Standard-Model brane substantially alleviates both effects. Using a Gaussian localizer, realistic Dirac neutrino masses are obtained with perturbative, order-one Yukawa couplings, while the brane-visible sterile excitations form a shell spectrum with $M_s^2=8μ^2 s$, and squared active--sterile mixings that decrease as $1/s$. The localization scale therefore separates the experimentally relevant sterile-neutrino spectrum from the gravitational compactification scale. We derive the resulting three-flavour structure and study its phenomenological consequences for reactor oscillations and tritium beta decay, including comparisons with current flat-LED and KATRIN constraints.

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the possibility that Dark Matter contains particles that interact with known matter only through gravity, the least interacting particles (LIPs). Specifically, we consider the case of a dark Proca vector field, which does not mix in the kinetic term with any of the vector bosons of the Standard Model if a discrete symmetry (Z2 ) is imposed. Breaking this symmetry introduces kinetic mixing. Here, we study two realization mechanisms, namely kinetic mixing with the electromagnetic field after electroweak symmetry breaking (Scenario A) and kinetic mixing with the weak hypercharge gauge field (Scenario B). We also consider generalized kinetic terms that violate parity and time-reversal symmetries.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The multiplicity distribution (MD) of charged particles in high-energy proton-proton collisions remains an intricate aspect of quantum chromodynamics (QCD). Traditional models, such as the Negative Binomial Distribution (NBD), were fitted and successfully described MDs at lower energies, but fail at higher energies, such as those probed by the Large Hadron Collider (LHC), where a ``shoulder-like'' structure emerges. To address this, we employ the Double Negative Binomial Distribution (DNBD), a weighted combination of two NBDs, representing contributions from soft and semi-hard processes. This model typically involves six free parameters, but by incorporating the $k_T$ factorization formalism and introducing an energy scale $Λ$, we effectively reduce the number of free parameters from six to four while maintaining a strong fit to high-energy MD data. The unintegrated gluon distribution used in this work is derived from the Golec-Biernat-Wüsthoff (GBW) model. Additionally, we investigate the KNO scaling and use the $k_T$ factorization formalism to describe the pseudo-rapidity distribution. Our approach demonstrates excellent agreement with recent experimental data, yielding low $χ^2$ values and highlighting the potential for improvements using more realistic gluon distribution model. This analysis provides valuable insights into the interplay of soft and semi-hard processes in high-energy collisions.

</details>

Abstract: Conventional estimates of $J/ψ$ weak decays, based on independent single-charm decays, suggest a branching fraction of order $10^{-8}$. We calculate the inclusive weak branching fraction using the optical theorem, including single-charm decay, charged- and neutral-current annihilation, and their interference. The heavy-quark expansion identifies the leading pair-annihilation structure and motivates vacuum saturation for the leading color-singlet component. With tree-level hard kernels and leading-logarithmic Wilson coefficients at $μ_0=M_{J/ψ}$, we obtain $B_{\rm weak}=(7.96^{+3.43}_{-0.43}\pm1.24)\times10^{-8}$. The contribution mediated by the $W$ boson alone, i.e. single-charm decays and $W$-exchange annihilation without the $W$-$Z$ interference, has a branching fraction of $(2.37^{+1.64}_{-0.42}\pm0.13)\times10^{-8}$. For the flavor sectors $(ΔC,|ΔS|)=(0,1)$ and $|ΔC|=1$, the branching fractions are $(4.97^{+16.44}_{-4.97}\pm0.99)\times10^{-10}$ and $(1.86^{+0.20}_{-0.06}\pm0.02)\times10^{-8}$, respectively, including charge-conjugate channels. The first uncertainty estimates missing perturbative corrections through scale variation over $1~\mathrm{GeV}\leqμ\leq3M_{J/ψ}$. The second is a hadronic uncertainty, obtained by jointly varying each vacuum-saturation and kinetic/chromomagnetic input within relative ranges of $Λ_{\rm QCD}/m_c\simeq20\%$. Abstract: We present the chiral Nelson-Barr relaxion model, an extension of the Nelson-Barr relaxion framework with a gauged chiral $\mathrm{U}(1)$ symmetry. Abstract: The weak mixing angle, $\sin^2θ_W$, is a fundamental parameter of the Standard Model and a sensitive probe of electroweak physics across various energy scales. At low momentum transfer, the Standard Model prediction approaches a well-defined low-energy plateau, providing a target for precision tests of the electroweak theory. At the lowest momentum transfer, atomic parity violation (APV) in cesium provides one of the most precise determinations of the weak mixing angle. Here, we review and update the status of this determination and compare it with other available probes. We then consistently reanalyze low-energy neutrino scattering data and combine the resulting determinations from coherent elastic scattering (CE$ν$NS) and neutrino-electron scattering. The complementary sensitivity of these processes to electroweak couplings enables a neutrino-based determination of the low-energy weak mixing angle, accounting for electroweak radiative corrections and relevant theoretical uncertainties. Our global analysis yields $\sin^2θ_W^{\rm low}(ν\,\text{data}) = 0.233^{+0.007}_{-0.006}$. We assess the consistency and relative impact of the individual neutrino measurements and discuss the prospects for future CE$ν$NS and neutrino-electron scattering experiments to improve the precision of this determination. Our analysis provides an independent test of the Standard Model at low momentum transfer, complementary to that provided by atomic parity violation.

Abstract: We present an updated global analysis of the transverse-momentum-dependent (TMD) helicity distributions of the nucleon from measurements of the longitudinal double-spin asymmetry $A_{LL}$ in semi-inclusive deep-inelastic scattering (SIDIS). The analysis incorporates target- and detected-hadron-mass corrections, next-to-next-to-leading-order matching in the small-$b_T$ operator product expansion, and an improved treatment of correlated systematic uncertainties. The present data constrain the up- and down-quark helicity TMDs, whereas the sea-quark and gluon distributions remain weakly constrained. After integration over transverse momenta up to the hard scale, the up- and down-quark distributions agree with collinear helicity distributions within uncertainties. Using the extracted TMDs and the Wandzura-Wilczek-type approximation, we predict the $\cosφ_h$ modulation of $A_{LL}$ and find agreement with the HERMES and CLAS measurements. We also provide predictions for SIDIS $A_{LL}$ at CLAS12 and for longitudinal single-spin asymmetries in $W^\pm$ production with leptonic decays at STAR kinematics. An impact study with pseudodata for the proposed Electron-Ion Collider in China (EicC) indicates substantial potential to improve constraints on helicity TMDs at intermediate $x$, including the presently weakly constrained sea-quark sector.

Abstract: Charged lepton flavor violation is an important probe of physics beyond the Standard Model. Although $ΔL_μ= -ΔL_e = \pm 1$ lepton flavor violating (LFV) processes, such as $μ\to eγ$ and $μ\to 3e$, are severely constrained experimentally, there is still plenty of room for LFV processes involving lepton number violation (LNV). In this paper, we investigate such processes at a $μ^+μ^+$ collider that remain even if processes with $ΔL_μ= -ΔL_e = \pm 1$ are forbidden by discrete symmetries. We consider processes induced by doubly charged scalars, which can be either an $SU(2)_L$ singlet or a component of an $SU(2)_L$ triplet. The cross sections of these processes at the collider depend on the representation of the scalar unless the muon beam is unpolarized. In the case of $ΔL_μ= -ΔL_e = 2$, we discuss the combination of the muonium-to-antimuonium transition and the $μ^+μ^+ \to e^+e^+γ/Z$ processes at the collider, and how the model parameters, such as the scalar couplings and mass, can be determined from experimental measurements. Doubly charged scalars are often utilized in models for generating neutrino masses, and they can also be used to construct models with LNV processes at a muon collider. If neutrino masses are generated at tree level, the LNV processes at the collider are suppressed, and therefore, we consider models that do not induce neutrino masses at tree level. LNV processes, such as $μ^+μ^+ \to t\bar b t\bar b$ and $μ^+μ^+ \to μ^+ e^- t\bar b t\bar b$, are possible in these models. Even if LNV is indicated by searches at hadron colliders, it is worthwhile to pursue these processes as clean LNV signals at a lepton collider.

</details>

Conclusion: 喷注内Λc+/D0比值的符号变化（增强vs.压低）可有效判别强子化机制；z^{ch}_{||}谱和径向结构为探测非碎裂强子化提供了与能量损失无关的新探针；该工作为未来喷注标记粲强子实验提供关键基线与预测。

Abstract: Jets separate the products of hard scatterings from the underlying event and thus help to clarify the origin of the enhanced baryon-to-meson ratios observed from small to large collision systems. In p+p collisions, simulated with PYTHIA8 using color reconnection (CR-BLC Mode 2) as a representative beyond-leading-color scenario, the in-jet ratio is enhanced at low-to-intermediate $p_T$ relative to the inclusive one---opposite to the light-flavor $Λ/K^0_S$ case---because the color-reconnection-induced excess of the ratio remains correlated with the jet and survives the jet requirement, while the fragmentation baseline shows no such enhancement. In Pb+Pb collisions, described by the SHELL model with a coalescence plus fragmentation hybrid hadronization, the in-jet ratio is instead suppressed moderately at intermediate $p_T$, because coalescence hadrons assembled from soft thermal partons carry no correlation with the jet direction and are preferentially discarded by the jet selection. The radial profile of the in-jet ratio rises with the distance from the jet axis in both systems, with the lowest-$p_T$ interval displaced farthest for color reconnection and the intermediate-$p_T$ interval for coalescence. These results provide the baseline and predictions needed for future jet-tagged charmed-hadron measurements in both collision systems.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高能物理中无监督共振异常检测问题，避免依赖先验信号模型，同时保持背景质量谱形状。

Abstract: RIDDLE is a new method for resonant anomaly detection that uses a scalar Stein-witness function to identify discrepancies between unlabeled signal-region events and a background distribution inferred from dijet-mass sidebands. The witness is optimized in a latent representation of auxiliary jet observables against a differentiable background reference, without requiring an explicit signal model. The method is evaluated using simulated LHC Olympics dijet events and compared with idealized and supervised benchmarks that share the same statistical construction but have access to additional signal or background information. RIDDLE demonstrates strong signal--background discrimination comparable to the idealized benchmark, while largely preserving the background dijet-mass spectrum under anomaly-score selection. Tests incorporating additional angular information and deliberately mass-correlated features further demonstrate the method's robustness under the studied conditions. Together, these results highlight the potential of RIDDLE as a robust, data-driven approach for enhanced resonance searches that combines effective anomaly discrimination with background-shape preservation.

Abstract: To match the experimental precision targeted by future collider programs, theoretical predictions for Drell-Yan production must incorporate higher-order mixed Quantum Chromodynamics and electroweak corrections. In this work, we present for the first time the ${\mathcal{O}}(α_s^2α)$ corrections to the inclusive cross-section for $Z$-boson production in the threshold limit. By systematically evaluating the missing singlet contributions, we obtain the complete set of ${\mathcal{O}}(α_s^2α)$ quark form factors, which we use to construct the soft-virtual cross-section by exploiting renormalization group invariance, the K+G evolution equation, and universal soft-collinear factorization. Our results capture the dominant threshold logarithmic enhancements, improve the theoretical scale uncertainties, significantly reduce electroweak input scheme dependence, and establish a crucial step toward complete ${\mathcal{O}}(α_s^2α)$ corrections indispensable for future precision programs.

Abstract: We review neutrino mass as a probe of restored parity in left-right symmetric theories, emphasizing the distinct Majorana and Dirac realizations. In the Majorana theory, lepton-number violation connects the seesaw mechanism to neutrinoless double beta decay, heavy-neutrino production and the Keung-Senjanovic process. The Dirac alternative removes these characteristic signals while retaining a relation between left- and right-handed flavor. The right-handed leptonic mixing matrix can then be reconstructed from low-energy data and the spontaneous parity-breaking parameter. Requiring the two lepton Yukawa matrices to admit a common real parity basis turns this reconstruction into a compatibility condition that determines the allowed Dirac phase on each branch. For three generations, this requirement reduces to a single rephasing-invariant CP-odd obstruction, which also governs the right-left modulus mismatch and the relative CP orientation. The small Dirac masses also restrict the parity deformation and its induced strong-CP phase. For compressed mixed-sign neutrino spectra, a tiny parity deformation can nevertheless generate large leptonic CP violation, despite stringent strong-CP constraints. The resulting branch-dependent correlations connect leptonic CP, the absolute neutrino mass and strong CP. We place this construction alongside radiative and unified approaches to predicting leptonic CP violation.

Abstract: Light dark matter can be boosted by cosmic-ray-dark matter interactions, which opens the possibility of direct-detection searches with large neutrino detectors. We further consider the possibility of utilizing directional information from the displaced vertex associated with the capture of the final-state neutron. However, we find that directional information does not materially improve the sensitivity due to the large nucleon scattering angle.

### [89] [SIGNN++: Robust Graph Neural Star Identification on Earth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30186)
*Jeongbin Sohn,Jina Lee,Max Yen,Dowan Gwon,Jeahn Han,Floyd Hepburn-Dickins,Brian Coltin,Ryan Soussan,Pyojin Kim*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: SIGNN++ 是一种基于学习的恒星识别（SID）框架，专为GNSS拒止的夜间陆地环境设计，通过改进噪声建模、引入傅里叶变换边缘特征和RANSAC姿态精化，显著提升了在真实陆地条件下的鲁棒性与识别精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的恒星识别方法主要在合成或非陆地环境中评估，其在真实陆地环境下的可靠性尚不明确，亟需提升对实际观测干扰（如大气扰动、光污染等）的鲁棒性。

Method: 在SIGNN图神经网络基础上，引入三方面改进：1）更贴近真实陆地观测的噪声模型用于训练；2）对角距离边特征进行傅里叶变换以增强表征能力；3）采用RANSAC优化的透视星图配准实现恒星识别与姿态精化。

Result: SIGNN++在真实陆地条件下显著优于原始SIGNN及公开基线方法，恒星识别准确率与求解率更高，姿态精度相当，且计算耗时满足连续运行需求。

Conclusion: SIGNN++有效提升了学习型恒星识别方法在GNSS拒止陆地导航中的实用性与可靠性，为深空与近地自主导航提供了可扩展的技术路径。

Abstract: We present SIGNN++, a learning-based star identification (SID) framework for celestial navigation in GNSS-denied nighttime environments. Although researchers have developed learning-based SID methods to improve robustness to observation noise, prior evaluations have focused mainly on synthetic or non-terrestrial environments, leaving their reliability under real terrestrial conditions unclear. To address these limitations, SIGNN++ extends SIGNN, a graph neural network-based SID method, with three components: a more realistic noise model for training data, Fourier Transform (FT) of angular-distance edge features, and RANSAC-based Perspective Star Alignment for identification and attitude refinement. The results show that SIGNN++ is more robust to terrestrial observation disturbances and achieves higher star identification accuracy and solved rates than the original SIGNN and publicly available SID baselines, while maintaining comparable attitude accuracy and practical computation time for continuous operation. The implementation code of SIGNN++ and datasets will be available at https://signnpp.github.io/signnpp/.

</details>


### [90] [Astro2Method: Benchmarking LLMs on Machine Learning Method Selection in Astronomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30282)
*Xingzhong Fan,Hangfeng He,Fatemeh Nargesian*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了Astro2Method基准，用于评估大语言模型在天文学中选择合适机器学习方法的能力；实验表明，直接提示在简单问题上表现良好，但在复杂问题上效果有限，而提供被遮蔽方法名的源论文流程描述可显著提升复杂问题的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准未充分探索大语言模型（LLMs）在天文学中辅助选择合适机器学习方法的能力，而该任务需兼顾天文与ML专业知识，存在实际需求。

Method: 构建了包含500个专家评审二元问题的Astro2Method基准（源自346篇天文论文），设计了Easy（完整解释）和Hard（部分解释）两类问题；对比测试了直接提示、RAG、多种智能体方法（如ReAct、Self-Ask、CRITIC）及网络搜索等策略，并引入遮蔽方法名的源论文流程描述作为输入。

Result: 直接提示在Easy问题上准确率达87.50–92.55%，Hard仅58.87–64.52%；RAG与多数智能体方法无一致提升；网络搜索仅在成功定位源论文时有效；而提供遮蔽方法名的源流程描述可显著提升Hard准确率，达到或超过网络搜索效果。

Conclusion: 相关实验信息（如源论文流程）对区分ML方法至关重要，但LLMs目前仍难以从相关文献或领域知识中可靠推断此类信息。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) is increasingly used in astronomy, but choosing a suitable method for a scientific problem often requires expertise in both astronomy and ML. Large language models (LLMs) could help astronomers make such choices, yet this ability remains underexplored in existing benchmarks. We introduce Astro2Method, a benchmark of 500 expert-reviewed binary questions from 346 astronomy papers (2024/01-2026/06), based on clear performance differences and complete (Easy) or partial (Hard) explanations from the authors. Across five LLMs, direct prompting achieves 87.50-92.55% accuracy on Easy questions but only 58.87-64.52% on Hard. Using historical papers, neither retrieval-augmented generation nor most agent methods with additional reasoning or retrieval (including ReAct, Self-Ask, and CRITIC) bring consistent improvement. Agents required to search the web improve accuracy mainly when they find the papers the questions are based on, with no consistent improvement otherwise. Providing the source paper's pipeline description with method names masked improves Hard accuracy, matching or exceeding web search. Together, these results suggest that relevant experimental information helps distinguish ML methods, but inferring it from related studies and domain knowledge remains challenging for LLMs in astronomy.

</details>


### [91] [A Technical Reanalysis of the Viking Carbon Assimilation-Pyrolytic Release Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30368)
*Jan Spacek,Clay A. Abraham,Steven A. Benner*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文重新评估了1976年维京号火星探测器的碳同化-热释实验（CAPyR）结果，指出高氯酸盐的存在可能导致有机碳在热解过程中被氧化，使放射性信号从Peak 2转移到Peak 1，从而影响对生物活性的判断；实验结果仍与存在耐热但热敏感的代谢生命形式一致，且尚无定量的非生物机制能同时解释CAPyR与标记释放实验（LR）的热响应模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视维京号CAPyR实验中可能被忽视的化学干扰（如高氯酸盐氧化效应），以更准确判断其是否支持火星存在生命。

Method: 基于已发表的CAPyR实验数据，结合高氯酸盐在热解中氧化有机碳的已知化学行为，分析Peak 1和Peak 2信号的可能来源及生物/非生物解释的兼容性，并对比Hubbard（1979）等矿物模拟实验。

Result: CAPyR中显著的Peak 2信号无法被现有非生物机制（包括矿物催化）在火星相关条件下完整复现；高氯酸盐的存在可能削弱Peak 2、增强Peak 1，导致低估碳同化；C5预处理（120°C/90°C）后仍有活性，而C2（175°C）则完全失活，呈现热敏感性；CAPyR与LR实验共同暗示同一土壤样品中存在热敏的氧化与还原代谢活动。

Conclusion: CAPyR结果仍与火星表层存在代谢活跃、热敏的微生物生命相容；当前尚无定量、全面的非生物模型能解释全部观测特征，尤其是与LR实验协同表现出的双热敏代谢模式，因此生命假说仍未被证伪。

Abstract: The 1976 Viking Carbon Assimilation-Pyrolytic Release (CAPyR) experiment tested whether carbon from $^{14}$CO$_2$ and $^{14}$CO entered Martian soil during a 120-hour incubation in a form retained during a 120 $^\circ$C purge but released by pyrolysis at 635 $^\circ$C. Untrapped radioactivity was measured as Peak 1; material retained in an organic vapor trap was oxidized and measured as Peak 2. Net Peak 2 was intended to represent synthesized reduced organic carbon. Seven of nine usable incubations at Chryse and Utopia Planitia yielded significant net Peak 2 signals, including the dark U1 run. An underreported Viking Lander 2 gas leak introduces uncertainty into U1-U3. The C1 signal corresponds to approximately 10 or 30 pmol of carbon, assuming derivation entirely from CO or CO$_2$, respectively. We reassess these results in light of the possible presence of perchlorate salts in Viking soils. Oxidation of fixed organic radiocarbon during pyrolysis could have shifted label from Peak 2 to Peak 1, a perchlorate-specific effect not considered in previous CAPyR analyses. Hubbard's (1979) mineral-substrate experiments produced abiotic Peak 2 signals without reproducing the heat sensitivity. No demonstrated abiotic mechanism has reproduced all CAPyR observations under Mars-relevant conditions. The observations remain consistent with a biological carbon-assimilation system whose relevant activity survives C5's 120 $^\circ$C/90 $^\circ$C pretreatment but is strongly impaired by C2's 175 $^\circ$C pretreatment. Together with the Labeled Release experiment, CAPyR suggests oxidative and reductive chemistry in separate aliquots from common Martian soil acquisitions. Metabolically active life combines these processes; a quantitative abiotic explanation of their combined heat-labile response patterns remains to be demonstrated.

</details>


### [92] [The Pandora SmallSat: post-launch operations and mission update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30372)
*Trevor O. Foote,Elisa V. Quintana,Jessie L. Dotson,Knicole D. Colón,Thomas Barclay,Pete Supsinskas,Jordan Karburn,Dániel Apai,Christina Hedges,Benjamin V. Rackham,Jason F. Rowe,David Ciardi,Emily A. Gilbert,Natalie H. Allen,Jessie Christiansen,Néstor Espinoza,Nguyen Fuda,Aylin Garcia Soto,Thomas P. Greene,Kelsey Hoffman,Rae Holcomb,Benjamin J. Hord,Aishwarya R. Iyer,Aurora Y. Kesseli,Veselin B. Kostov,Nikole K. Lewis,Sarah E. Logsdon,Andrew W. Mann,Megan Mansfield,James Mason,Peter McGill,Julie Moquin,Brett M. Morris,Gregory Mosby,Susan E. Mullally,Elisabeth R. Newton,Joe P. Renaud,Yoav Rotman,Suman Saha,Joshua E. Schlieder,Luis Welbanks,Lindsey S. Wiser,Allison Youngblood,Robert T. Zellem*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: Pandora SmallSat is a NASA mission to study exoplanet atmospheres via transmission spectroscopy while mitigating stellar contamination (e.g., star spots) using simultaneous visible photometry and near-IR spectroscopy.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stellar photospheric inhomogeneities—especially star spots—contaminate high-precision transmission spectra, hindering reliable retrieval of exoplanet atmospheric composition.

Method: Pandora employs simultaneous multiwavelength observations: visible-light photometry over a full stellar rotation period and near-IR transmission spectroscopy, enabling modeling and subtraction of stellar spot contributions.

Result: Enables disentanglement of stellar and planetary signals in transmission spectra, improving accuracy of exoplanet atmospheric composition measurements for Earth- to Jupiter-sized planets orbiting mid-K to late-M stars.

Conclusion: Pandora addresses a critical systematic limitation in exoplanet atmospheric characterization and advances the capability to reliably infer atmospheric properties, supporting future missions like JWST and shaping exoplanet science in the 2020s.

Abstract: The Pandora SmallSat is a NASA flight project designed to study the atmospheres of exoplanets. Transmission spectroscopy of transiting exoplanets provides our best opportunity to identify the makeup of planetary atmospheres in the coming decade, and is a key science driver for HST and JWST. Stellar photospheric inhomogeneity due to star spots, however, has been shown to contaminate the observed spectra in these high-precision measurements. Pandora will address the problem of stellar contamination by collecting long-duration photometric observations sampled over a stellar rotation period with a visible-light channel and simultaneous spectra with a near-IR channel. These simultaneous multiwavelength observations will constrain star spot covering fractions of exoplanet host stars, enabling star and planet signals to be disentangled in transmission spectra to then reliably determine exoplanet atmosphere compositions. Pandora will observe exoplanets with sizes ranging from Earth-size to Jupiter-size and host stars spanning mid-K to late-M spectral types. Pandora was selected in early 2021 as part of NASA's inaugural Astrophysics Pioneers Program. Herein, we present an overview of the mission, including the science objectives, operations, the observatory, science planning, and upcoming milestones as we prepare for launch readiness in 2025.

</details>


### [93] [Photometric classification and out-of-taxonomy detection of ZTF transients with FLARE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30656)
*Argyro Sasli,Felipe Fontinele Nunes,Xuanrui Zhang,Dimitrios Kourtesis,Saina Nikmehr,Alexandra Junell,Jesper Sollerman,Antoine Le Calloch,Sushant Sharma Chaudhary,Sneha Maharjan,Maojie Xu,Avyukt Raghuvanshi,Steven L. Groom,Joahan Castaneda Jaimes,Josiah Purdum,Roger Smith,Mansi M. Kasliwal,Matthew J. Graham,Ashish A. Mahabal,Michael Coughlin*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出FLARE，一个用于时域巡天数据的光度分类与异常检测模块，旨在提升对未知暂现源的识别能力，并已在ZTF巡天的Fritz平台实际部署运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光度分类器是时域巡天（如ZTF）中筛选需光谱观测目标的关键环节，也是发现训练类别之外新型暂现源的首要机会；现有方法对未见类别（out-of-taxonomy）暂现源检测能力有限，亟需兼顾分类精度与异常发现能力。

Method: 提出FLARE模块，结合光度分类与异常检测：利用目录上下文信息提升分类性能；采用盲检测与模拟人工复审循环评估异常发现能力；在冻结特征上添加新类别头（head）或组合异常分数进行新家族识别；并基于接收光度数据校准单位时间误报率。

Result: 在10497个ZTF光谱证认暂现源（6类）及53个未知族样本上验证：目录上下文显著提升macro F1；盲检测在1%误报率下召回率低，但模拟复审仅检查15%数据即可召回83%未知样本；对隐藏的潮汐撕裂事件（TDE），冻结特征+新头检测率达68%，优于异常分数组合（57%），重训练为新类后达85%；模块已在Fritz平台实时运行，报警以单位时间误报率形式输出。

Conclusion: FLARE有效平衡了高精度光度分类与新型暂现源发现能力，尤其通过特征空间再利用和渐进式复审策略显著提升对未知类别的敏感性，具备实际巡天部署价值。

Abstract: Photometric classifiers decide which of the million nightly alerts of time-domain surveys receive one of the few thousand spectra taken each year, and they are the first place where a transient outside the trained classes can be noticed. We present flare, the photometric classification and anomaly-detection module of AppleCiDEr, evaluated on 10 497 spectroscopically confirmed ZTF transients in six classes and 53 objects from families outside the taxonomy. Catalogue context raises the macro F1 score, with the largest gains where the defining information is absent from the light curve. Blind detection recovers few out-of-taxonomy objects at a 1% false-alarm rate, while a simulated review loop recovers 83% of them after inspecting 15% of the stream. The first confirmations of a new family are most useful in the feature space: with the same eleven labels, a head on the frozen features detects 68% of the tidal disruption events hidden from the training against 57% for a combiner over the anomaly scores, and retraining with them as a new class reaches 85%. The module runs in production on Fritz, the ZTF instance of SkyPortal, and its alarm is reported as a false-alarm rate per unit time on the stream, calibrated on the photometry that the service receives

</details>


### [94] [Astrometric detection of rocky exoplanets from inexpensive space platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30806)
*Peter G. Tuthill,George Ayad,Donald G. Dansereau,Thomas Holland,Connor J. Langford,Milo Langker,Kieran Larkin,Grace Piroscia,J. Gordon Robertson,Karel Valenta,Tim White,Asen Christov,Kyran Grattan,Sergey Karpov,Rico Landman,Pavel Ripka,Petr Schovánek,Jeffrey Smith,Frans Snik,Simon P. Worden,Ondřej Zelenka*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用小型、超稳定望远镜（口径<0.5米）进行高精度天体测量，以探测邻近单星（如Epsilon Eridani、Tau Ceti）周围类地行星的新光学概念，突破传统依赖遥远参考星的限制，实现微角秒级测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测邻近类太阳恒星周围地球质量行星是天文学核心目标，但传统天体测量受限于精度与参考星稳定性；窄角天体测量（如TOLIMAN）虽有进展，却仅适用于极少数多星系统，亟需拓展至孤立单星。

Method: 提出并仿真验证新型光学概念：采用口径小于0.5米的超稳空间望远镜，专用于孤立单星的窄角微角秒级天体测量；通过多年专用观测计划，在低成本轨道平台上实施。

Result: 仿真表明，该方案可对Epsilon Eridani、Tau Ceti等关键邻近系统开展全面类地行星搜寻，达到探测温带轨道上岩石行星所需的微角秒精度。

Conclusion: 无需旗舰级投入，基于超稳小型望远镜的窄角天体测量是探测太阳系近邻类地行星的可行且具成本效益的新路径，显著扩展了适用目标范围。

Abstract: The detection of orbiting exoplanets by way of astrometric monitoring of the host star to reveal gravitationally induced reflex motion requires extreme precision and measurement stability when targeting Earth-mass objects. Despite this, astrometry is widely regarded as the most promising avenue for the most desired of all targets: true Earth analogue systems in the solar neighbourhood. Formerly thought to require flagship class investment, recently several smaller concepts such as the TOLIMAN and SHERA missions have shown that detections are possible, at least in principle, from more modest space platforms. A key enabling feature is that these missions exploit narrow-angle astrometry. Observing only multiple star systems where the target provides its own astrometric reference greatly alleviates the challenges confronted by traditional astrometry with its reliance on distant, faint field stars. This principle underlies the TOLIMAN mission, for which progress and status is reported. However, only a tiny handful of nearby star systems are suited to such a TOLIMAN-like architecture. This paper discusses speculative optical concepts featuring ultra-stable telescopes of modest (<0.5m) aperture designed for astrometric monitoring of isolated single stars at the micro-arcsecond scales required for the discovery of Earth mass objects orbiting sun-like stars in temperate orbits. In simulation, highly sought-after neighbourhood systems such as Epsilon Eridani and Tau Ceti can be exhaustively searched for rocky planets with dedicated observing campaigns spanning several years, all from relatively inexpensive orbital platforms.

</details>


### [95] [Science-driven design and performance evaluation of a Silicon Laue Component lens for hard X-ray polarimetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31287)
*L. Abalo,N. M. Barrière,M. W. Beijersbergen*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文评估了一种基于硅晶体（SiLCs）的Laue透镜，用于80–200 keV能段对恒星级黑洞和强磁中子星进行X射线偏振测量；结果表明其在低于100 keV时可达约5%的偏振测量精度（MDP99），但在全频段未达该指标，仍为高偏振源及系统联合优化提供重要参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬X射线（>80 keV）难以用掠入射光学聚焦，Laue透镜利用晶体衍射提供替代方案；本文旨在评估基于硅晶体堆叠的Laue透镜在高能X射线偏振测量中的可行性与性能。

Method: 基于XL-Calibur型偏振计模型，推导80–200 keV波段对100 mCrab源观测100 ks所需的最小可探测偏振（MDP99）对应的有效面积要求；采用轴上光线追迹法，模拟18 m焦距、含Si(220)和Si(111)反射、63角秒带宽、90%填充因子的理想对准SiLC透镜性能。

Result: 该透镜在80–200 keV连续覆盖，有效面积从~375 cm²（80 keV附近）降至~200 cm²（180 keV）；5% MDP99需有效面积分别达260/400/740 cm²（三能段），而实际设计给出~320/250/220 cm²，对应MDP99约4.5%/6.4%/9.5%；焦点处50%和90%能量集中直径分别为5.4 mm和9.8 mm。

Conclusion: 该SiLC Laue透镜在<100 keV具备近似5%的偏振灵敏度，但未能在整个80–200 keV波段维持5% MDP99；适用于强偏振硬X射线源，并为光学、探测器响应与背景的端到端联合优化提供基准设计。

Abstract: Focusing hard X-rays above 80 keV is challenging for grazing-incidence optics. Laue lenses offer an alternative based on crystal diffraction. We assess a lens based on Silicon Laue Components (SiLCs), consisting of stacked silicon plates, for polarimetry of accreting stellar-mass black holes and strongly magnetized neutron stars in the 80-200 keV band.
  We derive effective-area requirements from the minimum detectable polarization at 99% confidence (MDP99) for an XL-Calibur-inspired polarimeter observing a 100-mCrab source for 100 ks. We evaluate an 18-m-focal-length lens combining Si(220) and Si(111) reflections, with a 63 arcsec diffraction bandpass and a 90% optical filling factor, using on-axis ray tracing of a perfectly aligned lens with uniform crystal curvatures.
  An MDP99 of 5% requires mean effective areas of approximately 260, 400, and 740 cm^2 in the 80-100, 100-130, and 130-200 keV bands, respectively. The lens spans radii of 356-1453 mm and continuously covers 80-200 keV. Its effective area reaches approximately 375 cm^2 near the low-energy boundary and falls to approximately 200 cm^2 at 180 keV. The focal-plane diameters enclosing 50% and 90% of the focused signal are 5.4 mm (62 arcsec) and 9.8 mm (112 arcsec). Mean effective areas of approximately 320, 250, and 220 cm^2 give reference MDP99 values of approximately 4.5%, 6.4%, and 9.5% in the three bands.
  The design reaches few-percent polarimetric sensitivity below 100 keV but does not maintain a 5% MDP99 across 80-200 keV under the adopted detector assumptions. It remains relevant for more strongly polarized hard components and provides a reference for end-to-end optimization of the optics, detector response, and background.

</details>


### [96] [Simulation-based inference of profile evolution for high-precision pulsar timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31450)
*Sai C. Susarla,Gregory Ashton,A. Parthasarathy,K. De Man,J. P. W. Verbiest*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于仿真推断的轮廓域计时方法，利用条件归一化流模型处理脉冲星频率-相位图，实现对色散量、到达时间及轮廓演化参数的快速联合后验估计，无需显式似然函数，便于集成多种物理效应（如非高斯噪声、抖动、散射等）到脉冲星计时流程中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星平均脉冲轮廓随观测频率系统性变化，若未建模会引入到达时间测量偏差，影响高精度计时；现有轮廓域计时方法依赖计算昂贵的随机采样，难以用于大规模分析。

Method: 提出基于仿真的推断方法：构建一个以卷积神经网络提取的频率-相位图特征为条件的归一化流模型，通过训练实现从单张频率-相位图直接输出色散量、到达时间及轮廓演化参数的联合后验分布。

Result: 该方法能从单张频率-相位图瞬时输出带不确定性的联合后验估计，且不确定性经已知真值验证可靠；支持将非高斯噪声、自噪声、抖动、散射和轮廓演化等复杂效应直接纳入仿真器，无需为每种效应重新推导似然函数。

Conclusion: 该似然自由方法将仿真器作为连接脉冲星物理属性的接口，显著提升轮廓域计时的效率与可扩展性，为在脉冲星计时中融合机器学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Pulsars are exceptional cosmic laboratories, the most stable of which rival atomic clocks. Nearly all of that precision rests on one observable, the average pulse profile unique to each pulsar and stable over decades. These profiles change shape systematically with observing frequency, reflecting the physics of the emitting pulsar beam, but if left unmodelled, it can bias the arrival-time measurements, undermining precision timing. Profile-domain timing has been proposed to address this effect, but current implementations use stochastic sampling, which is computationally prohibitive and has limited the application in large-scale analyses. Here, we present a simulation-based inference approach to profile-domain timing, using a normalising flow conditioned on a convolutional embedding of the frequency-phase portrait. From a single portrait, it returns joint posteriors on dispersion measures, arrival times, and the parameters governing profile evolution instantaneously, with uncertainties verified against known inputs following an initial training run. The value of a likelihood-free approach is that the simulator becomes the interface to pulsar properties. Fundamental properties like non-Gaussian noise, pulsar self-noise, jitter, scattering, and profile change can be inferred by simulating them rather than by deriving a new likelihood for each, opening a promising pathway to include machine learning methods into pulsar timing workflows.

</details>


### [97] [A Generalized Framework for Rapid Polarization Response Generation in Wide Field-of-View Compton Polarimeters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31559)
*Mohamed Gamal Zaid,Merlin Kole,Mark McConnell,Fabio Muleri,Hancheng Li*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文研究了伽马射线暴（GRB）在数十keV至约1 MeV能量范围内的偏振测量问题，提出了一种改进的康普顿偏振计建模方法，解决了宽视场平面偏振计中离轴入射导致的方位角散射角分布（ASAD）高次谐波问题，并通过GEANT4模拟验证，提出了两种高效建模模式，显著减少蒙特卡洛模拟次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRB偏振测量多依赖于轴上调制因子μ₁₀₀，但宽视场平面偏振计中GRB常以离轴方式入射，导致Klein-Nishina投影引入ASAD高次谐波，单一调制因子无法准确刻画；且缺乏在完整硬X射线/软γ射线能段内对离轴效应的无近似数值评估。

Method: 本文数值求解Klein-Nishina投影下的ASAD，覆盖整个GRB峰值能段（数十keV–1 MeV）及任意入射与偏振角；计入探测阈值影响；以POLAR为实例，提出两种建模模式：Mode 1仅需每能量点一个非偏振模拟；Mode 2额外增加两个特定偏振角的偏振模拟；均经GEANT4理想偏振计模拟验证。

Result: 定量揭示了离轴入射引入的高次谐波成分；Mode 1对绝大多数GRB可准确恢复POLAR实测偏振度（极亮GRB略有低估）；Mode 2可完全复现模拟ASAD至统计噪声水平，并精确重建所有测试GRB的偏振度；两种模式均已开源。

Conclusion: 单一调制因子不足以表征宽视场康普顿偏振计的离轴响应；本文提出的无近似数值模型与两种高效仿真模式，显著提升偏振分析精度与效率，适用于任意平面康普顿偏振计的设计与数据分析。

Abstract: Polarimetry is the most direct probe of the unsettled radiation mechanism and magnetic field structure of gamma-ray burst (GRB) prompt emission at the energies where it peaks, tens of keV to about 1 MeV. Compton polarimeters use the azimuthal scattering angle distribution (ASAD), a 180 degree modulation on axis. GRB measurements have largely relied on the on-axis modulation factor $μ_{100}$. For a wide field-of-view planar polarimeter, GRBs arrive off axis, where the Klein-Nishina projection adds harmonics to the ASAD. Muleri (2014) described this projection and evaluated it in a low-energy expansion for X-rays. We evaluate it numerically without approximation over the whole prompt-emission energy band for any incidence and polarization angles, quantify the off-axis harmonics a single modulation factor cannot capture, and verify the model against GEANT4 simulations of an ideal polarimeter. We also include the effect of the detection threshold on the ASAD. With POLAR as the case study, we present two modes that build the full polarization response with at least 95 percent fewer Monte Carlo simulations. Mode 1 takes only the unpolarized simulation per energy and recovers the polarization measured with POLAR's full GEANT4 responses for all but the brightest GRBs, where it underestimates the polarization degree. Mode 2 adds two polarized simulations per energy at distinct angles and reproduces the simulated ASADs to their statistical noise and the polarization of every GRB tested. Both modes are released as open-source software for any planar Compton polarimeter.

</details>

### [98] [Impact of Gaia-Sausage-Enceladus merger revealed by a single cosmic clock: thick-to-thin disc transition, gas disc shrinking and bar formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30237)
*Natsuki Funakoshi,Daisuke Kawata,Jason L. Sanders,Ioana Ciucă,Robert J. J. Grand,Noriyuki Matsunaga,Brian Thorsbro,HanYuan Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过重新校准Mira变星的周期-年龄关系，将其与APOGEE红巨星的年龄标度对齐，发现银河系核星盘（NSD）恒星形成高峰期（8.5–10.5 Gyr前）与盖亚-香肠-恩克朗达斯（GSE）并合末期高度重合，暗示GSE可能触发了银河系棒结构的形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究银河系棒的形成是否与最后一次重大并合事件——GSE并合相关；此前NSD和GSE的年代学基于不同恒星示踪体和年龄标度，无法直接比较。

Method: 利用Kepler星震学年龄训练的贝叶斯神经网络，将Mira变星周期-年龄关系校准至APOGEE红巨星年龄标度；结合Gaia DR3自行数据与基于作用量的准等温分布函数建模，并通过垂直速度弥散匹配Mira变量与红巨星的年龄依赖关系。

Result: NSD恒星形成上升与峰值被精确定位于8.5–10.5 Gyr前（红巨星年龄标度），与GSE引发的恒星爆发末期、厚盘向薄盘过渡及气体盘收缩时间一致。

Conclusion: 若NSD恒星形成确实标记棒形成，则GSE并合可能为棒的形成提供了种子，其带来的气体及潮汐扭矩驱动的外盘气体内流增强了银心区的重子主导性，从而促成棒的形成。

Abstract: Whether the formation of the Galactic bar was connected to the Gaia-Sausage-Enceladus (GSE) merger, the last significant merger experienced by the Milky Way, remains an open question. The bar formation epoch has been inferred from the star formation history of the nuclear stellar disc (NSD) traced by Mira variables, and the GSE merger epoch from the age-metallicity relation of red giants. However, these epochs rest on different stellar tracers with different age scales and cannot be compared directly. We recalibrate the Mira period-age relation onto the age scale of APOGEE red giants, with ages from a Bayesian neural network trained on Kepler asteroseismic ages. We fit the Gaia DR3 proper motions of 2394 O-rich disc Mira variables with an action-based quasi-isothermal distribution function whose parameters vary with period, and derive the period-age relation by matching their vertical velocity dispersion as a function of period to that of the APOGEE red giants as a function of age in Funakoshi et al. (2025). This places the NSD star-formation rise and peak at 8.5-10.5 Gyr ago on the red giant age scale, overlapping with the final stage of the GSE-induced starburst, thick-to-thin disc transition and the contraction of the star-forming gas disc. If NSD star formation traces bar formation, these coincidences suggest a possible scenario in which the GSE seeded the bar formation, while fresh gas supplied by the GSE and outer disc gas driven inward by its tidal torques, increased the baryonic dominance of the inner Galaxy.

</details>


### [99] [The THESAN-ZOOM project: the delayed rise of Lyman continuum escape fractions in bursty high-redshift galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30265)
*Parth Mehrotra,Aaron Smith,Yuan Bian,William McClymont,Ewald Puchwein,Rahul Kannan,Laura Keating,Xuejian Shen,Mark Vogelsberger,Sandro Tacchella,Matthew Hayes,Crystal L. Martin,Cody Carr,Anand Utsav Kapoor,Shyam Menon,Alaina Henry*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过THESAN-ZOOM辐射-流体动力学模拟与COLT辐射转移后处理，研究高红移星系中Lyman连续谱（LyC）光子逃逸与恒星形成历史的时间关联，发现LyC逃逸分数与近期恒星形成率比值（SFR₁₀/SFR₁₀₀）存在约16 Myr的延迟相关性，该延迟随星系质量减小而变长、变弱，并揭示了光子产生与气体透明度演化在时间上的协同作用对再电离贡献的关键影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高红移星系LyC光子逃逸的时序特征，以厘清其如何调控宇宙再电离过程；尤其关注恒星反馈打开逃逸通道与恒星形成衰减之间的动态时序关系。

Method: 基于THESAN-ZOOM模拟中13个z > 3的高分辨率晕，结合COLT辐射转移后处理，在~10 Myr时间分辨率下分析LyC逃逸分数与各类恒星形成指标（如SFR₁₀/SFR₁₀₀、Hα/UV比）的时序相关性及质量依赖性。

Result: 发现LyC逃逸分数与SFR₁₀/SFR₁₀₀呈显著正相关，典型延迟约16 Myr（质量越大延迟越短），相关系数0.4–0.6；延迟大致按M⋆⁻⁰.¹⁹和Mhalo⁻⁰.²⁷缩放；Hα/UV比也呈现类似延迟；但高透明度若发生在恒星形成已显著衰减期，则实际逃逸辐射仍微弱。

Conclusion: LyC逃逸并非仅由瞬时气体透明度决定，而是光子产生（近期恒星形成）与传输（反馈驱动的气体演化）在时间上重叠的结果；该时序特性对识别观测中的‘漏光星系’及其再电离贡献具有关键启示。

Abstract: The ionizing radiation that galaxies release into their surroundings depends on whether efficient Lyman continuum (LyC) escape coincides with strong photon production. Stellar feedback can open escape paths as the source population fades, linking the escaping radiation to a galaxy's recent history. We investigate this timing in thirteen high-redshift (z > 3) target haloes from the THESAN-ZOOM radiation-hydrodynamic simulations, post-processed with COLT radiative transfer and sampled at ~10 Myr intervals. The clearest delayed relationship links $\text{SFR}_{10}/\text{SFR}_{100}$ to the LyC escape fraction. Selecting the nearest positive cross-correlation peak gives typical lags of ~16 Myr and peak correlations of 0.4-0.6 in massive haloes, with longer and less coherent delays towards lower masses. The fitted delays scale approximately as $M_\star^{-0.19}$ and $M_\text{halo}^{-0.27}$, although the shortest lags approach the native snapshot spacing. Recurrent variations span multiple amplitude-dependent timescales, consistent with repeated episodes of star formation and feedback. Recent star formation closely tracks both intrinsic and escaping photon rates, while the escape fraction reflects the evolving gas transparency. The H$α$-to-UV luminosity ratio retains a delayed association with escape, offering an observable connection between burst phase and leakage. High transparency during a fading phase can nevertheless yield little escaping radiation. The temporal overlap of photon production and transmission therefore shapes both the amount and timing of escaping radiation, informing the interpretation of observed leakers and their contribution to cosmic reionization.

</details>


### [100] [The black hole-galaxy correlations of the 12 micron local AGN sample: a NuSTAR perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30285)
*M. Gliozzi,L. Spinoglio,J. A. Fernandez-Ontiveros,M. A. Malkan,J. K. Williams,M. Young. A. D. Oxley,S. Satyapal*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文检验了基于非活动星系校准的M-sigma关系是否适用于本地活动星系核（AGN）样本，并发现其预测黑洞质量的能力有限；同时发现AGN的MBH-Mgal关系与非活动星系不同，且Seyfert 1和HBLR星系的爱丁顿比高于Seyfert 2星系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证M-sigma关系在活动星系核（AGN）中是否仍可靠，因其被广泛用于校准RM方法、单历元法及直接估计窄线AGN黑洞质量。

Method: 利用本地代表性且偏差较小的12微米AGN样本，结合NuSTAR高质量X射线数据，采用X射线标度法估算黑洞质量，并与非活动星系的M-sigma和MBH-Mgal关系进行比较。

Result: 12微米AGN样本的M-sigma关系与非活动星系显著不同、弥散较大，预测能力有限；其MBH-Mgal关系与I型AGN一致但不同于非活动星系；Seyfert 1和HBLR星系的爱丁顿比系统性高于Seyfert 2星系。

Conclusion: 基于非活动星系校准的M-sigma关系不适宜直接用于AGN黑洞质量估计；AGN应建立独立的MBH经验关系；不同类型AGN可能存在不同的吸积物理状态。

Abstract: The M-sigma correlation of inactive galaxies plays a crucial role in estimating the black hole mass (MBH) in active galactic nuclei (AGN): 1) it is used to calibrate the reverberation mapping (RM) method; 2) the RM is the basis of the single epoch method, the most-used technique to determine MBH of broad-line AGN; and 3) in narrow-line AGN, MBH is often directly inferred from the M-sigma correlation. The main goal of this work is to test whether the M-sigma relation calibrated from inactive galaxies can be reliably applied to a local AGN population spanning Seyfert 1, Seyfert 2, and hidden broad-line region galaxies (HBLRs), and whether the resulting AGN relation has sufficient predictive power to be used as an indirect MBH estimator. To investigate this hypothesis, we used the 12 micron AGN sample, a local representative and largely unbiased sample. Since a substantial fraction of this sample has good-quality NuSTAR data, we estimated their MBH with the X-ray scaling method, which works for any AGN accreting at moderate or high level. We find that the M-sigma correlation of the 12 micron AGN sample is different from the one obtained with inactive galaxies and has a substantial scatter. Therefore, its predictive power for MBH estimates is fairly limited. We also find that the MBH-Mgal correlation based on the 12 micron AGN sample is consistent with the analogous correlation obtained from a large sample of nearby type 1 AGN and inconsistent with that of inactive galaxies. Finally, we find suggestive evidence that Seyfert 1 and HBLRs have similar and systematically higher Eddington ratios than Seyfert 2 galaxies.

</details>


### [101] [The LBT Y$_{\rm p}$ Project VI: Gas-Phase Chemical Abundance Patterns of Metal-Poor Nebulae](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30289)
*Noah S. J. Rogers,Evan D. Skillman,Richard W. Pogge,Erik Aver,Miqaela K. Weller,Danielle A. Berg,John J. Salzer,John H. Miller,Jayde Speigel,Allison L. Strom*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过大型双目望远镜对62个低金属丰度星云的光学与近红外观测，系统测定了气体相中N、O、Ne、S、Cl、Ar、K和Fe等元素的丰度，并分析其随金属丰度（O/H）的演化规律；发现Ne/O、S/O、Ar/O恒定且接近太阳值，Cl/O和K/O亦不随O/H变化，支持奇Z元素质子-α过程起源；Fe/O因尘埃消光而偏低，校正后[Fe/O] = −0.80，暗示存在能产生强电离辐射的贫铁恒星；N/O在多数星云中呈平台状（log(N/O) = −1.43±0.10），但5个星系显著偏高，作者探讨了其成因与系统误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定原初氦丰度Yp需准确理解低金属丰度星云中各类金属元素的丰度及电离校正，尤其需厘清不同元素随金属丰度的演化行为，以区分原初核合成、Ia型超新星、α过程及尘埃消光等物理效应。

Result: Ne/O、S/O、Ar/O恒定且与太阳值一致；Cl/O和K/O不随O/H变化，首次报道低金属星系中K丰度；Fe/O随O/H下降，尘埃校正后平均[Fe/O] = −0.80；log(N/O)在50个星云中呈稳定平台−1.43±0.10，5个星系显著偏离该平台。

Conclusion: 低金属丰度星云中多数α相关元素丰度比恒定，反映其共同起源与早期快速核合成；Cl、K行为类似α元素，支持奇Z元素的质子-α过程主导；Fe亏损表明尘埃形成显著，且暗示存在能提供硬电离光子的贫铁恒星；N/O平台及其异常体揭示了初级氮与次级氮贡献的分界，对星系化学演化与恒星反馈建模具有关键约束。

Abstract: Through a combined rest-optical and NIR observing campaign, the Large Binocular Telescope Primordial He Abundance ($Y_{\rm p}$) Project has delivered a precision measurement of $Y_{\rm p}$. Given the stringent requirements to measure gas-phase He abundances, the optical spectra contain emission features from a plethora of metals in the Interstellar Medium (ISM). We present gas-phase N, O, Ne, S, Cl, Ar, K and Fe abundance patterns in 62 low-metallicity nebulae. We assess ionization correction factors for each element and select those that reproduce the observed ionic abundance trends. The Ne/O, S/O, and Ar/O abundances do not evolve with metallicity and agree with the solar ratios. Given the Type Ia enrichment of S and Ar, the solar S/O and Ar/O suggest extended star formation histories in local galaxies relative to high-$z$ galaxies at fixed O/H. We report the first ISM K abundances in low-metallicity extragalactic nebulae; both Cl/O and K/O are constant with O/H, indicating the production of these odd-Z elements follows that of $α$ elements. Consistent with prior analyses, gas-phase Fe/O is observed to decrease with O/H due to dust depletion; correcting for this produces an average [Fe/O]$=$$-$0.80, implying Fe-deficient stars capable of producing hard ionizing spectra. Finally, the metallicity-insensitive log(N/O) plateau from 50 nebulae is $-$1.43$\pm$0.10, consistent with previous works. Five galaxies have N/O significantly enhanced relative to the primary plateau, and we discuss their properties, mechanisms that could bias measurements of N/O, and nucleosynthetic progenitors that can produce enhanced N at low metallicity.

</details>


### [102] [The PAU Survey: Active galactic nucleus galaxies in galaxy pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30309)

García-Bellido,H. Hildebrandt,M. Mezcua,R. Miquel,C. Padilla,P. Renard,E. Sánchez,S. Serrano,I. Sevilla-Noarbe,P. Tallada-Crespí*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本研究利用PAUS巡天的高精度测光红移数据，探究了红移z~1.0以内活动星系核（AGN）与星系对之间的关联，发现AGN在星系对中呈现随红移增加而变红的趋势，且更倾向于出现在动力学演化更先进的相互作用系统中，表明星系相互作用在中等红移下对触发AGN活动起着日益重要的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究活动星系核（AGNs）与星系对之间的物理联系，特别是在中等红移（z~1.0）范围内，以理解星系相互作用是否及如何触发AGN活动。

Method: 基于PAUS巡天数据识别出16,824个投影距离rp < 100 kpc、径向速度差ΔV < 3500 km/s的星系对；通过多波段AGN星表交叉证认得到270个成对AGN；构建红移、绝对星等和环境匹配的三类对照样本：孤立AGN、成对非AGN、孤立非AGN。

Result: 成对AGN的静止系g−r色指数随红移（z≳0.5）显著变红（M_g−M_r增大），而孤立AGN与成对非AGN无明显颜色演化（维持在~0.65）；含AGN的星系对更常表现为形态扰动、更小投影距离和更小速度偏移，符合更先进动力学相互作用特征。

Conclusion: 结果证实AGN活动与星系相互作用之间存在红移依赖关系，在中等红移下相互作用对触发AGN核活动的作用增强；凸显PAUS巡天在超越本地宇宙、高统计精度研究AGN-星系相互作用方面的独特能力。

Abstract: Aims. Leveraging the high-precision photometric redshifts of the Physics of the Accelerating Universe Survey (PAUS), we aim to investigate the connection between active galactic nuclei (AGNs) and galaxy pairs up to redshifts of $\sim1.0$. Methods. We identified 16,824 galaxy pairs with projected separations of $r_p < 100$ kpc and radial-velocity differences of $ΔV < 3500$ km s$^{-1}$. AGNs were selected by cross-matching PAUS galaxies with multi-wavelength AGN catalogues, yielding 270 AGNs in pairs out of a parent sample of 8,880 AGNs in PAUS. We constructed control samples of isolated AGNs; non-AGNs in pairs; and isolated non-AGNs matched in redshift, absolute magnitude, and environment. Results. Active galactic nuclei in pairs show progressively redder rest-frame colours with increasing redshift, particularly at redshifts of $z\gtrsim 0.5$, whereas isolated AGNs and non-AGNs in pairs show no significant colour evolution and remain at $\mathrm{M_g-M_r}\sim0.65$. Pairs hosting AGNs are also preferentially morphologically disturbed interacting systems found at smaller projected separations and velocity offsets, consistent with more dynamically advanced interactions. Conclusions. These results provide evidence of a redshift-dependent link between galaxy interactions and AGN activity, suggesting that interactions play an increasingly important role in triggering nuclear activity at intermediate redshifts. This study highlights the unique capability of PAUS to probe AGN-galaxy interaction connections beyond the local Universe with unprecedented statistical power.

</details>


### [103] [Optical-Infrared Extinction Variation in Nearby Molecular Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30336)
*Marshall Hobson-Ritz,Sarv Parteek Singh,Eddie Schlafly,Lee Mundy,Alberto Bolatto*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用APOGEE光谱巡天与多波段测光数据，测量了9个邻近致密分子云的消光曲线参数R(V)，发现R(V)与色余E(B-V)大多呈正相关，但相关性强弱因云而异；且高相关性云中R(V)弥散度大，细分区域后显著减小，表明除密度外的局地环境因素影响R(V)-E(B-V)关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尘埃消光曲线是探测尘埃颗粒性质（尤其是尺寸分布）的重要工具；致密分子云中因辐射屏蔽增强及尘粒-尘粒、尘粒-气体碰撞频率上升，尘埃性质预期发生显著变化。

Method: 利用APOGEE光谱巡天数据结合PS1、2MASS和WISE的十波段测光数据，测量9个邻近致密分子云的消光曲线形状参数R(V)，并分析其与色余E(B-V)的关系及云内空间变化。

Result: 多数分子云中R(V)与E(B-V)呈正相关，但相关性强弱差异显著；强相关云中R(V)在固定E(B-V)下弥散度高，细分区域后弥散度大幅下降。

Conclusion: R(V)-E(B-V)关系不仅受密度影响，更受局地环境条件（如辐射场、动力学过程等）调控。

Abstract: The dust extinction curve is an important probe of the properties of dust grains, particularly their size distribution. The properties of dust grains are expected to change significantly in dense molecular clouds due to shielding from interstellar radiation and increased frequency of grain-grain and grain-gas collisions. We measure the dust extinction curve shape parameter R(V) in 9 nearby, dense molecular clouds using data from the APOGEE spectroscopic survey, in combination with ten-band photometry from PS1, 2MASS, and WISE. We analyze the results to determine how the extinction curve varies in nearby molecular clouds. We find that most molecular clouds show a positive correlation between R(V) and E(B-V), but the strength of the correlation varies significantly between clouds. In addition, we find that the clouds with the strongest correlation tend to have very high dispersion in R(V) at fixed E(B-V), which tightens dramatically when clouds are subdivided into separate regions. This suggests that local environmental conditions beyond density shape the R(V)-E(B-V) relation.

</details>


### [104] [Optical and near-infrared s-process abundance scales in the Galactic disc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30346)
*Andrés E. Piatti*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文揭示了光学与近红外（NIR）光谱在钕（Nd）s-过程丰度测量中存在系统性偏差，发现SDSS-V/NIR管道因H波段分子森林（CN/CO）导致的连续谱压制而人为放大Nd丰度变化达5.54倍；提出经验校准方法，消除了NIR数据中虚假的负径向梯度，证实该偏差是谱线遮蔽引起的观测假象，而非真实银河演化趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决近期光学与近红外s-过程丰度尺度（尤其是钕Nd）之间出现的不一致问题。

Method: 通过Gaia-ESO（光学）与SDSS-V/MWM（近红外）数据的直接交叉匹配，分析大气参数及Ce、Nd丰度一致性；诊断APOGEE H波段分子森林对连续谱的压制效应及其对chi^2拟合丰度反演的影响；建立经验校准框架以统一两种波段结果。

Result: 发现Nd在NIR中的丰度变化被系统性放大5.54倍；识别出高金属丰度下CN/CO分子森林导致局部连续谱下降，迫使chi^2最小化算法高估Nd丰度；校准后消除了NIR中虚假的-0.052 dex/kpc负径向[Nd/Fe]梯度。

Conclusion: Nd丰度在近红外波段的异常扩展是谱线遮蔽（line-blanketing）导致的光谱学假象，而非真实的银河化学演化信号；光学与校准后的NIR结果一致支持正向[Nd/Fe]径向梯度。

Abstract: A discrepancy has recently emerged between optical and near-infrared (NIR) s-process abundance scales, particularly for Neodymium (Nd). We investigated this issue using a direct cross-match between the optical Gaia-ESO Survey (DR5) and NIR SDSS-V/MWM (DR19). While atmospheric parameters and Cerium (Ce) abundances exhibit robust 1:1 correlations, Nd displays a systematic X-shaped vertical stretching. Our analysis reveals that the SDSS-V pipeline expands intrinsic Nd variations by a scaling factor of 5.54 relative to the stable optical baseline. This phenomenon is driven by baseline compression in the APOGEE H-band, where the growth of the molecular forest (CN and CO) at high metallicities depresses the local continuum. This forces the chi^2 minimisation engine in the NIR abundance determination to inflate derived abundances to reach mathematical convergence. We provide an empirical calibration framework to reconcile these disparate regimes. This correction effectively eliminates the contradictory negative radial [Nd/Fe] gradient of -0.052 dex/kpc reported in recent NIR surveys, bringing them into alignment with the rising trends established by the optical high-resolution Gaia-ESO survey. These results demonstrate that the observed scale expansion is a spectroscopic artefact of line-blanketing rather than a physical Galactic trend.

</details>


### [105] [JWST observations of the planetary nebula NGC 6302 IV: PAH spectral characteristics, formation and evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30441)
*Nicholas Clark,Els Peeters,Jan Cami,Mikako Matsuura,Alexander G. G. M. Tielens,Patrick J. Kavanagh,Michael J. Barlow,Joel Kastner,Kathleen E. Kraemer,Hektor Monteiro,Raghvendra Sahai,G. C. Sloan,N. C. Sterling,Jeremy R. Walsh,L. B. F. M. Waters,Nicholas J. Wright,Isabel Aleman,Bruce Balick,Jeronimo Bernard-Salas,Charmi Bhatt,Joris Blommaert,Harriet L. Dinerstein,Naomi Hirano,Olivia Jones,Francisca Kemper,Eric Lagadec,Paula Moraga Baez,Maryam Torki,Peter A. M. van Hoof,Kevin Volk,Roger Wesson,Albert Zijlstra*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用JWST MIRI IFU光谱观测研究富氧行星状星云NGC 6302中多环芳烃（PAHs）的形成与演化，发现PAHs可能在当前行星状星云阶段原位形成，源自CO光解产生的小分子烃类，并揭示其尺寸、电荷态和边缘结构的空间变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管在富氧行星状星云（PNe）中已观测到多环芳烃（PAHs）和碳质尘埃，但其在该环境中的形成与演化机制仍不明确。

Method: 利用JWST MIRI IFU对NGC 6302中心源进行中红外积分场光谱观测，提取四个表征PAH发射空间变化的模板光谱，分析主要及次要PAH特征（如6.2、7.7、8.6、11.2、12.7 μm等）的强度、宽度、峰值波长及比值，并据此推断PAH电荷态、尺寸分布与边缘结构。

Result: 检测到典型的PAH谱带，其中11.2 μm为B类（常见于PNe），6–9 μm为A类（PNe中较少见）；平均75% PAH呈阳离子态；PAH尺寸与结构存在空间变化，从大而松散到小而致密；PAHs很可能在当前PN阶段原位形成，源于CO光解产生的小分子烃。

Conclusion: NGC 6302中的PAHs并非继承自前驱星阶段，而是在富氧PN环境中通过小分子烃的气相化学途径新形成，其物理化学性质（尺寸、电荷、边缘结构）受局部辐射场与屏蔽条件调控。

Abstract: Polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbons (PAHs) and carbonaceous dust have been observed in Oxygen-rich planetary nebulae (PNe), yet their formation and evolution in these environments is an open question. We aim to study the formation and evolution of the PAHs in the O-rich PN NGC 6302, by characterizing the PAH emission throughout this object. We obtained JWST MIRI IFU spectroscopic observations of NGC 6302 pointed at its central source. From these, we extract four template spectra that are representative of the variation in PAH emission throughout NGC 6302. We detect the major PAH features at 6.2, 7.7, 8.6, 11.2, and 12.7 $μ$m, and several minor PAH features. The template spectra vary in terms of their PAH feature widths, peak wavelengths, and relative intensities. The 11.2 $μ$m PAH feature is class B, typical for PNe. The 6-9 $μ$m PAH features are class A, which is less common for PNe. We estimate the PAH charge to be 75% cations on average, based on the 7.7/11.2 PAH feature ratio. The relative strengths of the 11-15 $μ$m PAH features indicate a range of PAH edge structures. To ensure consistency in our observations and explain variable PAH feature widths, we propose a variation in the size of the PAHs throughout NGC 6302, and suggest variation in the shielding of these PAHs. The PAHs range from large and non-compact, to small and compact. We find that NGC 6302 formed PAHs in-situ in the current PN phase, likely from small hydrocarbons formed by the photodissociation of CO.

</details>


### [106] [A Potential New Dimension of Astrophysical Aromatic Functionalization: Phosphaethynyl (CP) Derivatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30509)
*Reace H. J. Willis,Vincent J. Esposito,Thomas H. Speak,Ilsa R. Cooke,Ryan C. Fortenberry*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过量子化学计算提出了一种在低温气相中由环丙烯亚基（c-C3H2）与磷炔基（CP）自由基反应生成磷炔基环丙烯亚基（c-C3HCP）的热力学可行路径，并探讨了CP与苯、萘等芳香分子反应生成相应CP取代芳烃的可能性；计算提供了这些分子的振动-转动光谱参数，指出其红外探测困难但射电探测可行，预示CP取代芳香分子可能成为天文化学新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴于c-C3H2和CP已在星际环境中被探测到，作者旨在探索它们反应生成新型含磷有机分子（如c-C3HCP及CP取代PAHs）的可能性，并评估其天文观测可行性。

Method: 采用量子化学方法（包括显式关联耦合簇四阶力场、二阶振动微扰理论、密度泛函理论）研究反应路径、过渡态、振动频率与转动常数。

Result: 发现c-C3H2 + CP反应为无势垒过程，可高效生成c-C3HCP；CP + 苯反应存在方法依赖的微弱过渡态；计算获得c-C3HCP、C6H5CP及CP-萘异构体的高精度振转参数；预测这些分子红外信号弱且易被PAH特征掩盖，但因具有大偶极矩，适合射电波段探测。

Conclusion: c-C3HCP是值得在富磷天体环境中搜寻的候选分子；CP与芳香分子的反应可能开启一类新型天文芳香官能团化分子的研究前沿，但CP取代PAHs的形成路径仍需进一步确认。

Abstract: Quantum chemical computations outline a thermodynamically viable, low-temperature, gas-phase formation pathway for phosphaethynyl cyclopropenylidene ($c$-C$_3$HCP), resulting from cyclopropenylidene ($c$-C$_3$H$_2$) and the phosphaethynyl (CP) radical. Given the detections of both $c$-C$_3$H$_2$ and the CP radical toward circumstellar shells along with the provided reaction pathway, $c$-C$_3$HCP is a potentially present astro-molecule which could be searched for in phosphorus rich environments. The reaction of CP and benzene (C$_6$H$_6$) to produce phosphaethynyl benzene (C$_6$H$_5$CP) is also explored. A marginally emerged, but tentative, transition state is identified for this reaction using certain quantum chemical approaches but not others. Consequently, the formation of CP-substituted benzoid polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbons (PAHs) in astrophysical environments from CP + PAH reactions is possible but currently uncertain. Additionally, explicitly correlated coupled cluster quartic force field and second-order vibrational perturbation theory rovibrational analysis of the vibrational frequencies and rotational constants of $c$-C$_3$HCP are provided, as well as density functional theory predictions of the same for CP-benzene (C$_6$H$_5$CP) and both isomers of CP-naphthalene (C$_1$$_0$H$_7$CP). Our results suggest that IR searches for these molecules would likely be unsuccessful due to the low intensities and coincidence with PAH features, but their large dipole moments could make them feasible to detect with radio astronomy. The barrierless formation of $c$-C$_3$HCP and possibly barrierless synthesis of C$_6$H$_5$CP stand as examples for future investigations of other CP-substituted aromatic molecules, marking the potential beginning of a new frontier of astronomical aromatic functionalization.

</details>


### [107] [Rest-frame Ultraviolet Variability of Quasars in the Cosmic Himalayas Overdensity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30570)
*Jiaqi Lin,Zhenya Zheng,Bin Ma,Yongming Liang,Lin Long*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了高红移超密环境（CH）中类星体的紫外光变特性，发现其阻尼时间尺度略短、渐近振幅略高，但统计显著性不足；模拟表明需约60个类似质量的样本才能以80%概率达到3σ显著性检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大尺度环境是否不仅影响类星体触发，还影响其光变行为。

Method: 利用CH区域11个z≈2.18的I型类星体，结合SDSS、PS1和ZTF多源测光数据构建长达27年的g/r波段光变曲线；通过DRW参数与系综结构函数（SF）对比匹配控制样本，并开展注入-回收模拟评估偏移可探测性。

Result: CH类星体在g/r波段呈现较短DRW阻尼时间（中位偏移−0.32/−0.13 dex）和略高渐近振幅；所有DRW比较均不显著（最大p=0.16）；系综SF略高于控制样本，但绝大多数滞后 bins 仍在95%置信包络内；模拟预测−0.32 dex真实偏移需约60个高质量类星体才有望达3σ显著性。

Conclusion: 目前结果仅提示大尺度环境可能影响吸积流光变，尚无确凿证据；未来LSST等更大更精密样本将对此提供关键检验。

Abstract: Some high-redshift overdensities show an enhanced incidence of AGNs, suggesting that large-scale environment may influence supermassive black hole fuelling and triggering. Whether environment also affects AGN variability remains unclear.Using the Cosmic Himalayas (CH), an extreme concentration of 11 type 1 quasars at z=2.16-2.20, we investigate possible offsets in rest-frame ultraviolet variability relative to quasars with comparable redshifts, black hole masses, and Eddington ratios.We construct a parent sample of 1,138 Stripe 82 quasars at 2.11<=z<=2.25 and combine photometry from the SDSS, the PS1, and the ZTF to build observer-frame g- and r-band light curves spanning up to 27~yr.
