We compare DRW parameters and ensemble structure functions (SFs) with control pseudo-samples matched in black hole mass and Eddington ratio, and use injection--recovery simulations to forecast the detectability of a CH-like offset.The CH quasars show a tendency toward shorter DRW damping timescales (median offsets of -0.32 and -0.13 dex in g and r) and slightly higher asymptotic amplitudes. None of the four DRW comparisons is statistically significant, including the largest offset in the g-band damping timescale (p=0.16). Ensemble SFs generally exceed matched-control medians, although 18 of 19 lag bins per band remain within the 95% pointwise envelopes. Assuming a true uniform -0.32 dex g-band timescale offset, injection--recovery simulations predict an 80% probability of nominal 3sigma detection with approximately 60 quasars of comparable variability scatter and data quality.If confirmed, the tentative variability offsets may suggest that large-scale environment influences accretion-flow variability beyond its potential role in quasar triggering. Larger samples with higher-precision light curves, such as those expected from the LSST, are required to test this possibility.

</details>


### [108] [Lomb-Scargle Recovery of Periodic Binary Signals across Black Hole Mass and Cadence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30772)
*Karan Akbari,Sayantan Bhattacharya*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过在TNG100-1宇宙学模拟的并合光变曲线中注入周期信号，检验Lin等人（2026）提出的超大质量双黑洞搜寻中的信号恢复率，并发现其对质量、光度设定与观测采样策略高度敏感；尤其指出LSST似采样下正弦信号恢复率显著提升，但锯齿信号仍难以探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Lin等人（2026）基于PTF数据得出的周期信号恢复分数在更广质量范围（跨越四个数量级）和不同观测采样策略下的普适性与可靠性，尤其是针对超大质量双黑洞搜寻的关键效率指标。

Method: 将Lin等人使用的正弦波和锯齿波信号注入TNG100-1模拟中黑洞并合产生的光变曲线；光度依据SDSS DR7类星体质量-光度关系（低质量端外推）赋值；采用PTF实际采样、Stripe 82采样及LSST-like采样三种策略；在各频率bin中使用10^5条纯噪声光变曲线设定Lomb-Scargle检测阈值。

Result: 在固定绝对星等Mi=−23时，正弦信号恢复率随黑洞质量升高而下降；但使用DR7实测光度关系时，恢复率在~10^8 M⊙以上反而上升；PTF实际数据中正弦恢复率为39.8%，低于Lin等人报告的44.7%；LSST-like采样下升至76.9%，远高于其报告的22.6%；锯齿信号在所有测试中（含LSST采样）于>10^8 M⊙时仍大多未被检出；提供了按0.5-dex质量区间和采样策略划分的恢复率表格。

Conclusion: Lin等人所用恢复分数存在系统性偏差，其依赖于光度-质量关系假设与采样策略；LSST将大幅提升正弦信号探测能力，但对物理上更真实的锯齿信号探测仍受限；未来研究需结合更真实的光变模型与采样模拟以校准探测效率。

Abstract: Searches for periodic supermassive black hole binaries in quasar light curves rely on recovery fractions, the share of injected signals detected, which forecasts use as detection efficiencies. Lin, Charisi, & Haiman (2026) measured them for the Lomb-Scargle periodogram on damped-random-walk light curves matched to 12,400 bright, high-mass Palomar Transient Factory (PTF) quasars with Sloan Digital Sky Survey Data Release 7 (DR7) masses. To test them over four decades of mass, we inject their sinusoidal and sawtooth signals into light curves of mergers in the TNG100-1 cosmological simulation, with magnitudes drawn from DR7 quasars of similar mass (extrapolated at low mass). We apply their threshold in each frequency bin, set by $10^5$ noise-only light curves per binary, on observed PTF cadences, one Stripe 82 cadence and an LSST-like cadence (Legacy Survey of Space and Time). At fixed absolute magnitude $M_i=-23$, sinusoid recovery falls toward high mass, and with DR7 magnitudes it climbs above about $10^8\,M_\odot$, so the magnitude assignment sets the sign of the trend. Above $10^8\,M_\odot$, where their sample lies, PTF sinusoid recovery is 39.8 against their 44.7 per cent. At LSST-like cadence sinusoid recovery is 76.9 per cent, about three times their 22.6 per cent, for reasons mostly unidentified. Denser sampling of the same PTF baselines did not lower recovery in any test. Even at LSST-like cadence we miss most sawtooth signals above $10^8\,M_\odot$. We tabulate recovery per 0.5-dex mass bin and cadence, with low-mass values likely too high for a deeper survey.

</details>


### [109] [The Milky Way's Early Disk Was Built in Bursts. II. The Multi-component Age-Metallicity Structure of the High-$α$ Disk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30659)
*Tiancheng Sun,Maosheng Xiang,Jifeng Liu,Meng Zhang,Yaqian Wu*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过分析约14.5万颗Gaia DR3次巨星的年龄-金属丰度分布，揭示高α盘存在两个主要恒星形成成分（分别峰值在~10.6 Gyr和~12.4 Gyr），支持其恒星形成历史是间歇性而非连续的，并可能与早期气体吸积事件（如Low-energy–Kraken–Heracles）相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高α盘的恒星形成历史是连续还是间歇性仍不明确，需借助大样本高精度测光与动力学数据加以厘清。

Method: 采用前向建模方法，利用Gaia DR3中约145,000颗满足特定金属丰度、径向距离和垂直高度范围的次巨星样本，同时考虑个体年龄与金属丰度误差及选择效应进行校正。

Result: 重建出高α盘年龄-金属丰度分布包含两个主成分：主成分峰值在~10.6 Gyr、[Fe/H]≈−0.38；更老成分峰值在~12.4 Gyr、[Fe/H]≈−0.79；还发现主成分或可进一步分为~11.1 Gyr和~10.0 Gyr两个子成分。

Conclusion: 高α盘的形成并非单一平滑过程，而是由多次增强的恒星形成事件构成，与含重复气体吸积的宇宙学模拟定性一致，且最老成分可能与~12.3 Gyr的Kraken/Heracles并合事件有关。

Abstract: Whether the high-$α$ disk experienced a continuous or episodic star-formation history remains an open question. Here we investigate the age--metallicity distribution of the high-$α$ disk using approximately 145,000 Gaia DR3 subgiants with $[\mathrm{Fe/H}]>-1$, 6.5~kpc $\lesssim R\lesssim9.5$~kpc, and $|Z|\lesssim1.5$~kpc. We adopt a forward-modelling framework that accounts for individual age and metallicity uncertainties and corrects for sample selection effects. The reconstructed distribution reveals two components: a broad, dominant population peaking at $\sim10.6$ Gyr and $[\mathrm{Fe/H}]\simeq-0.38$, and an older, more metal-poor population peaking at $\sim12.4$ Gyr and $[\mathrm{Fe/H}]\simeq-0.79$. These features suggest an episodic history of star formation and chemical enrichment in the high-$α$ disk, qualitatively consistent with cosmological simulations in which recurrent gas accretion drives variations in the star formation rate. The age of the older component is close to the $\sim12.3$~Gyr epoch inferred for the proposed Low-energy--Kraken--Heracles accretion event, suggesting a possible connection between this early interaction and enhanced disk star formation. There is tentative evidence that the dominant population is possibly composed of two sub-components with age distributions peaking at $\sim11.1$ Gyr and $\sim10.0$ Gyr, respectively. Together, these results favour a picture in which the high-$α$ disk was built through recurrent episodes of enhanced star formation rather than a single temporally smooth phase, even for the regime of ${\rm [Fe/H]}>-1$.

</details>


### [110] [A 13 Year Black Hole Microlensing Survey in Omega Centauri](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30673)
*Zack Freeman,Maximilian Häberle,Anil Seth,John Zaris,Matthew Whitaker,Carl L. Rodriguez,Elena González Prieto,Andrea Bellini,Callie Clontz,Sebastian Kamann,Mattia Libralato,Nadine Neumayer,Jay Strader,Doğa Veske*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用哈勃太空望远镜13年观测数据，通过微引力透镜效应在球状星团半人马座ω中探测恒星级黑洞的可能性，但未发现确凿的黑洞透镜事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接探测球状星团中的恒星级黑洞极为困难，仅发现少数处于双星系统中的黑洞；而微引力透镜提供了一种独立、无偏的探测手段，尤其适用于孤立黑洞。

Method: 基于哈勃望远镜近200幅单波段图像，构建新型代价函数拟合预期约1年长的微透镜信号，并用Omega Centauri的蒙特卡洛模拟生成合成事件验证完备性；同时训练神经网络以高效识别真实透镜事件。

Result: 在全部观测数据中未发现可信的黑洞微透镜候选体；根据完备性分析和模拟预测，实际未观测到事件与早期理论预期不符。

Conclusion: 当前数据不支持在Omega Centauri中存在大量可探测的孤立恒星级黑洞；该结果对黑洞保留率及致密天体演化模型提出约束；所用光度数据已公开供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Star clusters are born with massive stars that leave behind a large population of stellar mass black holes. These black holes may account for a large fraction of binary black hole mergers seen in gravitational waves as the dense environment enables retention of black holes and the formation of binaries. However, direct measurements of stellar mass black holes in globular clusters are limited, with just a few found in binary systems thus far. In this paper, we examine the possibility of detecting black holes through microlensing in the Milky Way's most massive star cluster, Omega Centauri. We take advantage of a unique dataset of nearly 200 single-band images taken over 13 years with the Hubble Space Telescope to look for the $\sim$1 year long events expected from microlensing of cluster stars by stellar mass black holes. We develop a novel cost function to fit the lensing signatures that we verify using synthetic events, quantifying our completeness. These synthetic events are based on a recent Monte Carlo simulation of Omega Centauri. We also use these synthetic events to train a neural network to most efficiently distinguish observed lensing events. Application of this algorithm to our observed data, and subsequent visual follow-up, show no convincing candidate black hole lensing events. Based on analysis of the simulation data and our completeness, we find that no events would be expected in the data contrary to the expectations from earlier work. We release the photometric dataset used in this work for future use.

</details>


### [111] [A recurring changing state AGN discovered in the SAMI Galaxy Survey and SDSS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30816)
*Scott M. Croom,Rebecca McElroy,Dougal Dobie,Thomas Dunn,Yasmeen Janschek,Tyler Klinger,Shaun Parasher,Mitchell Spanner,Oliver J. Thorp*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文详细分析了红移z=0.0582的星系J085337.26+014303.6中的活动星系核（AGN），发现其经历了至少一次完整的“变脸”AGN周期（类型1.9→1→1.9），且变化源于物理状态改变而非遮蔽变化；光学波段AGN贡献弱（~10%），而WISE W2波段AGN亮度可与宿主星系相当；宽巴尔末线存在多成分结构，暗示宽线区存在空间上分离的两个区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Changing-look AGN（CLAGN）的物理机制，尤其是区分光谱类型变化是由吸积率变化还是尘埃遮蔽变化引起。

Method: 结合SDSS和SAMI巡天数据进行时域光谱分析，辅以WISE红外测光数据，开展详细光谱建模与宽线成分分解。

Result: 确认该AGN在2001–2022年间完成至少一次完整CLAGN循环（1.9→1→1.9），WISE光变曲线暗示2020年可能短暂回归1.9型；光学AGN贡献仅约10%，而W2波段AGN光度可达宿主星系总光度；宽Hα线显示双成分：低态下存在极宽成分（FWHM=5000–6000 km/s），高态下主导成分为较窄宽线（FWHM~2000 km/s）。

Conclusion: 该CLAGN的变化由中心引擎物理状态（如吸积率）变化驱动，而非视线方向上的遮蔽变化；宽线多成分结构支持宽线区存在空间分离、动力学不同的子区域。

Abstract: We present a detailed analysis of the AGN in galaxy J085337.26+014303.6 at z=0.0582. A combination of SDSS and SAMI Galaxy Survey data shows that this object has gone through at least one full changing-look AGN cycle, from type 1.9 in 2001 to type 1 in 2015 and 2021, and then type 1.9 again in 2022. While the broad lines in this object show evidence of dust obscuration, detailed spectral analysis plus WISE photometry demonstrate that the changes in this object are related to a physical state change, not changing obscuration. In fact, the WISE light curve suggests that this object may have briefly returned to a type 1.9 state in 2020. In the optical, the host galaxy dominates the light (e.g. only ~10 per cent contribution from the AGN at 4000 Angstoms within a central aperture), even in the high state. In contrast, the brightest WISE W2-band measurements place the AGN at a similar luminosity to the entire host galaxy. The broad Balmer lines show evidence of multiple components, with a weak very-broad component (FWHM=5000-6000 km/s) visible in the low type 1.9 state, and a stronger broad component of FWHM~2000 km/s dominating in the high type 1 state. We propose that this is caused by two spatially distinct regions within the broad line region.

</details>


### [112] [Discovering supernovae in the Virgo Cluster during the LSST era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30840)
*Jozsef Vinko,Eniko Regos,Krisztian Sarneczky*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文预测了在LSST巡天的10年运行期内，在室女座星系团中发现极年轻超新星的数量，重点关注不同子结构和星系类型，并将通过Shadow-LSST项目进行观测验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大视场、高深度、高频率巡天时代的到来，有望在爆炸后数小时内发现邻近富星系团中的新型爆发暂现源，亟需对探测概率和数量进行理论预估。

Method: 基于室女座星系团（距离约16.5 Mpc）的结构与星系分布特性，结合LSST巡天参数，建模并估算其不同子结构和星系类型中极年轻超新星在10年内的预期发现数量。

Result: 给出了室女座星系团各子结构及星系类型中未来10年内LSST可能探测到的极年轻超新星数目预测。

Conclusion: 该理论预测为即将开展的Shadow-LSST观测项目提供了关键科学依据和目标指引，有助于优化后续观测策略与资源分配。

Abstract: In the era of unprecedentedly wide, deep and high-cadence sky surveys the chance for discovering new explosive transients in nearby, rich galaxy clusters within hours after explosion is expected to increase dramatically. In this paper we focus on the Virgo cluster ($D \simeq 16.5$ Mpc), and estimate the expected numbers of new, very young supernovae in different sub-structures and galaxy types of the cluster during the proposed lifetime (~10 years) of the Legacy Survey of Space and Time (LSST) by the Vera Rubin Observatory. These predictions will be tested observationally during the ongoing Shadow-LSST discovery and follow-up program led by University of Arizona in the upcoming years.

</details>


### [113] [A neural-network model to find correlations in astrochemical space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30842)
*Daniel Giovanni Vargas,Darío Barreiro-Lage,Carlos Cabezas,José Cernicharo,Germán Molpeceres*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自然语言处理和神经网络的机器学习方法，将分子结构（化学字符串）与金牛座分子云TMC-1中的柱密度关联起来，生成具有天文化学意义的嵌入表示，用于预测已知及未知分子的柱密度，并据此筛选出若干潜在可探测新分子，揭示近年TMC-1分子发现对星际化学认知具有变革性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测新的星际分子是天体化学的核心任务，近年来探测到的分子数量与多样性显著增长，亟需数据驱动方法系统理解分子结构与其在星际介质中丰度（柱密度）之间的关系，并指导未来观测目标选择。

Method: 构建基于化学字符串（如SMILES）的NLP模型，生成天体化学信息丰富的分子嵌入；将其输入神经网络进行柱密度回归建模；在原始TMC-1分子数据集及分布外分子上训练与验证；利用学习到的隐空间向量开展分子结构置换与可探测性分析；并按历史探测时间分段训练以评估认知演进。

Result: 模型在训练+验证集/验证集上达到MSE=0.17/0.58、MAE=0.29/0.59、R²=0.88/0.51；隐空间向量能捕捉化学关系与可探测性；通过结构置换识别出多个TMC-1中有望被探测的新候选分子及大量非极性分子；历史分段训练表明近年探测显著重塑了星际化学图景。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动框架不仅提升了柱密度预测能力（含分布外泛化），更提供了新型‘数据驱动的天体化学直觉’，推动从经验式发现迈向可解释、可预测的星际分子科学。

Abstract: The detection of new interstellar molecules is central to astrochemistry, and arguably the most transformative development in the field in past decade has been the increase in both number and diversity of detected species. Using natural language processing models, we develop a machine-learning pipeline, similar to other approaches reported in the literature, to connect molecular structures, encoded as chemical strings, and molecular column densities in TMC-1. The workflow generates astrochemically informed embeddings that can be employed in subsequent supervised learning tasks. These representations are used within a neural-network framework to perform column-density regression on both the original molecular inventory and out-of-distribution species. The resulting model achieves mean squared error values of 0.17/0.58 and mean absolute error values of 0.29/0.59, and coefficients of determination of 0.88/0.51 for the train+validation and validation datasets. The latent vectors learned by the model are rich mathematical objects that capture the underlying chemical relations and also the potential interstellar detectability of the molecule. Using these vectors in molecules obtained from systematic permutations of the TMC-1 inventory, we identify several promising candidates for future detection in this cloud, together with a large number of apolar species. By training the model on datasets reflecting different historical epochs of molecular detections in TMC-1, we show that recent molecular detections have substantially reshaped our understanding of interstellar chemistry, in a transformative, rather than incremental, way. Beyond accurate prediction of column-density trends for both in-distribution and out-of-distribution molecules, the latent-space representations produced by the model provide a stepping stone for developing new forms of data-driven astrochemical intuition and exploration.

</details>


### [114] [Stability of rotation axis of galactic disks in HESTIA suite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30922)
*Naira R. Arakelyan,Sergey V. Pilipenko,Stefan Gottlöber,Noam I. Libeskind,Gustavo Yepes,Yehuda Hoffman*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过HESTIA模拟发现，约1/4的银河系或M31类似星系在近60亿年内气体盘自转轴方向发生显著偏转（>45°），多数由卫星星系吸积引起；这些星系60亿年前角动量幅值普遍偏低，支持本星系群近60亿年演化相对宁静的观点，并解释了星系自转轴与大尺度结构取向难以建立关联的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释星系旋转轴与大尺度结构取向之间缺乏明显相关性的原因，并检验本星系群近期演化是否平静。

Method: 基于HESTIA数值模拟数据，统计分析银河系和M31类似星系中气体盘旋转轴方向在近6 Gyr内的变化，并追溯其成因（如卫星吸积）及早期角动量特征。

Result: 约1/4的类似星系出现>45°的旋转轴偏转；其中6/7例由单个卫星吸积导致；所有发生偏转的星系在6 Gyr前角动量幅值均低于样本中位数。

Conclusion: 卫星吸积可显著改变星系气体盘取向，这既支持本星系群近6 Gyr演化较宁静，也解释了旋转轴与大尺度结构方向难相关联的现象。

Abstract: We have found that in approximately 1/4 of Milky Way or M31 analogues in the HESTIA suite of simulations there is a significant, $>45^\circ$, change in the axis of rotation orientation of the gaseous disk during the last 6 Gyr. In 6 out of 7 such cases the change is likely caused by the accretion of individual satellites, while in one case such a connection is not obvious. All galaxies that underwent a significant change in rotation axis direction also had angular momentum amplitudes below the sample median 6 Gyr ago. Our findings support the idea that last 6~Gyr was a quiet phase of evolution in the LG. On the other hand, the mechanism we detect, that satellites can flip galaxy orientation, can explain the difficulties in identifying correlations between the rotation axis and the orientation of large-scale structure elements.

</details>


### [115] [EP J062132.0+623837: A Multi-wavelength Study of a Changing-look Active Galactic Nucleus Discovered by Einstein Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30959)
*Lin-Na Xu,Ning Jiang,Tinggui Wang,Shifeng Huang,Yibo Wang,Zhenfeng Sheng,Mingxin Wu,Jiazheng Zhu,Huaqing Cheng,Chichuan Jin,Jun-Jie Jin,Dongyue Li,Yuan Liu,Jianwei Lyu,Jian-Guo Wang,Yun-Fei Xu,Yongquan Xue,Zhen Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文报告了利用爱因斯坦探针（EP）卫星发现并确认的一个变脸活动星系核（AGN）EP J062132.0+623837，该源表现出显著的多波段光变和光谱型由1.8型向1.5型转变的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用宽视场时域X射线巡天（如EP）系统性发现和研究变脸AGN，弥补光学巡天的局限，探索AGN中心引擎物理机制及吸积过程的快速变化。

Method: 结合EP/WXT与FXT、Swift/XRT的X射线观测，以及光学光谱与中红外光变曲线的多波段数据分析，开展定位、光度演化、能谱拟合与光谱分类比对。

Result: 确认EP J062132.0+623837为z=0.084的变脸AGN：X射线持续活跃（L_X≈10^43–10^44 erg/s，Γ≈1.5），光学光谱显示宽巴尔末线增强，光谱型由Type 1.8变为Type 1.5，并存在约2010年起的剧烈增亮与后续强变异性。

Conclusion: EP首次探测到的该变脸AGN案例，证实了宽视场X射线时域巡天在发现高振幅、快速变脸AGN方面的独特能力，为系统搜寻此类天体开辟了新途径，可与光学巡天形成有力互补。

Abstract: EP J062132.0+623837 is a new X-ray source identified in 14-day binned survey data from the Wide-field X-ray Telescope aboard the Einstein Probe (EP). Subsequent X-ray observations with the Follow-up X-ray Telescope (FXT) on board EP localized the source to the central region of the galaxy 2MASX J06213207+6238390 at $z=0.084$. Here, we present a comprehensive analysis of the multi-wavelength data for this object. During the FXT and Swift/XRT follow-up, the source remained X-ray active, with observed luminosities of $(3-9)\times10^{43}$ erg s$^{-1}$, and its X-ray spectra were well described by power-law models with photon indices of $Γ\sim1.5$. The optical spectrum of the host galaxy clearly reveals a broad-line active galactic nucleus (AGN) exhibiting significantly enhanced broad Balmer emission lines compared to an archival spectrum obtained four years earlier, thereby marking a changing-look transition from Type 1.8 to Type 1.5. Furthermore, long-term optical and mid-infrared light curves indicate that the nucleus underwent dramatic brightening around 2010 and has since shown substantial variability. Given the spatial coincidence and multi-wavelength behavior, we identify EP J062132.0+623837 as a changing-look AGN, initially detected by EP and subsequently confirmed to exhibit large-amplitude variability across multiple bands. This case study demonstrates the great potential of wide-field time-domain X-ray surveys for discovering changing-look AGNs, paving a new avenue for their systematic identification as a powerful complement to ongoing optical surveys.

</details>


### [116] [Self-Supervised Representation Learning for JWST Galaxy Images and Low Resolution Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31078)
*Yang Cheng,Morgan Fouesneau,Ivelina G. Momcheva,Tobias Buck,Shengxiu Sun*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自监督多模态模型，利用稀疏的JWST单波段图像（F150W）与近红外光谱（PRISM）联合学习，高精度估计红移1–3星系的恒星质量，性能优于传统SED拟合法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SED拟合法需要多波段覆盖数据，而JWST观测常受限于带宽和时间，导致数据稀疏；本文旨在利用自监督多模态学习，从有限单波段图像与光谱中提取足够信息以准确估计恒星质量。

Method: 构建自监督多模态模型，联合训练单波段F150W图像与观测帧1–2 μm PRISM光谱；采用跨模态CLIP对齐策略增强单模态表征能力，并学习联合图像-光谱嵌入用于恒星质量回归。

Result: 在红移1 < z ≤ 3样本上实现R² ≈ 0.9、σ_NMAD = 0.24 dex的恒星质量估计；跨模态CLIP对齐使图像单模态R²提升+0.04、光谱单模态R²提升+0.09，缩小了单模态与联合模态性能差距。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了在数据稀疏条件下，自监督多模态建模可有效替代传统多波段依赖方法，为未来JWST等望远镜的快速星系物理量估计提供了新范式。

Abstract: A self-supervised multimodal model recovers galaxy stellar masses from sparse JWST data that lack the multi-band coverage required by traditional SED fitting. We train on single-band F150W imaging paired with observed-frame 1-2 $μ$m PRISM spectra and obtain a joint image-spectrum representation that recovers stellar mass at $1<z\le3$ with $R^2 \sim 0.9$ and $σ_{\mathrm{NMAD}} = 0.24$ dex; cross-modal CLIP alignment further raises single-modality performance by $ΔR^2 = +0.04$ (imaging) and $+0.09$ (spectroscopy), narrowing the gap to joint-modality inference. The code and documentation are available at \href{https://github.com/2003cy/neurips2026_ssl_jwst}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [117] [A Catalog of Spectral Properties for DESI DR1 Quasars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31416)
*Qiaoya Wu,Yue Shen*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DESI DR1中171万多个类星体的光谱特性增值目录，包括连续谱分解、发射线测量、黑洞质量估计、系统红移校正及光学冕线拟合，并揭示了发射线比与爱丁顿比的系统性关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提供一个社区资源，支持类星体人口统计、黑洞吸积、发射线物理及宽线区、冕线区和窄线区关联等研究。

Method: 对DESI DR1中1717774个唯一类星体（1774723条光谱）进行统一光谱分解，涵盖连续谱、宿主星系（z<1）、FeII发射，并测量Lyα到Hα的主要宽窄发射线；同时估算单历元黑洞质量、光度及爱丁顿比；校正系统红移；局部拟合多个光学冕线。

Result: 提供了包含光谱分解、发射线参数、黑洞质量、系统红移（与DESI管线红移在88.6%样本中偏差<500 km/s）、以及多个光学冕线（如NeV、FeVII、FeXI）拟合结果的增值目录；发现若干发射线比（含冕线）随爱丁顿比系统变化。

Conclusion: 该目录是面向天文学界的重要数据资源，有助于深化对类星体物理、黑洞增长及气体结构的理解。

Abstract: The Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) is carrying out a five-year spectroscopic survey designed to map the large-scale structure of the Universe and obtain spectra for millions of galaxies and quasars over a wide redshift range. We present a value-added catalog of spectral properties for 1,717,774 unique quasars (1,774,723 spectra) in the DESI Data Release 1. We perform a uniform spectral decomposition of the quasar continuum, host galaxy emission (at $z<1$), and \FeII\ emission, and measure the line properties of major broad and narrow emission lines from Ly$α$ to H$α$ covered by DESI spectra. We also provide single-epoch black hole mass estimates (from H$β$, \MgII, and \CIV), bolometric luminosities, and Eddington ratios for these quasars. We provide systemic redshifts that account for the intrinsic velocity shifts of individual lines; they agree with the DESI pipeline redshifts to within 500~km s$^{-1}$ for 88.6\% of the sample, and are most useful at $z>1$ where the pipeline redshift is heavily constrained by blueshifted high-ionization lines. We also provide local fits to several optical coronal lines, including \NeV~$\lambda3426$, [\ion{Fe}{7}]~$\lambda5722$, [\ion{Fe}{7}]~$\lambda6088$, and [\ion{Fe}{11}]~$\lambda7893$. We show that several emission-line ratios, including those involving coronal lines, vary systematically with Eddington ratio. The catalog is intended as a community resource for studies of quasar demographics, black hole accretion, emission-line physics, and the connection between the broad-, coronal-, and narrow-line regions.

</details>


### [118] [SpecLess: A Multi Survey Python Package for Machine Learning Classification of Galaxy Morphology and Activity from Photometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31545)
*Uzay Aydin,Mehmet Tanriver*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了SpecLess开源Python包，用于在不同天文巡天数据上进行光度星系分类（形态与活动性），通过XGBoost模型实现高F1分数，并量化了跨巡天迁移时的性能下降，揭示了光学与中红外波段光度特征的互补作用及模型对巡天系统差异的方向依赖敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决光度星系分类模型在不同巡天数据间泛化能力差的问题，即在某一巡天训练的模型难以直接迁移到另一巡天而保持高性能。

Method: 构建基于SDSS+AllWISE、KiDS+VIKING和DESI Legacy Imaging Surveys光度数据的XGBoost分类器，完成两类任务：Galaxy Zoo 1形态分类（椭圆/旋涡）和SDSS光谱活动类型分类（starforming/starburst/Seyfert/LINER）；采用匹配样本评估跨巡天迁移性能，并开展特征消融、颜色重拟合、波段替换等分析。

Result: 各巡天内模型取得高宏F1：形态分类达0.9332（SDSS）和0.9237（KiDS），活动分类达0.7637（SDSS）和0.7398（DESI）；跨巡天迁移存在显著性能损失（如SDSS→DESI活动分类F1下降0.0660）；光学与中红外波段特征具有互补性；颜色重拟合等方法无法一致恢复目标巡天性能。

Conclusion: SpecLess为多巡天光度星系分类提供了可复现基线与开源框架，证实跨巡天迁移性能受系统差异影响且具方向性，强调需发展更鲁棒的跨域建模方法。

Abstract: Broad band photometry enables scalable galaxy classification without spectroscopy at inference time, yet strong within survey performance may not transfer when the same galaxies are measured by another survey. We present SpecLess, an open source Python package implementing two photometric classification tasks: high confidence Galaxy Zoo 1 elliptical and spiral morphologies, and four SDSS spectroscopic activity reference classes (starforming, starburst, Seyfert, and LINER). Using SDSS+AllWISE, KiDS+VIKING, and DESI Legacy Imaging Surveys photometry, survey specific XGBoost classifiers achieve held out macro F1 scores of 0.9332 (SDSS; 35,554 galaxies) and 0.9237 (KiDS; 3,269) for morphology, and 0.7637 (SDSS; 16,931) and 0.7398 (DESI; 11,975) for activity. Starforming and starburst references are recovered more effectively than Seyfert and LINER, while feature ablations reveal complementary contributions from optical and mid infrared photometry.
  We quantify catalogue dependence using separately trained common feature models evaluated on matched galaxies with identical labels and data partitions. Relative to target trained models, macro F1 deficits are 0.0153 (SDSS to KiDS) and 0.0354 (KiDS to SDSS) for morphology (12,301 galaxies; 20 splits), and 0.0660 (SDSS to DESI) and 0.0406 (DESI to SDSS) for activity (11,975 test galaxies). Colour only refits, additive corrections, and optical infrared substitutions reveal direction dependent sensitivity without consistently recovering target trained performance.
  SpecLess v0.3.0 distributes four pretrained, survey specific classifiers through a unified Python interface. The package provides a reproducible baseline for photometric galaxy classification across multiple surveys while establishing an extensible, open source foundation for future developments in classification methods and photometric catalogue support.

</details>


### [119] [Odd radio circle J1555+2726: Observational evidence for shock re-acceleration of fossil plasma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31664)
*L. Bruno,B. Koribalski,R. P. Norris,A. Ivleva,T. Venturi,A. Botteon,C. J. Riseley,K. Dolag,J. R. Callingham,T. W. Shimwell*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过LOFAR、uGMRT和MeerKAT射电数据，分析了奇异地外射电源ORC4的形态与谱性质，发现其谱指数随半径变平、存在反曲率及异常色-色图特征，表明其电子成分复杂，可能源于激波对化石相对论电子的再加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ORCs（奇特射电圈）的形成机制尚不明确，激波可能起关键作用；本文旨在通过高分辨、宽频段射电观测，揭示ORC4的物理本质。

Method: 利用LOFAR、uGMRT和MeerKAT在50–1284 MHz频段的数据，构建高分辨率、高灵敏度的射电图像与谱指数图，并测量积分谱、射电光度及径向谱变化。

Result: 观测显示环与填充发射的亮度对比随频率降低而减弱；测得积分谱指数α=1.06±0.03，150 MHz光度为(5.4±0.5)×10²⁵ W Hz⁻¹，环处谱变平至α∼0.5；但发现负曲率与异常色-色图，排除热粒子激波加速与标准老化模型。

Conclusion: ORC4中存在多种能谱电子共存，最可能是激波对化石相对论电子的再加速所致：环由高能再加速电子主导，低能再加速电子则更均匀分布于整个椭球状体积内。

Abstract: Context. Odd radio circles (ORCs) are peculiar extragalactic sources recently discovered. They are characterised by one or more bright rings extending on scales of hundreds of kiloparsecs, and are filled with fainter emission. A massive elliptical galaxy lies at the centre of ORCs. While their formation mechanism is debated, a shock is plausibly involved. Aims. We aim to constrain the nature of ORC J1555+2726 (ORC4) through the analysis of its morphological and spectral properties. Methods. We utilised Low Frequency ARray (LOFAR), upgraded Giant Metrewave Radio Telescope (uGMRT), and MeerKAT radio data at 50-1284 MHz to obtain images and spectral index maps at high resolution and sensitivity. Results. Both the resolution and observing frequency affect the observed morphology. In particular, the brightness contrast between the ring and the filling emission becomes progressively smoother from higher to lower frequencies. We measured an integrated spectrum of slope $α=1.06\pm 0.03$, a radio power of $P_{\rm 150}= (5.4\pm 0.5)\times 10^{25} \; {\rm W \; Hz^{-1}}$ at 150 MHz, and a radial spectral flattening down to $α\sim0.5$ at the ring location. However, we inferred a negative (convex) spectral curvature and a peculiar spectral shape in radio colour-colour diagrams, which imply spectral flattening towards high frequencies. These properties cannot be explained by shock acceleration of thermal particles and standard ageing. Conclusions. The observed features suggest that a mixture of electrons with different spectra coexist within ORC4 volume, likely associated with shock re-acceleration of a fossil relativistic reservoir. The ring emission is plausibly dominated by higher-energy electrons re-accelerated at the shock front, while lower-energy re-accelerated electrons are more uniformly distributed across the whole source (possibly ellipsoidal) volume.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.CO'></div>

# astro-ph.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [2Flow2SN: Dusty Supernova Cosmology with Photometric Redshifts and Contamination using Neural Simulation-Based Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30244)
*Benjamin M. Boyd,Maximilian Autenrieth,Kaisey S. Mandel,Matthew Grayling,Madeleine Ginolin,Lisa Kelsey,Stephen Thorp*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: FlowSN2是一个结合分层贝叶斯建模与基于仿真的推断（SBI）的模块化统计框架，用于LSST时代的Ia型超新星光度测距，能同时处理选择效应、光度红移误差和非Ia污染，并通过归一化流实现似然估计的可重用性与宇宙学模型无关的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LSST时代Ia型超新星光度测距需在大规模数据下同时处理选择效应、光度红移不确定性及非Ia型污染，现有方法难以兼顾统计严谨性与计算可扩展性。

Method: 提出FlowSN2框架：融合分层贝叶斯建模与仿真推断（SBI），使用归一化流进行神经似然估计；将问题重构为对光变曲线摘要与光度红移的联合推断；引入标准模型解耦本征超新星变化与宿主星系尘埃效应；采用贝叶斯重采样传播分类不确定性，无需显式污染似然。

Result: 在五组不同宇宙学参数的模拟测试中，准确恢复真实宇宙学与种群参数，后验分布与解析解及纯Ia基线高度一致；支持跨宇宙学模型（如w0waCDM）的免再训练推理；支持零额外成本的贝叶斯模型比较；可扩展至十余万超新星。

Conclusion: FlowSN2为宽视场光度巡天提供了可解释、统计严谨且可扩展的宇宙学推断解决方案，有助于解决超新星标准化与暗能量演化等关键争议。

Abstract: Photometric Type Ia supernova (SN Ia) cosmology in the era of the Vera C. Rubin Observatory's Legacy Survey of Space and Time (LSST) demands statistical frameworks that simultaneously account for selection effects, photometric redshift uncertainties and non-Ia contamination at scale. We present FlowSN2, a modular statistical framework combining hierarchical Bayesian modelling and simulation-based inference (SBI), that reframes photometric SN cosmology from a regression task to a joint inference problem over light curve summaries and photometric redshifts. By employing neural likelihood estimation via normalising flows, FlowSN2 approximates the data distributions of selected objects in a form that can be reused across different cosmological models, achieving amortisation over the Hubble diagram. This amortisation enables inference across alternative cosmological parameterisations, such as $w_0w_a\mathrm{CDM}$, away from the fiducial training cosmology. The framework integrates a standardisation model that disentangles intrinsic SN variations from extrinsic host-galaxy dust effects and adopts a principled Bayesian resampling approach to propagate SN classification uncertainty, without requiring an explicit contaminant likelihood. Across simulated tests spanning five cosmological parameter sets, our framework recovers true cosmological and population parameters, with posteriors in close agreement with both tractable analytical solutions and pure-Ia baselines. Conveniently, the framework enables Bayesian model comparison at negligible additional computational cost, providing a valuable tool for addressing debates surrounding SN standardisation and evolving dark energy. Capable of scaling to over a hundred thousand SNe without retraining, FlowSN2 offers an interpretable and statistically principled solution for cosmological inference with upcoming wide-field photometric surveys.

</details>


### [121] [Density-split analyses of the pairwise kinetic Sunyaev-Zeldovich effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30267)
*Finn A. Roper,Aritra K. Gon,Yan-Chuan Cai,John A. Peacock*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于局部密度分割的成对kSZ（kinetic Sunyaev-Zeldovich）估计器，以分离收敛与发散流、降低速度分布方差，从而提升对σ₈和Ωₘ等宇宙学参数的约束能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准成对kSZ估计器因欠密区外流与过密区吸积的近似抵消导致信号方差大、信噪比低，尤其在大尺度上更显著；需改进以提升宇宙学参数约束精度。

Method: 将星系按其局部超密度分组，分离收敛与发散运动对应的kSZ信号，并利用DESI类星系巡天模拟与Simons Observatory CMB噪声模型进行信息量分析。

Result: 密度分割后的成对kSZ探测显著性仅小幅提升，但联合DESI与Simons Observatory LAT数据时，对σ₈和Ωₘ的约束精度分别提高11倍和4倍。

Conclusion: 密度分割方法虽未大幅提高kSZ探测显著性，却能显著增强关键宇宙学参数（尤其是σ₈）的约束能力，为未来多信使宇宙学提供新途径。

Abstract: We consider the dependence of the kinetic Sunyaev-Zeldovich (kSZ) effect on the environments of foreground cosmological structures. Standard pairwise kSZ estimators exploit the fact that galaxies below separations of $\sim 200\,h^{-1}{\rm M}_\odot$ fall towards each other on average, yielding a negative mean pairwise kSZ signal. However, this signal is the residual from a near-cancellation between outflows from underdense regions and infall towards overdensities giving a large variance in the pairwise velocity distribution and hence a low signal-to-noise ratio for the mean kSZ signal. This effect is especially marked at the largest scales, where the pairwise velocity distribution becomes more symmetric about zero. We show that splitting galaxies according to their local overdensity allows the convergence and divergence flows to be separated, yielding pairwise kSZ signals of opposing sign, with a reduction in variance for each subset. We use simulations to perform a detailed analysis of this phenomenon, comparing the cosmological information content in the global pairwise kSZ signal with our new density-split pairwise kSZ estimator. We consider galaxy catalogues comparable to datasets from the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument, together with cosmic microwave background instrumental noise characteristic of recent and planned ground-based experiments. We predict that the density-split pairwise kSZ will increase the significance of detection by only a modest factor; nevertheless, we expect improvements by factors of respectively 11 and 4 in constraining $σ_8$ and $Ω_{\rm m}$ when using the combination of Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument and Simons Observatory Large Aperture Telescope surveys.

</details>


### [122] [Optimal cosmological cross-correlation analysis with U-Net-based processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30294)
*Weitong Yan,Daniel Grün,Oliver Friedrich,András Kovács*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用U-Net学习构建优化相关性的示踪场，以超越传统两点统计（如功率谱）在宇宙学交叉相关分析中的局限性，尤其针对非高斯场（如对数正态场）展现出更优的相关性提取能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统宇宙学交叉相关分析依赖于两点统计，但其在非高斯场中无法捕获全部信息；而晚期宇宙场普遍存在非高斯性，因此需探索更高阶统计方法。

Method: 使用1D U-Net对配对模拟场（高斯与对数正态场）进行训练，以示踪场为输入、目标场为损失函数的一部分，学习最大化Pearson相关系数的变换；并与高斯极限下的解析线性解对比。

Result: 在高斯场中U-Net逼近线性解（有效匹配滤波器）；在对数正态场中，U-Net将平均Pearson相关系数从线性估计的0.857提升至0.953，表明其利用了功率谱之外的信息。

Conclusion: U-Net可有效提取超越功率谱的相关信息，为增强宇宙学交叉相关分析提供了有前景的新框架。

Abstract: Cosmological cross-correlation analyses are commonly based on two-point statistics, which are optimal for Gaussian random fields but do not capture all relevant information in the presence of non-Gaussianity, which is ubiquitous in late-time cosmic fields. This motivates investigations of higher order statistics, and one method is to use machine learning to construct a correlation-optimized tracer field that can utilize cross-correlation information beyond two-point statistics. Using a tracer field as input and a paired target field as part of the cost function, we test whether a U-Net can learn a transformed tracer field that maximally correlates with the target field. We train a 1D U-Net on paired simulated fields and study controlled cases based on Gaussian and log-normal random fields. We train and quantify performance using the Pearson correlation coefficient with the target field and compare the network output with the analytic linear solution known in the Gaussian limit. For Gaussian fields, the U-Net reproduces the expected linear solution closely and behaves as an effective matched filter in frequency space. For log-normal fields, the network departs from the linear estimator and achieves an improved correlation with the target field, increasing the mean Pearson correlation from 0.857 for the linear estimator to 0.953 for the U-Net output. This indicates that the network is sensitive to information beyond the power spectrum. These controlled 1D experiments show that U-Net-based tracer transformation can effectively extract correlation-relevant information beyond the power spectrum, providing a promising framework for enhancing cosmological cross-correlation analyses.

</details>


### [123] [ULTRAMAN: A Proposed ultrasound detector for macroscopic dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30507)
*Oem Trivedi,Robert J. Scherrer,Alfredo Gurrola*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: ULTRAMAN是一种新型被动超声阵列实验，利用宏观暗物质（macros）穿过介质时产生的相干声学扰动来探测其存在，通过多通道超声信号重建超音速轨迹，从而区分不同类型的宏观暗物质候选体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有暗物质探测器主要依赖闪烁、电离等能量沉积信号，难以有效探测宏观暗物质；ULTRAMAN旨在通过声学响应提供一种新途径，弥补当前探测手段在宏观质量尺度上的空白。

Method: 设计并构建基于同步多单元超声阵列的被动超声实验系统（ULTRAMAN），建模宏观粒子在介质中的停止本领与相互作用截面到声压的映射关系，分析多通道声学信号的时间-空间相关性以识别单次超音速穿越事件。

Result: 证明了典型宏观暗物质（如核子团、轴子夸克团、原初黑洞等）在声学可观测量空间中占据可区分区域；现有超声技术已具备开展地面事件级搜索的可行性。

Conclusion: ULTRAMAN为宏观暗物质提供了新颖、可行且互补的探测范式；零结果将对宏观暗物质的质量与丰度参数空间施加新的约束。

Abstract: We propose ULTRAMAN (ULTRAsound Macro ANalyzer), a passive ultrasound array experiment designed to search for macroscopic dark matter (macros) through coherent acoustic disturbances generated as compact objects traverse a controlled target medium. Unlike existing dark matter detectors that primarily rely on scintillation, ionization, or other signatures of energy deposition, ULTRAMAN exploits the acoustic response of matter to reconstruct individual highly supersonic trajectories using synchronized multielement ultrasound arrays. The framework connects macro stopping power and interaction cross section to the resulting acoustic pressure, while the expected event rate probes the macro mass and abundance. A genuine candidate would appear not as an isolated radio frequency spike, but as a correlated multichannel track consistent with a single highly supersonic passage through the target. Representative macro, nuclearite, axion quark nugget, and primordial black hole benchmarks are shown to occupy distinct regions of the resulting acoustic observable space. Existing ultrasound technology offers a realistic route to an event-level terrestrial search for macroscopic dark matter, with null observations translating into complementary constraints on its parameter space.

</details>


### [124] [Same Events, Weaker Bound: Model Dependence in Magellanic Microlensing Limits on Primordial Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30510)
*David Morales-Zapien,Lucas Brown,Stefano Profumo*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文重新评估了OGLE对麦哲伦云的微引力透镜观测对原初黑洞（PBHs）作为暗物质所设限的可靠性，指出在考虑银河系晕、恒星透镜种群及大麦哲伦云晕等最新观测约束后，原有20年严格限制可能被削弱达6倍甚至一个数量级以上；同时，若考虑原初密度涨落，行星质量尺度的限制也会减弱3.4–6倍。作者强调应明确报告暗物质限制所依赖的模型假设，并建议采用保守假设推导限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OGLE微引力透镜观测对PBH暗物质的限制依赖于若干天体物理模型假设，而这些假设（如银河系晕结构、透镜星族、LMC晕性质等）已因新观测数据而更新，亟需重新评估其对限制结果的影响。

Method: 在保持OGLE已探测事件数和巡天效率不变的前提下，系统性地替换并测试多个关键天体物理模型成分（银河系晕、恒星透镜分布、LMC晕），并引入原初密度涨落效应，量化各修改对PBH丰度上限的影响。

Result: 各模型更新单独可使20年严格限制削弱约6倍；组合极端情形下削弱超一个数量级；引入原初聚类后，行星质量（~10^{-8}–10^{-5} M⊙）区间限制减弱3.4–6倍。

Conclusion: 当前PBH作为暗物质的观测限制高度依赖建模假设；未来应明确定义并公开所用假设，并优先采用保守模型以确保限制的稳健性与可比性。

Abstract: OGLE microlensing of the Magellanic Clouds sets the leading bounds on primordial black holes (PBHs) as dark matter from $10^{-8}$ to $10^{3}\,M_\odot$. Holding the detected events and survey efficiencies fixed, we show that changes to the Milky Way halo, the stellar lens populations and the Large Magellanic Cloud halo, each motivated by recent data, weaken the published strict 20-year bound by up to $\simeq6$, and by over an order of magnitude in an extreme stress test. With primordial clustering, the planetary-mass bound weakens by a factor $3.4$--$6$. We strongly recommend that compact-object dark matter limits be quoted together with their modeling assumptions and, generally, be derived under conservative assumptions.

</details>


### [125] [Constraints on interacting dark energy with a constant equation of state in light of DESI DR2: The role of the interaction term](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30784)
*Zhao-Yu Li,Tian-Nuo Li,Guo-Hong Du,Sheng-Han Zhou,Yun-He Li,Jing-Fei Zhang,Xin Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了暗能量与暗物质相互作用（IDE）模型，利用CMB、BAO和Ia型超新星数据，发现Q∝ρ_de类相互作用模型（特别是Q=βH₀ρ_de）显著偏好非零相互作用和w<-1，且其有效状态方程演化类似w₀wₐCDM模型并穿越幽灵界限，贝叶斯证据显示该模型优于ΛCDM及其他IDE模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期DESI的BAO分析显示暗能量状态方程可能随时间演化并穿越w=-1的幽灵界限；而暗能量-暗物质相互作用也可能导致表观的幽灵穿越，因此需系统检验不同IDE模型对当前观测数据的解释能力。

Method: 结合Planck、ACT、SPT的CMB数据，DESI DR2的BAO测量，以及PantheonPlus、DES-Dovekie、DESY5的Ia型超新星数据，对四类典型相互作用项Q的IDE模型进行参数约束和贝叶斯模型比较。

Result: Q=βH₀ρ_de模型在CMB+DESI+DES-Dovekie联合数据下给出β=-2.52^{+0.80}_{-0.54}（3.2σ偏离零）和w=-1.510^{+0.170}_{-0.120}（3.0σ偏离-1）；其有效状态方程呈现红移演化并穿越幽灵界限，且贝叶斯证据支持其优于ΛCDM及其他三类IDE模型。

Conclusion: Q∝ρ_de类相互作用（尤其是Q=βH₀ρ_de）能更好拟合当前多信使宇宙学数据，暗示暗能量与暗物质可能存在非引力相互作用，为解决幽灵穿越现象提供了有竞争力的物理解释。

Abstract: Recent DESI baryon acoustic oscillation (BAO) analyses suggest a preference for an evolving dark energy equation of state (EoS) that crosses the phantom divide $w=-1$. Interactions between dark energy and dark matter can also produce an apparent phantom-divide crossing in the effective dark energy EoS. In this work, we investigate interacting dark energy (IDE) models with a constant EoS parameter $w$, using cosmic microwave background (CMB) observations from Planck, ACT, and SPT, DESI DR2 BAO measurements, and type Ia supernova data from PantheonPlus, DES-Dovekie, and DESY5. We consider four typical forms of the interaction term $Q$. Our results show that the IDE models with $Q\proptoρ_{\rm de}$ generally exhibit a stronger preference for nonzero dark-sector interactions. In particular, for the CMB+DESI+DES-Dovekie data, the IDE model with $Q=βH_0ρ_{\rm de}$ yields $β=-2.52^{+0.80}_{-0.54}$ and $w=-1.510^{+0.170}_{-0.120}$, corresponding to deviations from $β=0$ and $w=-1$ at the $3.2σ$ and $3.0σ$ levels, respectively. Interestingly, the effective EoS of the IDE model with $Q=βH_0ρ_{\rm de}$ exhibits a redshift evolution resembling that of the $w_0w_a$CDM model, crossing the phantom divide. Bayesian evidence further indicates that this IDE model is favored over $Λ$CDM and the other three IDE models. Our analysis helps identify which interaction forms better account for current cosmological observations.

</details>


### [126] [GPAge: a Global Parameterization based on cosmic Age for some unified early-time and late-time solutions to the Hubble tension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30961)
*Jia-Le Ling,Shao-Jiang Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文扩展了基于宇宙年龄的参数化方法（PAge），使其能够同时描述早期和晚期宇宙，并在统一暗流体框架下完成了辐射主导时期的背景和扰动分析；结果表明，即使联合调节早期物质类密度和晚期膨胀历史，仍不足以解决哈勃常数张力，暗示需要超出简单调整物质密度的再复合前新物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对超过5σ的哈勃张力，需对ΛCDM模型进行修正；大量早期/晚期解决方案促使研究者构建能统一约束可行解空间的现象学参数化方法。

Method: 将原始PAge参数化拓展至涵盖辐射主导时期，并在统一暗流体框架下完成背景与扰动理论的完整构建，使其可高精度复现目标宇宙学模型。

Result: 全局PAge参数化相较于ΛCDM改善了最佳拟合，但未能显著提升H₀以缓解哈勃张力；改变早期物质类密度并结合灵活的晚期膨胀历史仍不足解决问题。

Conclusion: 解决哈勃张力需引入再复合之前的、超越简单调节物质类密度的新物理机制。

Abstract: The over $5σ$ Hubble tension continues to motivate modifications of the standard $Λ$-cold-dark-matter ($Λ\rm CDM$) model. The large number of proposed resolutions for the Hubble tension, broadly divided into early- and late-time solutions, motivates phenomenological parametrizations to constrain the viable solution space. Recently, there is a growing interest in unifying early- and late-time solutions. In this work, we extend the original Parametrization based on cosmic Age (PAge) to cover both the early and late Universe. Specifically, we incorporate the radiation-dominated epoch at the background level and complete the perturbation sector within a unified-dark-fluid framework. The resulting parametrization can accurately reproduce target cosmological models at both the background and perturbation levels. Confronting this global PAge with cosmological observations, we find that it improves the best fit relative to $Λ\rm CDM$, but does not raise $H_0$ significantly enough to alleviate the Hubble tension. Our parametrizations indicate that varying the early-time matter-like density, even together with a flexible late-time expansion history, is insufficient to resolve the Hubble tension. These results suggest that resolving the Hubble tension requires pre-recombination physics beyond a simple retuning of the matter-like density.

</details>


### [127] [Assessing the consistency of the CPL dark-energy model with cosmological data: Simulation-calibrated neural posterior disagreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31044)
*Tian-Yang Sun,Tian-Nuo Li,Guo-Hong Du,He Wang,Jing-Fei Zhang,Xin Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经后验估计与流匹配后验估计之间差异的模型检验方法，用于评估Chevallier-Polarski-Linder（CPL）暗能量参数化模型与当前宇宙学观测（CMB+DESI+DESY5）的一致性；结果显示全局p值为0.524，表明CPL模型与当前数据一致，且该方法评估速度快（中位时间0.53秒）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期DESI观测暗示动力学暗能量的状态方程可能在CPL参数化下穿越幽灵界限，因此需系统检验CPL模型是否与当前多源宇宙学观测一致。

Method: 构建基于神经后验估计（NPE）与流匹配（flow-matching）后验估计之间不一致性的模型检验框架；二者均在相同CPL模拟数据上训练，其后验差异通过独立模拟校准为全局p值；并通过受控模拟验证p值可靠性及对模型偏离形式的敏感性。

Result: 对压缩后的CMB+DESI+DESY5数据向量进行检验，得到全局p值p_global=0.524，表明当前观测与CPL模型一致；方法中位评估时间为0.53秒。

Conclusion: CPL参数化模型目前仍与主流宇宙学观测数据兼容；所提出的快速、可校准的模型检验框架为后续暗能量物理本质研究提供了稳健工具。

Abstract: Recent DESI observations hint at a preference for dynamical dark energy with an equation of state that crosses the phantom divide within the Chevallier-Polarski-Linder (CPL) parameterization. In this work, we assess the consistency of the CPL parameterization with current cosmological observations. We develop a model check based on the disagreement between posteriors inferred by neural posterior estimation and flow-matching posterior estimation. Both estimators are trained on the same CPL simulations, and their posterior disagreement is calibrated using independent simulations to obtain a global $p$-value. Controlled simulations assess the reliability of the calibrated \(p\)-values and show how detection sensitivity depends on the form of departure from the assumed model. Applying the test to the compressed CMB+DESI+DESY5 data vector yields $p_{\rm global}=0.524$, indicating that the current observations are consistent with the CPL model. The method has a median evaluation time of only $0.53\,{\rm s}$, enabling efficient assessments of cosmological model adequacy. This framework enables efficient tests of dark-energy models, providing a firmer basis for investigating the physical origin of cosmic acceleration.

</details>


### [128] [Cosmological Inference with Gravitational-Wave Dark Sirens and 21-cm Intensity Mapping: Forecasts for LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA with MeerKAT and SKAO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31081)
*Dounia Nanadoumgar-Lacroze,Konstantin Leyde,Steven Cunnington,Tessa Baker,Ian Harrison,Ulyana Dupletsa,Simone Mastrogiovanni,Riccardo Murgia,Tommaso Ronconi,Matteo Schulz,Marta Spinelli*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了引力波（GW）与21厘米强度映射（HI IM）联合用于精确测量哈勃常数H₀的潜力，发现尽管观测限制（如波束卷积、噪声、前景处理）削弱了约束能力，但引入HI信息仍可使H₀不确定性分别比纯光谱标准烛光方法降低61%（MeerKLASS）和53%（SKA-Mid）；在最现实场景下，H₀精度达10%（MeerKLASS）和15%（SKA-Mid）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波提供直接的距离测量，21厘米强度映射追踪物质分布，二者协同有望显著提升哈勃常数H₀的测量精度，缓解当前宇宙学中的H₀张力问题。

Method: 基于LVK O5灵敏度下约150个信噪比>12的模拟引力波事件，联合MeerKLASS和SKA-Mid的模拟中性氢强度映射数据，在标准烛光框架下开展联合宇宙学与种群参数分析，并系统评估各类观测限制（波束、噪声、前景、天区覆盖）对H₀约束的影响。

Result: 忽略GW参数不确定性时，HI信息可实现亚百分比H₀约束；考虑实际观测限制后，HI信息仍使H₀不确定性较纯光谱标准烛光方法降低61%（MeerKLASS）和53%（SKA-Mid）；波束效应影响最大，噪声可能引发后验多峰性，前景处理不当会显著影响结果；最现实情形下H₀精度为10%（MeerKLASS）和15%（SKA-Mid）。

Conclusion: 引力波与21厘米强度映射联合是提升H₀测量精度的有效途径，但其性能高度依赖于对GW参数误差及HI观测系统误差（尤其是波束和前景）的准确建模与校准；未来需结合多实现统计以评估结果稳健性。

Abstract: Gravitational waves (GWs) and 21-cm intensity mapping (IM) are emerging as complementary cosmological probes: GWs provide direct luminosity-distance measurements, while neutral-hydrogen (HI) IM traces the matter distribution in redshift space. Their synergy offers a promising avenue for precise measurements of the Hubble constant. Within a standard-siren framework, we perform a joint cosmological and population analysis using $\mathcal{O}(150)$ mock GW detections with SNR > 12, as projected for one year of observations at LVK O5 sensitivity. We combine these with mock HI intensity maps from MeerKLASS and SKA-Mid, and study how observational limitations in both the GW and IM data affect the $H_0$ constraints. We find that uncertainty in GW parameter measurements is a dominant limitation on the cosmological constraints. Neglecting this uncertainty, the inclusion of HI information enables sub-percent constraints on $H_0$. We also find that observational effects in the HI surveys weaken the constraints, although they remain tighter than those obtained without HI information (spectral sirens). Despite accounting for HI IM observational limitations, including HI information reduces the $H_0$ uncertainty relative to the spectral-siren results by 61% for MeerKLASS and 53% for SKA-Mid. Because our results are based on a single realisation, they should be interpreted in light of possible realisation-to-realisation variability. Beam convolution most severely weakens the constraints. Instrumental noise can induce multimodality in the posterior, potentially biasing the inferred parameters. The results are sensitive to foreground under-cleaning and over-cleaning. Partial sky coverage has a relatively small impact. In the most realistic scenario, which accounts for both GW and HI IM limitations, we obtain $H_0$ precision estimates of 10% and 15% for MeerKLASS and SKA-Mid, respectively.

</details>


### [129] [A cosmic filament catalog for COSMOS-Web Data Release 1 across 0.5 < z < 3.5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31168)
*Qiaoyang Hao,Tao Wang,Weishan Zhu,Hong Guo,Peng Wang,Tiancheng Yang,Luwenjia Zhou*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合光度红移PDF的新方法，利用COSMOS-Web DR1数据构建了0.5 < z < 3.5的宇宙细丝星表，并发布了细丝、线段及细丝概率图等数据产品，为研究宇宙正午时期星系与大尺度结构的共同演化提供了坚实基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙细丝是星系形成和演化的主要场所，但其空间分布及其与星系性质（尤其在高红移下）的关系尚不明确。

Method: 基于DisPerSE方法，创新性地融入星系光度红移的概率分布函数（PDF），通过对每个二维层生成多个实现并叠加，构建“细丝概率图”（w_fil图），再从中提取细丝；参数优化使用TNG模拟生成的模拟星表，以最小化三维与二维投影细丝间的差异。

Result: 在0.5 < z < 3.5范围内检测到12,523条细丝（共136,210个线段），发布了细丝星表、配套线段星表及各层细丝概率图；验证显示提取结果与模拟中物理性质高度一致。

Conclusion: 该方法稳健可靠，所生成的数据产品为深入研究宇宙正午及更早时期的星系-环境协同演化提供了关键工具和坚实基础。

Abstract: Cosmic filaments represent the environments in which most galaxies form and evolve. However, the spatial distribution of filaments and the link between galaxy properties and their positions relative to filaments remains unclear, particularly at high redshifts. In this work, using the deep-field catalog COSMOS-Web Data Release 1, we construct a filament catalog spanning 0.5 < z < 3.5 that can be directly applied to studies of galaxy evolution and the galaxy--large-scale-structure connection at the peak epoch of galaxy and black hole formation. We developed a new procedure based on the DisPerSE method that fully incorporates the probability distribution functions (PDFs) of galaxy photometric redshifts by generating multiple realizations for each tomographic 2D slice. We then stacked these realizations to construct ``filament probability maps'' (w_ fil maps), from which filaments were subsequently extracted. The filament extraction parameters were optimized using mock catalogs generated from TNG simulations by minimizing the discrepancies between 3D and projected 2D filaments. Applying this procedure to the COSMOS-Web data, we detected 12,523 filaments (comprising 136,210 segments) across 0.5 < z < 3.5 and released a filament catalog, a companion segment catalog, and filament probability maps for each slice. Validation against astrophysical properties shows a strong agreement between the extracted filaments and those in the simulations, supporting the robustness of the filament extraction. These data products provide a robust foundation for investigating galaxy--environment coevolution at cosmic noon and beyond.

</details>


### [130] [Robust paths to primordial $B$ modes in the presence of polarised point source contamination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31175)
*Arianna Rizzieri,Giorgia Zagatti,David Alonso*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了极化河外点源（ERSs和DSFGs）对宇宙微波背景（CMB）B模探测的污染影响，发现ERSs若不加掩模会导致宇宙学及前景参数偏差；通过掩模高于~0.1 Jy的ERSs并辅以简单残余建模，可在几乎不损失精度的前提下实现无偏参数恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMB B模探测需有效控制和去除天体物理极化前景污染，尤其是地面实验中极化河外点源的影响尚需量化评估。

Method: 基于先进模型生成含极化ERSs和DSFGs的合成数据，模拟其在组分分离流程中的传播效应，并评估不同掩模与建模策略对最终参数约束的影响。

Result: DSFGs污染可忽略；未掩模的ERSs会显著偏差银河系同步辐射和透镜B模参数；掩模>0.1 Jy ERSs并边际化残余污染，可实现无偏且高精度的参数恢复。

Conclusion: 对极化河外点源实施合理掩模与残余建模是地面CMB实验获取可靠原初B模信号的关键步骤，该策略在精度与鲁棒性间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: A detection of primordial $B$ modes in the Cosmic Microwave Background (CMB) polarisation relies on a robust control and removal of contamination from astrophysical polarised foreground emission. We examine how much polarised extragalactic point sources may contaminate the cosmological signal in ground-based CMB experiments, propagating their impact through component separation to the final parameter constraints. We discuss masking and modelling strategies to ensure that the resulting constraints remain both accurate and precise under this source of contamination. We produce synthetic data containing polarised emission from extragalactic radio sources (ERSs) and dusty star-forming galaxies (DSFGs) following state-of-the-art models for both samples. We find that, while the DSFG contribution is likely too small to cause any significant bias to the cosmological or foreground parameters, the ERS contribution instead, if left unmasked, leads to a bias in the parameters describing the Galactic synchrotron and lensing $B$-mode components. For experiments targeting noise levels of order $σ_P\simeq 1\,μK\,{\rm arcmin}$, we find that masking all ERSs with fluxes above $\sim$0.1 Jy, coupled with a relatively simple model to marginalise over residual point-source contamination, achieves a suitable balance that allows for the robust recovery of unbiased cosmological and foreground parameters at a negligible cost in parameter uncertainties.

</details>


### [131] [A cosmological intercept tension in local distance ladders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31181)
*Haotian Dong,Marek Biesiada,Zhoujian Cao,Shao-Jiang Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过前后向一致性检验，利用Pantheon+数据和Planck-CMB先验，在ΛCDM框架下检验Ia型超新星在造父变星宿主星系与哈勃流样本之间的距离阶梯内部一致性；引入Manticore-局部速度场重构修正宿主星系的本动速度后，H₀推断值更接近CMB约束，截距张力显著降低（6.8σ→3.9σ，7.3σ→4.3σ），表明本动速度建模对缓解哈勃张力具有重要作用，但仍存在残余不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哈勃常数张力（Hubble tension）促使研究者直接检验本地距离阶梯各环节的内部一致性，尤其是第二、第三阶（Cepheid宿主SNe Ia与哈勃流SNe Ia）之间是否自洽。

Method: 在平坦ΛCDM模型下，对Cepheid宿主SNe Ia或哈勃流SNe Ia样本施加Planck-CMB先验，校准超新星绝对星等，再外推至另一样本以恢复H₀；采用Manticore-Local重建的红移修正来处理宿主星系本动速度，并比较修正前后两个样本的星等-红移关系截距差异。

Result: 本动速度修正使前向分析H₀从74.30^{+1.18}_{-1.20}降至71.37^{+1.23}_{-1.18} km/s/Mpc，后向分析从60.83^{+0.98}_{-0.97}升至63.25^{+0.95}_{-0.93} km/s/Mpc；截距张力显著减弱（前向6.8σ→3.9σ，后向7.3σ→4.3σ），但残余张力仍存在。

Conclusion: 本动速度建模可部分缓解距离阶梯内部的截距张力，凸显其在哈勃常数测量中的关键作用；剩余不一致提示需进一步排查样本选择及校准系统误差。

Abstract: The Hubble tension motivates direct tests of the internal consistency of the local distance ladder. We perform forward and backward consistency checks between Type Ia supernovae in Cepheid host galaxies and the Hubble-flow supernova sample using Pantheon+ data. Within flat $Λ$CDM, we impose Planck-CMB priors on one sample, infer the supernova absolute-magnitude calibration, and propagate it to the other sample to recover the Hubble constant, which should match the input value if the second and third rungs of the distance ladder align perfectly. We examine the impact of host-galaxy peculiar velocities using redshift corrections based on the Manticore-Local reconstruction. These corrections shift the recovered $H_0$ from $74.30^{+1.18}_{-1.20}$ to $71.37^{+1.23}_{-1.18}$ $\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}Mpc^{-1}}$ in the forward analysis and from $60.83^{+0.98}_{-0.97}$ to $63.25^{+0.95}_{-0.93}$ $\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}Mpc^{-1}}$ in the backward analysis, bringing both results closer to the input CMB constraint. Comparing the cosmological intercepts of the magnitude-redshift relation inferred from two rungs of the distance ladder before and after peculiar-velocity corrections, we find that the significance of the discrepancy decreases from $6.8σ$ to $3.9σ$ in the forward check and from $7.3σ$ to $4.3σ$ in the backward check. Peculiar-velocity corrections therefore partially alleviate the intercept tension, while a residual discrepancy persists within the adopted framework. Our results highlight the importance of local velocity modeling and motivate further tests of sample selection and calibration systematics in assessing distance-ladder consistency.

</details>


### [132] [Gravitational Wave Diffractive Images Reveal the Structure of Astrophysical Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31441)
*Han Gil Choi,Sunghoon Jung,Volodymyr Takhistov*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用引力波衍射效应探测暗物质等不可见质量分布的新方法，通过分析透镜密度的傅里叶变换与衍射图像的关系，结合数值波光学计算，评估下一代引力波探测器对扩展型暗物质结构、带晕原初黑洞及超辐射云的观测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波作为新型探针，可类比带电粒子散射探测原子核结构，用于探测传统手段不可见的扩展质量分布（如暗物质结构、原初黑洞晕、超辐射云）；现有引力透镜研究多局限于几何光学近似，忽略衍射效应，而该效应在特定参数下可提供关键结构信息。

Method: 在透镜势的一阶微扰（Born近似）下，推导引力波衍射中心像与投影透镜密度傅里叶变换的关系；将有限质量透镜的衍射振幅表示为点质量Born振幅乘以归一化质量形因子（类比核散射）；利用全数值波光学模拟，量化不同天体物理透镜模型（扩展暗物质、晕包裹的原初黑洞、超辐射标量云）的可观测性。

Result: 五年观测下，下一代探测器可区分扩展透镜与点质量透镜，探测暗物质占比低至O(10^{-5})；可区分晕包裹与无晕原初黑洞，探测原初黑洞暗物质占比低至O(10^{-6})；对近距离源-透镜系统，可识别质量约10^{-17}–10^{-12} eV的标量超辐射云的衍射特征。

Conclusion: 引力波衍射为刻画扩展型暗物质和致密天体周围环境提供了新框架，拓展了引力波天文学在粒子物理与天体物理交叉领域的探测能力。

Abstract: Scattering patterns encode the structure of extended objects, as demonstrated by charged particle scattering on atomic nuclei. Gravitational wave (GW) diffraction offers an analogous probe of otherwise invisible mass distributions. At linear order in the lens potential we derive expressions relating the central diffractive image contribution to a gravitationally lensed GW waveform to the Fourier transform of the projected lens density. For finite mass lenses this contribution takes the form of a point mass Born amplitude multiplied by a normalized mass form factor, analogous to that in nuclear scattering. As the frequency of GWs from a merging compact binary source evolves the signal samples this form factor across spatial scales, imprinting lens structure through interference with the primary image. Using full numerical wave optics calculations we quantify the observational reach for extended dark matter objects, primordial black holes halo-dressed by dark matter halos and superradiance clouds of ultralight bosons around black holes. For the considered source populations, five year observations with next generation detectors can distinguish extended lenses from point masses, probing dark matter fractions down to $\mathcal{O}(10^{-5})$, and halo-dressed primordial black holes from matched bare black holes, probing primordial black hole dark matter fractions down to $\mathcal{O}(10^{-6})$. For sufficiently close source-lens configurations, scalar superradiance clouds can produce distinguishable diffraction signatures for boson masses of approximately $10^{-17}-10^{-12}~\mathrm{eV}$. These results provide a framework for characterizing extended dark matter and compact object astrophysical environments.

</details>


### [133] [QUIJOTE scientific results - XII. Characterization of the polarized synchrotron emission at the power spectrum level in the QUIJOTE-MFI wide survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31469)
*F. Vansyngel,J. A. Rubino-Martin,P. de Ferra,R. B. Barreiro,R. T. Genova-Santos,B. Casaponsa,M. Fernandez-Torreiro,D. Adak,K. Aryan,M. Ashdown,J. M. Casas,F. J. Casas,R. Cepeda-Arroita,A. Fasano,D. Herranz,E. Martinez-Gonzalez,F. Poidevin,R. Rebolo,B. Ruiz-Granados,D. Tramonte,P. Vielva,R. A. Watson*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用QUIJOTE-MFI宽视场巡天（10–20 GHz）联合Planck和WMAP数据，在多极矩范围30 < ℓ < 130内分析了偏振同步辐射的功率谱，发现EE与BB谱均可用幂律拟合，BB谱略陡于EE；同步辐射频谱指数在不同银纬区间无显著空间变化；BB/EE功率比约0.2，显著低于尘埃；测得尘埃与同步辐射空间相关性为0.1–0.25；估算其对CMB B模观测的污染等效张量标量比r ~ 0.003（90 GHz）和r ~ 0.0002（150 GHz）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确分离银河系前景（尤其是同步辐射）对宇宙微波背景（CMB）B模信号的污染，是探测原初引力波的关键前提。本文旨在利用低频新数据（QUIJOTE）结合已有高频数据（Planck、WMAP），在功率谱域更精确刻画同步辐射的偏振特性（包括谱指数、EE/BB谱形、空间变化及与尘埃的相关性），以改进前景建模和污染评估。

Method: 在|b| > 20°天区，使用QUIJOTE-MFI（10–20 GHz）、Planck（30–353 GHz）和WMAP（23–94 GHz）的偏振数据，通过功率谱（EE和BB）联合拟合，采用幂律模型提取同步辐射的角谱斜率（α_EE, α_BB）和频率谱指数（β_s^EE, β_s^BB）；分析不同银纬切片下β_s的空间一致性；计算同步辐射BB与EE功率比及与热尘埃的交叉功率谱（空间相关性）；最后外推至典型CMB观测频段（90/150 GHz），估算其对B模的等效张量标量比r。

Result: EE和BB功率谱均被幂律良好拟合；α_EE = −3.24 ± 0.12，α_BB = −3.81 ± 0.34（暗示BB谱略陡）；β_s^EE = −3.26 ± 0.05，β_s^BB = −3.25 ± 0.10，且在不同银纬间无显著变化；BB/EE功率比≈0.2；尘埃-同步辐射空间相关性为0.1–0.25；同步辐射对90 GHz和150 GHz CMB B模测量的污染分别等效于r ∼ 0.003 和 r ∼ 0.0002。

Conclusion: 同步辐射偏振前景在所研究尺度上具有相对简单、稳定的幂律行为，其频谱指数在高银纬区域空间均匀，BB/EE比值较低且与尘埃存在中等程度空间相关；这些结果支持将同步辐射作为可建模前景处理，所估算的污染水平（尤其在150 GHz）表明其对下一代CMB实验探测小r值原初B模构成可控但需谨慎扣除的系统误差源。

Abstract: We present an analysis of the polarized synchrotron emission at the power-spectrum level in the multipole range $30 < \ell < 130$, using the recent QUIJOTE-MFI wide survey (10-20GHz) in combination with Planck and WMAP data. The EE and BB power spectra are well described by a simple power-law model, with no evidence for the need of additional parameters. The best-fit slopes are $α_{EE} = -3.24 \pm 0.12$ and $α_{BB} = -3.81 \pm 0.34$ for $|b|>20^\circ$, hinting toward a steepening of the BB spectrum relative to EE at the $\sim 1.6σ$ level. The synchrotron frequency spectral indices are found to be $β_s^{EE} = -3.26 \pm 0.05$ and $β_s^{BB} = -3.25 \pm 0.10$ for the same cut, in agreement with earlier real-space analyses. We find no evidence for spatial variations of $β_s$ across different Galactic latitude cuts, within current uncertainties. The synchrotron BB/EE power ratio is approximately 0.2 over the considered multipole range, lower than typical values for thermal dust, and consistent with previous measurements. We also measure a dust-synchrotron spatial correlation in the range 0.1-0.25 over $30 < \ell < 130$. Using these results, we estimate the level of synchrotron contamination in future CMB B-mode measurements, finding an average power in the considered sky masks equivalent to $r \sim 0.003$ at 90GHz and $r \sim 0.0002$ at 150GHz for the relevant angular scales.

</details>


### [134] [A Robust Estimator for the Lyman-$α$ Forest 1D Power Spectrum at High Redshift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31626)
*Molly Wolfson,Joseph F. Hennawi,Frederick B. Davies,Zarija Lukić*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种基于似然的Lyman-α森林通量功率谱P(k)估计方法，通过构建像元级协方差矩阵实现对高红移下复杂观测效应（如分辨率、噪声、缺失数据）鲁棒且近似最优的功率谱测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高红移下Lyα森林功率谱测量面临信噪比低、数据稀疏、观测效应复杂等挑战，传统傅里叶变换估计器受限；亟需一种能同时处理仪器效应、缺失数据和大尺度物理效应（如紫外背景涨落、再电离末期）的稳健估计方法。

Method: 构建像素级透射通量场协方差矩阵，该矩阵由预计算的k空间积分生成，显式包含仪器分辨率、像素化等效应；采用直接最大似然推断估计P(k)；在z≥5的模拟光谱（含解析高斯实现与前向建模含真实观测效应的模拟）上进行测试。

Result: 该似然估计器在测试尺度上能以预期误差精度恢复输入功率谱，并对有限分辨率、噪声、缺失数据等观测效应保持稳健；性能优于标准傅里叶变换估计器。

Conclusion: 所提似然基估计器为高红移Lyα森林功率谱测量提供了稳健、近似最优的新框架，特别适用于探测与再电离晚期及紫外背景相关的超大尺度涨落。

Abstract: The Lyman-$α$ (Ly$α$) forest flux power spectrum $P(k)$ is sensitive to a range of astrophysical and cosmological effects, including the thermal state of and large-scale fluctuations in the intergalactic medium and the nature of dark matter. Measuring $P(k)$ becomes increasingly challenging at high redshift, where fewer quasar spectra are available and higher Ly$α$ optical depths result in lower transmitted flux. In addition, observational effects and missing data complicate standard Fourier-transform estimators. Here, we validate a likelihood-based estimator for $P(k)$. The method constructs the pixel-level covariance matrix of the transmitted flux field using precomputed $k$-space integrals that account for instrumental resolution and pixelization, enabling direct maximum-likelihood inference of $P(k)$. We test the estimator using mock Ly$α$ forest spectra at $z \geq 5$, including analytic Gaussian realizations generated from known power spectra and forward-modeled simulation spectra incorporating observational effects relevant to high-redshift quasar surveys, such as finite spectral resolution, pixel sampling, noise, and missing data. We compare the recovered power spectra with both the input spectra and standard Fourier-transform estimates. We find that the likelihood-based estimator recovers the input power spectrum across the tested scales well within expected error and remains robust to the observational effects considered. This work establishes a framework for measuring Ly$α$ forest clustering with a robust, nearly optimal power spectrum estimator, with particular focus on quantifying large-scale fluctuations that may arise from ultraviolet background fluctuations or the late stages of reionization.

</details>


### [135] [Sound-Horizon-Agnostic Forecast for Constraints on $H_0$ and $Σm_ν$ from BAO, CMB Lensing, Supernovae and LSST-like Galaxy Lensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31660)
*Seyed Hamidreza Mirpoorian,Helena García Escudero,Levon Pogosian,Amy Wayland,David Alonso*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种不依赖于重子声速尺度rd标准早期宇宙校准的、对哈勃常数H0和中微子质量总和Σmν的联合限制方法，结合了LSST类弱引力透镜、Simons Observatory类CMB透镜、DESI BAO及扩展超新星数据，并系统考虑多种观测系统误差与高L CMB透镜信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决ΛCDM模型中对声速尺度rd早期宇宙理论校准的依赖问题，提供更模型无关的H0和Σmν约束，并检验早期宇宙物理假设（如再复合或早期暗能量）的影响。

Method: 将rd作为自由参数，联合分析LSST类星系弱透镜、Simons-Observatory类CMB透镜（L_max=5000）、DESI BAO和扩展超新星数据；显式边缘化光度红移标定、剪切乘性偏差、内禀对齐及重子反馈等弱透镜系统误差。

Result: 固定Σmν时，H0不确定性为0.7–1.5 km/s/Mpc（取决于先验）；Σmν自由变化时，H0约束减弱，但仍给出95%置信上限≈0.4 eV；rd-无关方法在修改再复合模型中无偏，在早期暗能量模型中失效。

Conclusion: 该rd-无关方法可稳健约束H0和Σmν，但其有效性依赖于标准早期宇宙物理假设；是检验ΛCDM一致性和探索新物理的重要工具。

Abstract: We present a sound-horizon-agnostic forecast for constraints on the Hubble constant $H_0$ and the sum of the neutrino masses $Σm_ν$ from a combination of LSST-like galaxy weak lensing, Simons-Observatory-like CMB lensing, the completed DESI BAO program, and expanded supernova samples. Treating the sound horizon at baryon drag $r_d$ as a free parameter, the method provides a consistency test of $Λ$CDM without relying on the standard early-Universe calibration of $r_d$. The analysis incorporates realistic galaxy weak-lensing systematics, explicitly marginalizing over photometric-redshift calibration, multiplicative shear biases, intrinsic alignments, and baryonic feedback, and extends the CMB-lensing analysis to $L_{\max}=5000$. For fixed $Σm_ν$, we forecast uncertainties of $σ(H_0)\simeq 1.5$ km/s/Mpc with our least informative prior set, $σ(H_0)\simeq 1.3$ km/s/Mpc with more restrictive priors, and $σ(H_0)\simeq 0.7$ km/s/Mpc with an additional informative prior on the amplitude of primordial fluctuations $A_s$. Allowing $Σm_ν$ to vary weakens the $H_0$ constraints, while still yielding a forecast 95% CL upper limit of about 0.4 eV on $Σm_ν$. We also clarify the scope of the $r_d$-agnostic approach through validation tests using mock data from modified recombination and early dark energy models. The method recovers unbiased constraints on $H_0$ for the former but not the latter, highlighting its dependence on assumptions about early-Universe physics.

</details>
