### [136] [Flexible Proposal Distributions for Gravitational-Wave Population Inference with Gaussian Mixture Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30187)
*Simon M. C. Yeung,Ignacio Magaña Hernandez,Jolien Creighton*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于高斯混合模型（GMM）和重要性采样的新方法，用于引力波源群体分析中的分层贝叶斯推断，以缓解蒙特卡洛方差问题并提升对尖锐分布特征的建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着引力波事件目录扩大，传统基于有限后验样本的蒙特卡洛积分面临方差增大、难以捕捉尖锐种群特征等问题；且事件后验样本数量固定，无法灵活增加采样。

Method: 用高斯混合模型（GMM）拟合各事件后验分布，并结合两种重要性采样方案（事件后验集合、及其与先验种群模型的混合）来估计分层似然；同时用GMM表示探测到的注入信号，从而可灵活生成额外样本用于事件积分和选择效应积分。

Result: 在模拟数据测试中，GMM估计器未表现出可检测的额外偏差，校准性能与传统后验样本估计器相当；应用于GWTC-3数据并采用不同质量模型时，结果一致。

Conclusion: 该方法解耦了数值采样预算与原始后验样本数，提升了计算灵活性，但需显式检验密度拟合不确定性和近似误差。

Abstract: Population analyses of gravitational-wave (GW) sources use hierarchical Bayesian inference to constrain astrophysical models while accounting for selection effects. The likelihood is usually estimated by Monte Carlo integration over finite sets of event posterior samples and simulated injections. As catalogs grow, more samples are needed to control the Monte Carlo variance, but the available event samples are fixed by parameter estimation. Sharp population features can also lie in regions poorly sampled by the event posteriors. We fit Gaussian mixture models (GMMs) to the event posteriors and evaluate the likelihood by importance sampling with two proposals: an ensemble of event posteriors and a mixture of that ensemble with a fiducial population. Together with a GMM representation of detected injections, this allows additional samples to be drawn for both event and selection integrals. Increasing the sample count reduces Monte Carlo variance but leaves uncertainty from fitting the densities to finite training sets. In simulated catalogs, the GMM-based estimators show no detectable additional bias relative to the posterior-sample estimator; their calibration is comparable within the limitations of the tests. An application to GWTC-3 with a different mass model gives consistent results across the estimators. The method separates the numerical sampling budget from the original sample count, while requiring explicit checks of density-fit uncertainty and approximation error.

</details>


### [137] [Partial geometrization of dark matter in metric $f(R)$ gravity: Exact reconstruction and growth constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30201)
*Davide Campanella Galanti,Oliver F. Piattella*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在f(R)引力理论中，几何效应能解释多少暗物质。研究发现，只有极小一部分（1-ξ ≤ 10^{-10}）暗物质可由时空几何提供，其余必须由传统粒子态暗物质承担；所有稳定模型均与太阳系实验（如卡西尼实验）或大尺度结构观测严重冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验f(R)类修改引力理论能否用纯几何机制替代部分甚至全部暗物质，同时满足宇宙学演化（ΛCDM背景）与局部引力实验约束。

Method: 通过重构满足ΛCDM背景膨胀历史的稳定f(R)理论，求解F=f_R的演化方程；分析其在辐射、物质、暗能量主导时期的解析解；结合稳定性条件（F单调递减）、高曲率极限行为（太阳系尺度）、线性扰动增长（fσ8、fσ8(z)）、以及Cassini等实验约束，对参数ξ进行多层级限制。

Result: 稳定f(R)模型中，几何贡献的暗物质比例1−ξ被严格限制：Cassini实验给出最强约束1−ξ ≲ 10^{−10}；大尺度结构σ8要求1−ξ ≲ 5×10^{−5}；DESI/SDSS增长率数据给出1−ξ ≲ 10^{−3}–10^{−2}；唯一可行模型必须在高曲率下恢复广义相对论（F→1），且F运行量最小。

Conclusion: 光滑演化的度规f(R)理论无法显著几何化暗物质——其贡献必为可忽略的小量，暗物质主体仍需由真实物质成分承担。

Abstract: We ask what fraction of dark matter can be accounted for by the geometry of a stable metric $f(R)$ theory, requiring the expansion history to be exactly equal to that of the $Λ$CDM model. Only a fraction $ξ$ of dark matter clusters as dust, whereas $f(R)$ supplies the rest, as well as $Λ$. We show that the background reconstruction reduces to a linear equation for $F\equiv f_R$, whose solution is $F-1=(1-ξ)\,Θ(a)$, with $Θ$ independent of $ξ$: the departure from general relativity is exactly proportional to the geometrized fraction. We solve this equation in closed form in the matter$+Λ$ and radiation$+$matter eras: in both, the solution is an elementary particular solution plus two hypergeometric modes. Stability requires $F$ to decrease monotonically in time, so $F$ cannot take its general-relativity value $F=1$ both at early and at late times. The effective gravitational coupling $G/F$ therefore necessarily runs: between the radiation era and the de Sitter attractor, $F$ changes by at least $0.837\,(1-ξ)$. In all stable reconstructions but one, $F-1$ grows as $R^{1/3}$ at high curvature, i.e., in dense environments such as the Sun, and local tests of gravity exclude them. The surviving member has $F\to1$ at early times: it recovers general relativity at high curvature and attains the minimal running of $F$. Keeping the linear growth factor at $k=0.1\,h\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$ within $10\%$ of its $Λ$CDM value requires $1-ξ\lesssim10^{-3}$, the weakest bound among all stable reconstructions; keeping $σ_8$ within $3\%$ requires $1-ξ\lesssim5\times10^{-5}$. Growth-rate data from DESI DR1 and SDSS DR16 give $1-ξ\lesssim10^{-3}$--$10^{-2}$, and the Cassini bound gives $1-ξ\lesssim10^{-10}$. A smoothly evolving metric $f(R)$ background can therefore geometrize only a negligible fraction of dark matter.

</details>


### [138] [Wormhole geometries and energy conditions in linear and non-linear $f(Q,L_m)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30224)
*Sara Rastgoo,Foad Parsaei*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在两种扩展的对称平移引力模型中静态球对称虫洞解的存在性，发现线性模型可支持满足能量条件的物理合理虫洞，而非线性模型可得到非奇异、渐近平坦的虫洞解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在对称平移引力框架下是否存在无需奇异物质（即不违反能量条件）的物理可行虫洞解，以弥合修改引力理论与可观测奇异时空之间的联系。

Method: 基于两类$f(Q,L_m)$引力模型（线性与非线性形式），通过作用量对度规变分导出场方程，并分析静态球对称虫洞解及其能量条件满足情况。

Result: 在线性$f(Q,L_m)=α(-Q)+βL_m$模型中，虫洞解可满足零能量条件（NEC），无需奇异物质；在非线性$f(Q,L_m)=-Q+L_m^α$模型中，借助线性及渐近线性物态方程，获得了非奇异、渐近平坦的虫洞解。

Conclusion: 对称平移引力的扩展模型为构建物理合理的虫洞提供了新机制，降低了对 exotic matter 的依赖，并为连接修改引力与天体观测特征提供了新路径。

Abstract: This study explores static and spherically symmetric wormhole configurations in an extended formulation of symmetric teleparallel gravity governed by $f(Q,L_m)=α(-Q)+βL_m $ and $f(Q,L_m)= -Q+L_m^α $ (where $Q$ is the non-metricity scalar, $L_m$ is the matter Lagrangian, and $α, β$ are constants). By varying the action with respect to the metric, we obtain the corresponding field equations. We conclude that the linear $f(Q,L_m)=α(-Q)+βL_m$ framework successfully supports physically realistic wormholes without requiring extreme energy condition violations. It has been demonstrated that spacetime solutions violating both the radial and lateral null energy conditions in general relativity can nevertheless satisfy the NEC in $f(Q, L_m)$ gravity. Within non-linear $f(Q,L_m)$ gravity, non-exotic asymptotically flat wormhole solutions are derived using linear and asymptotically linear equations of state. Our results open new avenues to link modified gravity models with the observable astrophysical traits of exotic spacetimes.

</details>


### [139] [Ricci-flat Seeds, Trombone Rescaling and Magnetizing Kerr Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30256)
*Liang Ma,H. Lu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类新的Ricci平坦旋转度规（Solution II），通过'去磁化' genuine Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson 黑洞得到，并与先前得到的 Solution I（来自KBR黑洞的去磁化）共同构成 Kerr 黑洞的两类 Ricci 平坦形变；研究了其全局结构、热力学、各种极限及相互联系，并探讨了在 Einstein-Maxwell 和 Kaluza-Klein 理论中的'再磁化'，指出需用适当 trombone 缩放增强 Ernst 变换以获得物理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建 Kerr 黑洞的新 Ricci 平坦形变族，并统一理解 KBR 类黑洞的去磁化与再磁化机制，尤其在不同引力理论框架下确保物理合理性。

Method: 基于对 genuine Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson（G-KBR）黑洞进行去磁化操作构造新解（Solution II）；系统分析其全局结构、热力学性质、极限情形及相互联系；进一步应用 Ernst 变换结合 trombone 缩放实施再磁化，分别在 Einstein-Maxwell 和 Kaluza-Klein 理论中检验解的物理可接受性。

Result: 获得一类新的 Ricci 平坦旋转度规 Solution II；确立了两类由参数 B 标记的 Kerr 黑洞 Ricci 平坦形变；揭示了各类极限间的局部连接；发现为使再磁化解物理合理，必须在 Ernst 变换中引入 trombone 缩放。

Conclusion: 去磁化 G-KBR 黑洞可生成新的物理上自洽的 Ricci 平坦解；Solution I 与 II 共同丰富了 Kerr 黑洞的形变谱；再磁化过程的成功依赖于 Ernst 变换与 trombone 缩放的协同作用，凸显了对称性缩放在构造精确解中的关键作用。

Abstract: We present a new family of Ricci-flat rotating metrics, which we refer to as Solution II, obtained by demagnetizing the genuine Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson (G-KBR) black hole. Together with Solution I, which was previously obtained by demagnetizing the Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson (KBR) black hole, this gives two families of Ricci-flat deformations of the Kerr black hole, each labeled by $B$. We study the global structures, thermodynamics, various limits, and the local connections among these limits. We also study the remagnetizations in both Einstein-Maxwell and Kaluza-Klein theories, and find that, to yield a physically acceptable solution, the Ernst transformation should be augmented by an appropriate trombone rescaling.

</details>


### [140] [Calculating gravitational waves at infinity: the missing link](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30263)
*Guido Da Re,Keefe Mitman,Leo C. Stein,Nils Deppe,Himanshu Chaudhary,Dongze Sun,Michael Boyle,Kyle C. Nelli,Mark A. Scheel,Saul A. Teukolsky*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的初始数据构造方法，用于在数值相对论中将有限区域的柯西演化结果与特征演化结合，从而在时空无穷远（未来零无穷）处准确计算引力波形，避免了虚假瞬态，并实现了从第一周期起的高精度波形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波科学需要在时空无穷远处计算波形模板，但数值求解爱因斯坦方程通常只在有限计算区域内进行柯西演化；需通过后续特征演化将时空紧化以获得无穷远处波形，而该过程依赖于与内部柯西解严格一致的初始数据，否则会产生长期虚假瞬态。

Method: 通过对已有初始数据设定进行严格一致性分析，识别其不足，并提出一种新的、与柯西格式无关的初始数据构造方法，确保特征演化起始时刻物理量及其导数与柯西解精确匹配。

Result: 新方法生成的波形从第一周期起即准确，能正确再现引力波记忆效应和晚期幂律拖尾等物理现象，并支持构建鲁棒、可靠的无穷远波形模板库。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了特征演化中初始数据不一致导致虚假瞬态的关键问题，为高精度、可重复的引力波模板生成提供了坚实基础，提升了数值相对论在引力波天文学中的实用性。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave science requires waveform templates to be computed at future null infinity, the asymptotic boundary of spacetime. Numerical solutions of Einstein's equations typically rely on a Cauchy evolution on a finite computational domain, but can produce waveforms at infinity by performing a subsequent characteristic evolution that compactifies the spacetime. Such waveforms capture expected physical effects, like gravitational-wave memory and late-time, power-law tails. But this characteristic evolution requires initial data consistent with the interior Cauchy solution to avoid producing spurious, long-lived transients. A careful analysis shows that previous prescriptions for this initial data were not sufficiently consistent and provides a way to remedy the problem. The new initial data yield accurate waveforms from the first cycle onward. The construction is independent of the Cauchy formulation and enables the production of catalogs of robust waveforms at infinity.

</details>


### [141] [Accurate initial data for Cauchy-characteristic evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30268)
*Guido Da Re,Keefe Mitman,Leo C. Stein,Nils Deppe,Himanshu Chaudhary,Dongze Sun,Michael Boyle,Kyle C. Nelli,Mark A. Scheel,Saul A. Teukolsky*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进Cauchy-characteristic evolution（CCE）初始数据的方法，通过修正角坐标并匹配径向二阶导数，有效消除了长期存在的虚假瞬态，显著提升了引力波模板在无穷远边界处的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于数值相对论的引力波模板在有限半径处计算并外推至未来零无穷远（ℐ⁺），但该方法无法准确捕捉动量流和记忆效应；而CCE虽理论上更优，却受限于引发长寿命虚假瞬态的不良初始数据。

Method: 识别出错误的初始角坐标是虚假瞬态的根源，构造满足世界管数据径向二阶导数匹配的初始数据，并求解与所得径向分布一致的角坐标。

Result: 成功消除虚假瞬态：双黑洞(2,2)模振幅上持续超1000M、幅度达10%的调制消失；正碰黑洞碰撞的晚期尾部被准确恢复；该方法兼容已有Cauchy模拟存档，支持重处理。

Conclusion: 所提方法显著提升CCE波形精度，为构建更高精度的引力波模板库和数据分析奠定基础。

Abstract: Accurate gravitational-wave (GW) templates from numerical-relativity (NR) simulations are essential for GW science. These templates are typically computed at finite radii and extrapolated to future null infinity $\mathscr{I}^{+}$, the asymptotic boundary of spacetime. However, this procedure is known to not accurately capture momentum fluxes and memory effects, limiting the accuracy of waveform models and data analyses that rely on them. Cauchy-characteristic evolution (CCE) instead integrates Einstein's equations along outgoing null hypersurfaces from a Cauchy simulation's finite-radius worldtube to $\mathscr{I}^{+}$. Its accuracy, though, has been limited by initial data that produce long-lived spurious transients, which contaminate several cycles of the waveform emitted by the binary coalescence. We identify incorrect initial angular coordinates as the source of these transients. We construct initial data that matches the Cauchy worldtube data through second order in the radial derivatives and solve for the angular coordinates consistent with the resulting radial profile. These improvements remove the unphysical transient: a $10\%$ modulation of a binary black hole's $(2,2)$ mode amplitude lasting over $1000\,M$ disappears, and the late-time tail of a head-on black hole collision is recovered. The construction is independent of the Cauchy formulation and allows archived simulations to be reprocessed with CCE, paving the way for improved GW catalogs.

</details>


### [142] [Quintaverse-Phantiverse Cosmologies in $f(Q,C)$ Gravity: a Non-coincident Geometrical Origin of Cosmic Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30302)
*Hamid Shabani,Avik De*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了f(Q,C)引力理论中二次形式拉格朗日量的渐近暗能量行为，通过构建动力学系统，发现晚期加速膨胀可由几何效应自然产生，无需引入独立暗能量成分；其中一类连接给出有效暗物质解（含压强为零物质与刚性流体），另一类则存在能量动量守恒与物质密度正定不可兼得的问题；两类均存在普适稳定加速解，并据此提出'Quintaverse'与'Phantiverse'新术语以区分类夸克与类幽灵吸引子，并用宇宙学观测工具进行了数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索f(Q,C)引力框架下不依赖传统暗能量成分而自然实现宇宙晚期加速膨胀的可能性，并厘清其与暗物质、能量动量守恒之间的内在关系。

Method: 将f(Q,C)引力的运动方程重写为等效动力学系统，针对两种对称性允许的仿射连接分类分析其相空间结构、不动点稳定性及物理约束（如物质密度非负、能量动量守恒）；引入状态方程参数化，定义并区分Quintaverse与Phantiverse吸引子；结合宇宙学参量（如宇宙学参数、statefinder）进行数值模拟与相图分析。

Result: 1）两类连接中均存在普适稳定的加速膨胀吸引子解；2）一类连接支持满足物理约束的有效暗物质解（含尘埃与刚性流体组分）；另一类则无法同时满足物质密度正定与能量动量守恒；3）加速解的状态方程参数可在-1附近连续调节，由此提出Quintaverse（w>-1）与Phantiverse（w<-1）概念；4）数值模拟验证了不同宇宙学演化路径及相空间行为。

Conclusion: f(Q,C)引力可通过纯几何机制解释宇宙加速膨胀，且能兼容有效暗物质行为；但其具体实现强烈依赖于仿射连接的选择；所提出的Quintaverse/Phantiverse分类为区分不同几何驱动加速机制提供了新范式。

Abstract: We investigate the asymptotic dark energy sectors of a quadratic form of $f(Q,C)$ Lagrangian. We reformulate the original equations of motion to obtain the equivalent dynamical system, for two non-trivial classes of affine connections allowed by symmetries of $f(Q,C)$ gravity. In these scenarios, the late-time accelerated expansion emerges as an intrinsic geometrical effect of the $f(Q,C)$ sector, without invoking an independent dark energy component. We find that an effective dark matter solution emerges for one class, whereas for the other class, the positivity of the matter density and the conservation of the momentum tensor cannot be simultaneously satisfied. This leads to the conclusion that there exists an effective dark matter solution for which the matter contribution is a combination of a pressureless matter as well as a stiff fluid. Furthermore, in both cases there always exists a universal stable accelerated expansion solution. Besides, the matter solutions are saddle points for those values of the model parameters that ensure the conservation of the energy momentum. In addition, we introduce a parameterization in which the equation of state parameter at the fixed point corresponding to accelerated expansion is characterized by a free parameter that quantifies its deviation from $-1$. We thereby inspired to invent the terms ``Quintaverse'' and ``Phantiverse'', corresponding to quintessence-like and phantom-like attractor solutions. Also, we numerically illustrate the evolution of different cosmological quantities and the phase-space diagrams. Particularly, the behavior of the Universe using two kinematic toolkits, namely, the cosmographic and statefinder variables, is discussed.

</details>


### [143] [Black Hole Shadows and Quasinormal Modes with Dark Matter at the Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30312)
*Armin Sadeghi,Javad T. Firouzjaee,Saeed Fakhry*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了暗物质晕对黑洞阴影和准正规模谱的影响，发现暗物质晕会增大黑洞阴影并降低准正规模的振荡频率和阻尼率，这些效应可通过光子球性质变化解释，且仅当暗物质集中在黑洞几个引力半径内时才可观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究暗物质晕如何影响黑洞在视界尺度上的两个关键可观测量：黑洞阴影和准正规模谱，以理解暗物质与黑洞相互作用的物理效应及其观测意义。

Method: 构建嵌入Hernquist暗物质晕的Schwarzschild黑洞模型，采用Einstein-cluster应力张量闭合爱因斯坦方程，分析不同密度趋近视界行为下的解，并研究其对阴影大小和准正规模的影响。

Result: 暗物质晕总是增大黑洞阴影；降低电磁波与轴向引力波准正规模的振荡频率和阻尼率，提高品质因子；效应源于光子球性质变化；仅当暗物质高度致密（集中在数个引力半径内）时效应显著。

Conclusion: 暗物质晕对黑洞阴影和准正规模有可观测影响，但当前EHT观测仅能约束足够致密的晕结构，且约束结果强烈依赖于将观测质量解释为黑洞质量还是系统总质量。

Abstract: In this work, we study the effect of a surrounding dark matter halo on two horizon-scale observables of a black hole: its shadow and quasinormal-mode spectrum. We consider a Schwarzschild black hole embedded in a Hernquist halo and construct a one-parameter family of solutions that preserve the total halo mass while varying how the halo density vanishes at the horizon. By closing the Einstein equations with an Einstein-cluster stress tensor, we show that horizon regularity requires the density to vanish at least linearly. Our results also indicate that the dominant energy condition is violated in a thin layer outside the horizon, highlighting the limitations of the Einstein-cluster description within the photon sphere. We demonstrate that, at fixed black hole mass, the halo always enlarges the shadow, with the effect becoming more pronounced for more massive and compact halos. Comparison with Event Horizon Telescope observations of Sgr\,A$^*$ and M87$^*$ shows that only sufficiently compact halos can be constrained, with the inferred bounds depending sensitively on whether the observed mass is identified with the black hole mass or the total mass of the system. We further show that the halo lowers both the oscillation frequencies and damping rates of electromagnetic and axial gravitational quasinormal modes, leading to higher quality factors. These spectral shifts are quantitatively explained by changes in the photon-sphere properties through the eikonal correspondence. Since the effects scale with the ratio of the halo mass to its size, they are observable only for dark matter concentrated within a few tens of gravitational radii of the black hole, which is the regime considered here.

</details>


### [144] [Conformal geodesics and the hyperboloid at timelike infinity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30466)
*Achilleas V. Koukas,Mariem Magdy,Juan A. Valiente Kroon*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过共形测地线构建了闵可夫斯基时空的类时无穷远扩展表示，用超曲面边界H⁺替代点i⁺，并与未来零无穷I⁺在角点C⁺相交，在共形高斯规范下推导了无质量标量场和自旋-2场的方程，并分析其渐近行为，为平直空间全息散射构造提供几何框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在渐近平坦时空中的散射描述需同时处理辐射与有质量的渐近态，这要求精确刻画零无穷与类时无穷之间的关系。

Method: 利用共形测地线性质，在爱因斯坦时空中将非类光度规测地线经局部重参数化转化为共形测地线；构造扩展的类时无穷表示，引入超曲面边界H⁺替代传统点i⁺，使其与未来零无穷I⁺在角点C⁺相交，并在保持I⁺位置不变的共形高斯规范下推导场方程。

Result: 得到了共形高斯规范下无质量标量场沿I⁺的输运方程，分析了其球谐模在类时无穷附近的渐近行为；显式导出了该规范下的无质量自旋-2场方程；阐明了该构造与射影延拓及扩展Carroll空间Ti的双曲底流形之间的联系。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了连接晚期时间渐近与零无穷渐近的几何框架，是构建适用于平直空间全息理论的散射理论的前提基础。

Abstract: A description of scattering in asymptotically flat spacetimes that includes both radiation and massive asymptotic states requires control of the relation between null and timelike infinity. Motivated by this problem, we use the properties of conformal geodesics to construct an extended representation of timelike infinity in Minkowski spacetime. The construction exploits the fact that every non-null metric geodesic in an Einstein spacetime is, up to an explicit local reparametrisation, a conformal geodesic. It replaces the point $i^+$ by a hyperboloidal boundary $\mathcal{H}^+$ meeting future null infinity $\mathscr{I}^+$ at a corner $\mathcal{C}^{+}$, while fixing the location of $\mathscr{I}^+$ in the associated conformal Gaussian gauge. In this gauge, we derive the equations governing massless scalar and spin-$2$ fields. In particular, we obtain a transport equation for the scalar field along future null infinity and analyse the asymptotic behaviour of its spherical-harmonic modes near timelike infinity. We also derive the explicit massless spin-$2$ equations in this gauge. Finally, we examine the relation to projective extensions and to the hyperbolic base of the extended Carrollian space $\mathrm{Ti}$. Our results provide a geometric framework for studying the connection between late-time and null asymptotics which is a prerequisite for scattering constructions relevant to flat space holography.

</details>


### [145] [Perturbations, eikonal limit and Hawking radiation of diverse external fields in a non-minimal charged black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30586)
*Yinan Jia,Zhen-Hao Yang,Xiao-Mei Kuang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文比较了无质量标量、电磁和狄拉克测试场在非最小耦合爱因斯坦-杨-米尔斯黑洞上的准正规模（QNMs）、灰体因子和霍金辐射，发现高阶模对非最小耦合参数ξ更敏感，而灰体因子和霍金辐射则受ξ影响较小但具物理意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解不同自旋场在非最小耦合Einstein-Yang-Mills黑洞背景下的动力学行为及其对霍金辐射的影响，特别是非最小耦合参数ξ如何调制准正规模、散射特性和热辐射谱。

Method: 采用WKB近似法和伪谱法计算准正规模；用六阶WKB法估算灰体因子；结合不稳定类光圆轨道的eikonal极限分析高频行为；比较三种自旋场（标量、电磁、狄拉克）的响应。

Result: 所有计算的准正规模均呈阻尼特性；高阶模比基模更敏感于ξ，部分呈现非单调依赖；大角动量下三者趋同于由类光圆轨道决定的eikonal行为；增大ξ提升振荡频率、减弱阻尼率；灰体因子主要依赖角动量，ξ修正较弱；部分波发射功率随ξ增大而下降，最低标量模略有非单调性。

Conclusion: 非最小耦合显著影响高阶准正规模的动力学特征，但对灰体透射率影响相对温和；霍金辐射变化综合反映了灰体因子与黑洞温度的共同调制，凸显ξ在强引力场量子效应中的关键角色。

Abstract: We compare the quasinormal modes (QNMs), greybody factors, and Hawking emission of massless scalar, electromagnetic, and Dirac test fields propagating on a non-minimal Einstein--Yang--Mills black hole. The QNMs are calculated using WKB and pseudospectral methods, while greybody factors are estimated with sixth-order WKB method. All computed modes are damped within the parameter range considered. Higher overtones generally exhibit stronger dependence on the non-minimal coupling $ξ$ than the fundamental mode, including non-monotonic behavior in the scalar and Dirac sectors. For large multipole numbers, all three fields follow a common leading eikonal description governed by the unstable circular null orbit. Increasing $ξ$ raises the orbital angular velocity and reduces the Lyapunov exponent, leading to higher oscillation frequencies and weaker damping rate. The greybody factors depend strongly on the multipole number, with weaker field dependent corrections from $ξ$. The partial-wave emission powers generally decrease with increasing $ξ$, while the lowest scalar mode shows mild non-monotonic behavior. This comparison reveals enhanced overtone sensitivity alongside comparatively small changes in transmission, whereas the Hawking emission reflects changes in both the greybody factors and the black hole temperature.

</details>


### [146] [Multi-orbit S-star probes of Lorentz violation at the Galactic Center](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30590)
*Jia-Yuan Fang,Jian-Ming Yan,Zong-Kuan Guo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用银河系中心S星（S2和S55）的模拟天体测量与光谱观测，结合SME框架下的洛伦兹破缺两体动力学模型，通过Fisher矩阵与MCMC分析，证明联合不同轨道取向的S星可有效打破几何简并，实现对纯引力SME八维参数空间的完整约束，达到10^{-3}–10^{-2}（时-空分量）和10^{-5}–10^{-4}（空间分量）量级的灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测洛伦兹对称性破缺在强引力场中的表现，特别是克服单颗S星观测中因轨道各向异性导致的SME参数简并问题。

Method: 基于GRAVITY合作组多星拟合结果构建S2与S55的模拟观测数据；引入最小纯引力标准模型扩展（SME）的洛伦兹破缺两体动力学，并叠加Schwarzschild一阶后牛顿（1PN）修正；采用Fisher矩阵分析与马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）方法进行参数估计与不确定性量化。

Result: 单独分析S2或S55可分辨8个独立SME方向中的7个；联合分析则可完全分辨全部8个方向；MCMC给出95%置信水平下时间-空间分量灵敏度约10^{-3}–10^{-2}，空间分量约10^{-5}–10^{-4}。

Conclusion: 不同轨道取向的S星联合观测是打破几何简并、解析洛伦兹破缺张量结构的有效手段，为在银河系中心强引力场中检验基础对称性提供了新途径。

Abstract: We investigate the potential of Galactic-center S-star observations to probe Lorentz violation in the minimal pure-gravity Standard-Model Extension (SME), focusing on parameter degeneracies associated with anisotropic orbital signatures. We construct simulated astrometric and spectroscopic observations of S2 and S55 using fiducial orbital and Galactic-center parameters based on the multi-star solution from the GRAVITY Collaboration and evolve the stellar trajectories using the leading Lorentz-violating two-body dynamics supplemented by the Schwarzschild first-post-Newtonian (1PN) correction. Separate Fisher-matrix analyses of S2 and S55 each resolve seven of the eight independent SME directions, while their joint analysis resolves all eight directions by combining complementary projections from the differently oriented orbits. A subsequent Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) analysis confirms the Fisher forecasts and reaches characteristic 95% credible-level sensitivities of approximately 10^(-3)-10^(-2) for the time-space components and 10^(-5)-10^(-4) for the spatial components. Our results demonstrate that combining S-stars with different orbital orientations is effective for breaking geometrical degeneracies and probing the tensor structure of Lorentz violation in the Galactic-center gravitational field.

</details>


### [147] [Decoherence of the quantum superradiant cloud from graviton emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30723)
*Lingyun Fu,Hidetoshi Omiya,Takahiro Tanaka,Hui-Yu Zhu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了引力子辐射对黑洞超辐射产生的玻色子云量子态的影响，发现其会抑制不同振幅云构型间的干涉，但保留符号相反构型间的干涉；当辐射引力子数达约1时，该效应显著，并在高占据数下可用相干态混合描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解引力子辐射如何影响由黑洞超辐射产生的宏观玻色子云的量子特性，特别是其纠缠性与经典性过渡问题。

Method: 理论分析结合量子场论方法，研究引力子发射对两模压缩态云的量子相干性、干涉效应及统计性质的影响，并计算相关量子态矩。

Result: 引力子辐射显著抑制不同振幅构型间的量子干涉，但不破坏符号反转下的干涉；当平均辐射引力子数≈1时抑制显著；高占据数下，一阶和二阶矩可由相干态系综精确再现。

Conclusion: 引力子辐射使玻色子云快速退相干并趋向经典统计描述，该过程远早于黑洞自旋显著减慢，表明超辐射云在天体物理尺度上本质上具有准经典行为。

Abstract: Black hole superradiance amplifies vacuum fluctuations into a macroscopic cloud of a massive bosonic field around a rotating black hole. Under free evolution, the quantum cloud is a two-mode squeezed state that remains entangled even at large occupation numbers, while its growing component admits a classical statistical description. In this work, we study how graviton emission changes the quantum state of the cloud. We find that graviton emission suppresses interference between cloud configurations with different amplitudes, but leaves interference between configurations that differ only by an overall sign unchanged. For typical configurations realized under vacuum fluctuations, the suppression becomes significant when the expected number of emitted gravitons is of order unity.We also show that, at larger occupation numbers, all first and second moments can be reproduced by a statistical mixture of coherent states. For the astrophysical black holes, both changes occur much before the spin-down of the black hole becomes significant.

</details>


### [148] [Matter Induced Bounds: Photon Ring, Lyapunov Exponent, Induced Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30736)
*Hocheol Lee,Bogeun Gwak*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了含各向异性物质的旋转黑洞中赤道光子环半径和李雅普诺夫指数的解析界限，发现其光子环半径存在由Kerr黑洞对应量给出的上界，并推导出与探针弦在光子环Penrose极限下的诱导温度和世界面表面引力相关的Lyapunov指数约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解含各向异性物质的旋转黑洞的强引力场特征（如光子环与准正规模），需建立其关键可观测量（如光子环半径、Lyapunov指数）的普适解析界限。

Method: 基于有限渐近质量和非负能量密度条件，结合赤道态方程参数恒定假设，推导光子环半径与Lyapunov指数的上下界；进一步利用Penrose极限下探针弦的世界面几何，导出诱导温度与世界面表面引力，并建立其与Lyapunov指数的关系。

Result: 得到光子环半径的上界（等于同质量角动量Kerr黑洞值）和下界（用外视界物理量表示）；Lyapunov指数受诱导温度与世界面表面引力约束，满足2πT_ind = κ_ind ≥ λ；该结果进一步限定了无质量标量场在短波极限下的准正规模虚部绝对值。

Conclusion: 含各向异性物质的旋转黑洞光子环结构仍受Kerr极限严格约束，且其动力学不稳定性（由Lyapunov指数表征）可通过弦论启发的热力学量（诱导温度、世界面表面引力）进行上界估计，揭示了强场引力、黑洞热力学与弦理论之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We investigate analytic bounds on the equatorial photon ring radius and the Lyapunov exponent in rotating black holes containing anisotropic matter. For a constant equatorial equation-of-state parameter, the finite asymptotic mass and nonnegative energy density imply an upper bound on the photon ring radius given by the Kerr photon ring radius with the same asymptotic mass and rotation parameters for co-rotating and counter-rotating motions. The lower bounds are written in terms of the quantities evaluated at the outer horizon. These bounds on the Lyapunov exponent constrain the absolute magnitude of the imaginary part of the quasinormal frequency of a massless scalar field within the eikonal limit. The worldsheet surface gravity and induced temperature were derived for a probe string in the Penrose limit along the photon ring. The induced temperature and worldsheet surface gravity give an upper bound on the photon ring Lyapunov exponent, $2 πT_\mathrm{ind} = κ_\mathrm{ind} \geq λ$.

</details>


### [149] [Axial Perturbations of Extended Tolman III in the New $f(R,T)$ Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30770)
*B. N. Jayawiguna,G. A. Sabian,H. S. Ramadhan,A. Sulaksono*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在f(R,T)引力理论框架下，利用扩展的Tolman III状态方程研究致密天体，并通过轴向引力扰动分析其观测特征（如星体稳定性与引力波回声），发现f(R,T)引力中有效势更宽更深、QNM频率虚部为负表明扰动衰减，且其回声序列比广义相对论更少；结合NICER观测数据，特定参数下的f(R,T)模型能更好复现GW170817事件的特征频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索修正引力理论（特别是f(R,T)引力）对致密天体物理性质及引力波观测信号的影响，以检验其相对于广义相对论的可观测差异。

Method: 采用扩展Tolman III物态方程，在f(R,T)=R+2χT引力框架下建模致密星体；引入基于物质拉格朗日量二阶变分的新形式化方法；通过求解轴向引力扰动得到有效势与准正规模（QNM），并用Bohr-Sommerfeld拟合和WKB近似计算频率；结合NICER观测约束中子星半径，对比GR与f(R,T)模型对GW170817回声频率的解释能力。

Result: f(R,T)引力中的有效势比GR更宽更深；QNM频率虚部为负，扰动指数衰减；超紧致构型可产生引力波回声，但f(R,T)模型产生的回声数少于GR；在特定参数（β=4.663×10⁻¹, χ=0.96π）下，f(R,T)模型能复现GW170817的特征频率，而GR不能。

Conclusion: f(R,T)引力理论可显著改变致密天体的扰动谱与引力波回声特征，为区分修正引力与广义相对论提供了潜在观测判据，尤其在多信使天文学背景下具有重要检验价值。

Abstract: We investigate compact objects using the extended Tolman III equation of state in the framework of $f(R,T)=R+2χT$ gravity. In the matter sector of the Lagrangian density, we employ a new formalism based on the evaluation of the second-order derivative of the matter Lagrangian with respect to the metric \cite{Jayawiguna:2026das}. We analyze the corresponding observational signatures through axial gravitational perturbations including stellar stability and gravitational echo. We find that the effective potential in $f(R,T)$ gravity is wider and deeper than its counterpart in general relativity (GR). The quasinormal-mode (QNM) frequencies are calculated using the Bohr--Sommerfeld (BS) fitting and both the lower- and higher-order Wentzel--Kramers--Brillouin (WKB) approximations. For the ultracompact configurations considered, trapped modes can tunnel through the potential barrier, giving rise to gravitational-wave echoes. The imaginary parts of the QNM frequencies are found to be negative, $\Im(ω_n M)<0$, indicating that the perturbations decay exponentially with time. Our results show that the echo train in GR contains more echoes than that in $f(R,T)$ gravity. Finally, assuming that the stellar object is a neutron star and adopting a stellar radius consistent with measurements by the Neutron Star Interior Composition Explorer (NICER), we find that the $f(R,T)$ gravity model yields a characteristic frequency consistent with the estimated frequency associated with GW170817 for $β= 4.663\times10^{-1}$ and $χ=0.96π$, whereas the corresponding GR model does not exhibit comparable agreement.

</details>


### [150] [Constrain the number of inner horizons from gravitational Noether charges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30812)
*Jing-Peng Ye,Run-Qiu Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过引力诺特定理的体-边界平衡结构，重新解释了黑洞内部多层视界存在的障碍，并将其推广到高曲率引力理论（如Lovelock引力），结合TCC类曲率条件和正Wald熵假设，证明在非退化事件视界内至多存在一个非退化内Killing视界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究物质与几何如何约束黑洞内部视界的数量，特别是在高曲率引力中传统ADM方法变得复杂的情况下，寻求更普适的分析框架。

Method: 利用引力诺特定理构造的Komar型积分，建立体-边界间的引力诺特荷平衡；引入TCC类曲率条件，并结合Wald熵的热力学正定性假设进行分析。

Result: 在Lovelock引力中，若满足所提曲率条件与正Wald熵假设，则非退化事件视界内至多存在一个非退化内Killing视界。

Conclusion: 黑洞内多视界的存在障碍本质上反映了体部引力响应与视界热力学之间的相容性，诺特定理提供了一种比ADM分解更自然、更普适的表述方式。

Abstract: A central question in the study of black hole interiors is how matter and geometry constrain the number of horizons. In Einstein gravity, one known method is to use the ADM decomposition of the field equations to derive a differential inequality for the lapse, which then constrains the possible multiple inner horizons. We show that the same obstruction can instead be formulated through the Komar integral as a bulk-boundary balance of gravitational Noether charge. In higher-curvature gravity, a direct ADM analysis becomes more complicated, whereas the gravitational Noether charge naturally retains this bulk-boundary structure. We naturally apply the gravitational Noether charge analysis to Lovelock gravity. We introduce a TCC-like curvature condition and prove that, together with the thermodynamically motivated assumption of positive Wald entropy, it allows at most one nondegenerate inner Killing horizon inside a nondegenerate event horizon. This suggests that the obstruction reflects a compatibility between bulk gravitational response and horizon thermodynamics.

</details>


### [151] [Parameter Constraints on $f(R^{1+ε},T_{μν}T^{μν})$ Gravity from Exotic Compact Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31032)
*S. Wilhelmina,B. N. Jayawiguna,A. Sulaksono*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在f(R,T²)修正引力理论框架下夸克星的质量-半径结构、物理可行性和稳定性，使用MIT袋模型状态方程，并通过多个观测致密天体（如HESS J1731-347、GW190814等）约束模型参数ε和α。结果表明该理论可兼容夸克星并合理再现观测质量-半径数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论虽成功，但在极端致密天体（高/低质量）情形下促使人们探索修正引力理论；f(R,T²)同时修改几何与物质部分，为研究夸克星提供新框架。

Method: 采用MIT袋模型状态方程，在f(R,T²)=R^{1+ε}+ακ²T_{μν}T^{μν}理论下求解恒星结构方程；对比精确模型、一阶泰勒展开及广义相对论结果；利用观测致密星质量-半径数据约束参数ε和α；检验能量条件与声速以评估物理可行性与稳定性。

Result: f(R,T²)模型能成功描述夸克星结构，参数ε和α经HESS J1731-347、GW190814、PSR J1614-2230和PSR J0437-4715等观测约束后，可复现其质量-半径关系；能量条件和声速分析表明模型具有物理合理性和稳定性。

Conclusion: f(R,T²)修正引力理论是一种有前景的广义相对论扩展方案，能自然容纳夸克星，并对当前观测到的最轻和最重致密天体给出一致解释。

Abstract: According to General Relativity (GR), gravity is described as the curvature of spacetime caused by mass and energy. Although GR has been highly successful in describing gravitational phenomena, exotic compact objects with high mass and strong gravitational fields, as well as low-mass objects, have motivated the study of modified theories of gravity. One of the modified gravity frameworks is $f(R,T^2) = R^{1 + ε} + ακ^2 T_{μν} T^{μν}$, which provides an extension of GR by introducing modifications to both the geometric and matter sectors. In this study, we investigate the mass-radius structure, physical viability, and stability of quark stars in $f(R,T^2)$ gravity using the MIT Bag Model equation of state. We compare results from the exact $f(R,T^2)$ model, its first-order Taylor expansion, and GR. The values of the gravity parameter $ε$ and $α$ are constrained by evaluating the ability of the model to reproduce the observed mass-radius configurations of compact objects, including HESS J1731-347 with a mass of $M = 0.77_{-0.17}^{+0.20} \, M_{\odot}$ and a radius of $R = 10.4_{-0.78}^{+0.86}$ km, GW190814 with a mass of $M = 2.6 \, M_{\odot}$, PSR J1614-2230, with a mass of $M = 1.928 \pm 0.017 \, M_{\odot}$ and a radius of $R > 10$ km, and PSR J0437-4715, with a mass of $M = 1.418 \pm 0.037 \, M_{\odot}$ and a radius of $R = 11.36_{-0.63}^{+0.95}$ km. We assess the physical viability and stability of the stellar configurations using the energy conditions (strong, weak, null, and dominant) and the speed of sound. Overall, the $f(R,T^2)$ gravity model can accommodate quark star configurations and reproduce the observed properties of massive compact objects, particularly those associated with GW190814 and the lightest compact object observed, HESS J1731-347, respectively.

</details>


### [152] [The subcritical accelerating Schwarzschild-Bertotti-Robinson family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31035)
*Kenneth Hong*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过精确的尺度无关字典，将有限ℓ中性Alekseev-Astorino Schwarzschild-Bertotti-Robinson族与亚临界加速Schwarzschild-Bertotti-Robinson解联系起来，表明Astorino参数ℓ表征的是亚临界加速度而非额外的中性黑洞自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清Astorino参数ℓ的物理含义，确定其是否代表新的黑洞自由度，还是仅对应某种加速度参数。

Method: 采用Weyl杆匹配、视界形状比较、独立锥度检验以及视界外度规与麦克斯韦场的精确重构等方法。

Result: 建立了有限ℓ中性Alekseev-Astorino解与亚临界加速Schwarzschild-Bertotti-Robinson解之间的严格对应关系；发现ℓ≠0时存在非零OP加速度且两极开口不等，但无加速视界；ℓ=0对应非加速解，|ℓ|→∞趋近临界边界|A/B|=1。

Conclusion: Astorino参数ℓ本质上参数化亚临界加速度，而非中性黑洞的独立自由度。

Abstract: We identify the general finite-$\ell$ neutral Alekseev--Astorino Schwarzschild--Bertotti--Robinson family with the subcritical branch of the independently obtained accelerating Schwarzschild--Bertotti--Robinson solution. The exact scale-free dictionary is \begin{equation*}
  B^2M^2=\frac{m^2}{b^2+\ell^2},\qquad
  AM=\frac{m\ell}{b^2+\ell^2},\qquad
  \frac{A}{B}=\frac{\ell}{\sqrt{b^2+\ell^2}}, \end{equation*} up to the north--south orientation convention. Thus finite real $\ell$ covers precisely the acceleration interval $|A|<|B|$: for $\ell\ne0$ the corresponding OP acceleration parameter is nonzero and the two axial openings are unequal, although no acceleration horizon is present. We establish the identification by canonical Weyl-rod matching and horizon-shape comparison, an independent conicity check, and an exact off-horizon reconstruction of the metric and Maxwell field. The centered limit $\ell=0$ is the nonaccelerating Schwarzschild--BR solution, whereas $|\ell|\to\infty$ approaches the critical boundary $|A/B|=1$ from below. The Astorino parameter $\ell$ therefore parametrizes subcritical acceleration rather than an additional independent neutral black-hole degree of freedom.

</details>


### [153] [Quasinormal modes of Type I perturbations of dilaton-Euler-Heisenberg black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31129)
*Yu-Xuan Wang,De-Cheng Zou,Yun Soo Myung,Sheng-Yuan Li,Ming Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了磁荷稀释-欧拉-海森堡（dEH）黑洞的一类准正则模（QNMs），发现其引力与电磁扰动耦合，且在非极端范围内所有模式均保持阻尼，支持这些分支的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究磁荷稀释-欧拉-海森堡黑洞中轴向引力与极向电磁扰动耦合下的准正则模行为及其稳定性。

Method: 采用直接积分法和切比雪夫伪谱法计算一类准正则模频率，并分析不同参数（如α、ζ、角动量ℓ）对频谱和阻尼特性的影响。

Result: 两类耦合分支的阻尼响应依赖于电荷和多极矩，符号可能相反；ζ<0时偶极阻尼随电荷增加而减小；ζ>0时一类模式电荷范围超出对应极化态的局域角梯度界；所有追踪模式在非极端范围内均保持阻尼。

Conclusion: 磁荷dEH黑洞的一类准正则模在非极端参数范围内保持阻尼，表明该类扰动模式稳定，支持黑洞整体线性稳定性。

Abstract: We study the type I quasinormal modes (QNMs) of magnetically charged dilaton--Euler--Heisenberg (dEH) black holes, where axial gravitational and polar electromagnetic perturbations are coupled. Direct integration and Chebyshev pseudospectral methods give consistent QNM frequencies for the fundamental branches continued from Schwarzschild at $\ell=1,2,3$. Varying $α$ at fixed $ζ=α-β$ changes the spectrum on the same background. The damping response depends on charge and multipole and can have opposite signs for the two coupled branches. For $ζ<0$, dipole damping decreases with charge toward horizon merger. For $ζ>0$, the Type I charge range extends beyond the complementary polarization's local angular-gradient bound. All tracked modes remain damped in the nonextremal ranges, supporting the stability of these branches.

</details>


### [154] [Gravitational waves from highly charged binary black holes in Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31177)
*Ryoto Tomie,Kai Togashi,Kitaro Taniguchi,Ryotaro Kase*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过全非线性数值相对论模拟，研究了爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-稀子（EMD）理论中带电双黑洞并合过程，重点分析了电荷大小与符号、稀子耦合常数a对引力波形、辐射通量及并合动力学的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解扩展引力理论（如EMD）中带电黑洞并合的可观测特征，检验稀子场对引力波信号及余留黑洞‘毛发’结构的影响，为未来引力波观测提供理论基准。

Method: 采用全非线性数值相对论方法，系统模拟不同输入电荷（同号/异号、弱/强）、不同稀子耦合参数a下的双黑洞并合；提取引力波形、电磁与标量辐射通量，并分析并合时间、余留黑洞性质。

Result: 弱电荷情形下波形接近广义相对论真空基线；强异号电荷下，a≠0的EMD波形明显偏离a=0的爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦基准，且a=1时并合时间缩短约62%；同号电荷并合时间比中性基线长15%-20%，辐射以四极电磁为主；a=1和√3时同号并合产生带‘二级稀子毛发’的带电残余黑洞，而异号并合则趋于无标量毛发的中性黑洞。

Conclusion: 稀子耦合显著调制强电荷双黑洞的并合动力学与辐射特征，尤其影响并合时标、辐射谱分布及余留黑洞的标量结构，表明引力波观测有望约束EMD类理论中的稀子参数。

Abstract: We perform fully nonlinear numerical-relativity simulations of electrically charged binary black hole mergers in Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton (EMD) theory, varying the magnitude and relative sign of the input charges and the dilaton coupling $a$. In the weak-charge sequence, the dominant gravitational waveforms remain close to the neutral vacuum baseline of general relativity and are visually indistinguishable across the sampled couplings, consistent with earlier EMD simulations. For the highly charged binaries with equal-magnitude opposite charges, however, the dominant gravitational strain exhibits visible differences between the EMD cases with $a\neq0$ and the charged Einstein-Maxwell reference at $a=0$. At $a=1$, the coordinate-time interval from the same separation to the waveform-based merger is approximately $62\%$ shorter than for the neutral binary. In this high-charge sequence at fixed input charges, increasing $a$ strengthens the scalar dressing while leaving the individual dilaton-weighted horizon charges nearly unchanged. Within the same sequence, increasing $a$ across the sampled values decreases the dipolar electromagnetic-flux peak, increases the scalar-flux peak, and modestly lengthens the merger interval. The sampled like-charged binaries have corresponding merger intervals approximately $15\%$-$20\%$ longer than the neutral baseline and emit predominantly quadrupolar electromagnetic radiation. At $a=1$ and $a=\sqrt{3}$, the like-charged mergers leave charged remnants with scalar fields consistent with secondary dilaton hair. The opposite-charge remnants instead approach neutral black holes without stationary scalar hair.

</details>


### [155] [Clarifications on black/white hole metastable relics as dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31226)
*Aurélien Barrau,Margaux Gajocha,Killian Martineau,Martin Teuscher,Jamy-Jayme Thézier,Ruth Durrer*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了黑洞蒸发可能在普朗克质量附近停止并形成寿命约为M^5的亚稳态遗迹，这些遗迹可能在光学或紫外波段发出强辐射，并提出了直接探测方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索黑洞蒸发终止于普朗克质量形成的遗迹是否可作为暗物质候选者，并研究其可观测性。

Method: 构建新模型，分析亚稳态黑洞遗迹的辐射特性与寿命，并设计其直接探测方案。

Result: 发现此类遗迹具有显著光学/紫外辐射亮度，甚至可媲美恒星，并提出可行的直接探测方法。

Conclusion: 亚稳态黑洞遗迹不仅是潜在暗物质候选体，还具备可观测性，为暗物质研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: The idea that the evaporation of black holes could end around the Planck mass and lead to relics possibly accounting for dark matter is quite old. In this article we focus on new models where the relics are metastable, typically with a lifetime of order $M^5$, where $M$ is the initial mass of the black hole. We show that those relics would actually be quite luminous, radiating -- possibly as much as the stars -- in the optical or UV band. We explicitly construct a proposal for their direct detection.

</details>


### [156] [Stability, Tidal Deformability, and Statistical Constraints of Anisotropic Compact Stars with a Generalized Polytropic Equation of State : Modelling Observation with Buchdahl Space-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31348)
*Ritabrata Biswas,Arpan Bhattacharya,Subhajit Pal*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在广义相对论框架下，结合Buchdahl时空与广义多方物态方程，构建了一个各向异性致密星模型，并通过Darmois-Israel拼接条件匹配外部Schwarzschild时空；模型成功应用于38颗观测致密星，满足多种物理合理性检验，给出了质量、半径、潮汐形变等参数的统计约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个物理上自洽、能描述观测致密星（如中子星）各向异性效应的相对论星体模型，并检验其在质量-半径关系、潮汐形变及稳定性等方面的预测能力。

Method: 采用Buchdahl度规作为内时空解，引入广义多方物态方程刻画各向异性流体；利用Darmois-Israel条件实现内外时空光滑拼接；对38颗致密星候选体进行参数拟合；通过Zeldovich条件、绝热指数、因果性、Herrera碎裂判据等检验物理可接受性；并开展MCMC统计分析。

Result: 获得正则的能量密度、压力、质量、各向异性及红移分布；满足力学平衡与稳定性条件；得到质量范围2.88–3.23 M⊙、潮汐形变参数Λ=22.55–68.38；发现质量与无量纲潮汐形变成反比关系。

Conclusion: 该各向异性模型在物理上合理、数学上自洽，能统一描述宽质量范围的致密星观测特征，为致密物质状态方程和引力理论检验提供了新工具。

Abstract: An anisotropic compact star model is constructed within the framework of general relativity by combining the Buchdahl space-time with a generalized polytropic equation of state. The model parameters are constrained through Darmois-Israel junction conditions by matching interior geometry smoothly with exterior Schwarzschild space-time. The proposed framework is applied to thirty-eight observed compact star candidates spanning a broad range of masses and radii. Regular and physically acceptable distributions of the energy density, radial pressure, tangential pressure, enclosed mass, anisotropy and gravitational redshift are obtained throughout the stellar interior. Energy density and pressure components are found to decrease outward, while the enclosed mass and gravitational redshift are found to increase monotonically towards the stellar surface. The radial pressure is required to vanish at the boundary, thereby determining the estimated stellar radius. The anisotropy is found to provide an additional outward contribution that supports the stellar configurations against gravitational collapse. The physical acceptability of the model is examined through the Zeldovich condition, radial adiabatic index, causality conditions and Herrera cracking criterion. Hydrostatic equilibrium is achieved through the balance among gravitational, hydrostatic and anisotropic forces. The tidal response is also investigated, and an inverse relation between the estimated mass and dimensionless tidal deformability is obtained. The calculated configurations span \(2.88\lesssim M_{\rm Est}/M_\odot\lesssim3.23\) and yield \(22.55\lesssimΛ\lesssim68.38\). MCMC analysis provides statistically constrained compactness and tidal parameters. Overall, a physically viable description of anisotropic compact stars is obtained.

</details>


### [157] [Chaotic dynamics and resonances in Einstein-Rosen standing wave spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31473)
*Adam A. Zychowicz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究爱因斯坦-罗森驻波时空中的测地线混沌行为，利用次谐波Melnikov函数法分析Poincaré映射，揭示了异宿网络的可容许类型及其随参数连续变化的演化规律，并识别出倍周期分岔等分岔现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示爱因斯坦-罗森驻波时空中测地线动力学结构的丰富性，超越此前对混沌异宿网络的已有认识。

Method: 应用次谐波Melnikov函数法分析Poincaré映射，追踪参数连续变化下异宿网络的演化。

Result: 明确了异宿网络的可容许类型，识别出包括倍周期分岔在内的多种分岔现象。

Conclusion: 该系统动力学结构远比先前认为的更丰富，参数演化中存在显著的非线性分岔行为。

Abstract: Recent work has revealed chaotic geodesic behavior in Einstein-Rosen standing gravitational wave spacetimes. The dynamics of massive test particles are dominated by chaotic heteroclinic networks. This study demonstrates that the dynamical structure of the system is far richer than previously thought. Applying the subharmonic Melnikov function method to Poincaré maps reveals the admissible types of heteroclinic networks. I trace the evolution of these structures under the continuous change of the solution's parameters. In this way, I identify bifurcation phenomena, including a period-doubling bifurcation.

</details>


### [158] [Testing charged Simpson-Visser-AdS spacetimes with twin-peak QPOs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31534)
*Saeed Ullah Khan,Javlon Rayimbaev,Muhammad Zahid,Tolaniddin Nurmukhamedov,Abat Muratov,Weiwei Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了带电Simpson-Visser-AdS时空中的圆轨道测地线和双峰高频准周期振荡（HF-QPOs），发现其轨道动力学与Reissner-Nordström-AdS黑洞高度相似，仅径向振荡频率含一个由正则化尺度b引入的修正因子；通过拟合两个X射线双星的HF-QPO数据，揭示了电荷Q与AdS半径l之间的强简并性，并指出所推断的‘变形’更可能源于忽略自旋的静态度规对Kerr黑洞的近似，而非真实电荷或宇宙常数的测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非标准黑洞时空（如带电Simpson-Visser-AdS）中高频准周期振荡（HF-QPOs）的物理解释，检验其是否能被观测数据约束，并厘清不同参数（电荷Q、正则化尺度b、AdS半径l）之间的简并性。

Method: 基于带电Simpson-Visser-AdS度规，推导测试粒子的圆轨道运动学量（能量、角动量、开普勒频率、ISCO），解析给出径向振荡频率及ISCO条件；采用相对论进动模型，结合GRO J1655−40和XTE J1550−564的HF-QPO观测数据及质量高斯先验，对参数(Q, l, b)进行拟合分析。

Result: 发现b仅通过因子1−b²/R²影响径向频率；拟合显示Q/M≈0.814或l≈40.6M可显著改善χ²（从32.9降至~0.13），且Q与l在参数平面中存在单一简并谷；b在Q固定时无法被数据有效约束（上限约3.9M）；Kerr黑洞（a≈0.3–0.4）可同样拟合数据。

Conclusion: 观测到的HF-QPO拟合优度提升反映的是轨道频率对不同时空变形（电荷、宇宙常数、自旋）的简并响应，而非对真实电荷或AdS宇宙常数的测量；所推断的‘变形’实质上吸收了被忽略的黑洞自旋效应。

Abstract: In this manuscript, we study circular geodesics and twin-peak high-frequency quasi-periodic oscillations (HF-QPOs) in the charged Simpson--Visser--anti-de Sitter (AdS) spacetime, which depends on a charge parameter $Q$, a regularization scale $b$ and an AdS radius $l$. Written in terms of the areal radius $R=\sqrt{r^{2}+b^{2}}$, the specific energy, angular momentum, Keplerian frequency and innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) of test particles coincide with those of the Reissner--Nordström--AdS black hole, so $b$ enters the epicyclic spectrum only through a factor $1-b^{2}/R^{2}$ in the radial frequency. We give this frequency and the ISCO condition in closed form. We then fit the relativistic-precession model to the HF-QPO pairs of GRO~J1655$-$40 and XTE~J1550$-$564, with their dynamical masses included as Gaussian priors. The Schwarzschild limit gives $χ^{2}=32.9$ for two degrees of freedom. One additional deformation removes this tension: $Q/M=0.814^{+0.059}_{-0.074}$ at fixed $l=100M$, or $l=40.6^{+6.2}_{-4.8}\,M$ at $Q=0$, both with $χ^{2}\simeq0.13$. These two solutions lie on a single degeneracy valley in the $(Q,l)$ plane, and, at $l=100M$, $b$ is left unconstrained up to $b\simeq3.9M$ (68\%), a limit set by the charge prior rather than by the data. A Kerr black hole with spin $a\simeq0.3$--$0.4$ reproduces the same pairs, so the inferred deformation most likely absorbs the spin neglected by the static metric. Our results therefore describe degeneracies among orbital-frequency deformations; they are not a measurement of an electric charge or of a cosmological constant.

</details>


### [159] [Perturbations and phenomenology of compact objects in quantum gravity theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31536)
*Andrea Spina*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了二次引力和渐近安全两种量子引力理论中致密天体的微扰动力学与现象学，重点分析了准正规模、黑洞阴影和霍金辐射等可观测效应，并探讨了其对原初黑洞及暗物质的潜在影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接量子引力模型与可观测的天体物理现象，特别是通过致密天体的微扰动力学来探索量子引力效应。

Method: 采用线性微扰方法分析静态球对称解的张量扰动；使用固有时间流方法构建并研究渐近安全引力中的动力学正则黑洞解；分析准正规模、黑洞阴影和霍金辐射等现象学特征。

Result: 在Einstein-Weyl引力中发现单极区Gregory-Laflamme型不稳定性及大质量场尾项的普适性；揭示标准eikonal对应关系的失效；识别出可模拟黑洞阴影的‘遮蔽奇点’；在渐近安全引力中得到稳定正则黑洞解，其阴影兼容当前观测约束，但霍金辐射温度更低、发射率受抑。

Conclusion: 致密天体的微扰动力学可作为桥梁，将不同量子引力模型与潜在可观测的天体物理现象联系起来，为检验量子引力提供新途径。

Abstract: This thesis investigates the perturbative dynamics and phenomenology of compact objects in two approaches to quantum gravity: quadratic gravity and asymptotic safety. Linear perturbations provide a tool to probe the physical properties of spacetimes and their potential observational signatures, including quasinormal modes, black hole shadows, and Hawking radiation. The first part focuses on Einstein-Weyl gravity, where an additional massive spin-2 degree of freedom modifies the dynamics of compact objects. We derive and analyze tensor perturbation for static, spherically symmetric solutions, identifying Gregory-Laflamme-type instabilities in the monopole sector and studying the time domain evolution of higher multipoles. We also investigate universal massive-field tails and the breakdown of the standard eikonal correspondence between quasinormal modes and null geodesics. Particular attention is devoted to veiled singularities, horizonless configurations that satisfy a form of weak cosmic censorship and can mimic black hole shadows. The second part examines regular black holes in asymptotically safe gravity. Using the proper-time flow approach, we construct and investigate a dynamical regular black hole solution arising from gravitational collapse. We analyze its perturbative stability and quasinormal mode spectrum, finding characteristic deviations from Schwarzschild black holes. We explore the phenomenological implications of quantum corrections for shadows and Hawking radiation. While shadow remains compatible with current constraints, the modified evaporation process features lower temperatures and suppressed emission rates, with implications for primordial black holes and their possible contribution to dark matter. Overall, this work demonstrates how the perturbative dynamics of compact objects can connect quantum gravity models to potentially observable astrophysical phenomena.

</details>
